However, these methods may suffer from redundant or ineffective sub-queries and insufficiently diverse query exploration. Moreover, due to the tightly interleaved nature of reasoning and retrieval, it remains unclear how the evolving reasoning process influences query intent. To characterize the evolution of query intent during reasoning, we introduce search momentum, which captures the accumulated retrieval tendency induced by historical changes in generated sub-queries along the reasoning trajectory. Through momentum-based analysis, we find that the query generation process tends to continuously evolve along existing retrieval trajectories. Based on this observation, we propose MAPS, a Macro-Adaptive Parallel Reasoning framework for regulating search momentum and mitigating query-intent drift. MAPS first retains the retrieved knowledge and a refined reasoning state, thereby reducing the influence of historical reasoning trajectories on subsequent sub-query generation. Subsequently, MAPS extends the conventional sequential reasoning process into a parallel reasoning paradigm. At each iteration, MAPS identifies complementary retrieval directions, generates corresponding sub-queries for parallel retrieval, and consolidates the retrieved evidence to update the knowledge and reasoning states. Experiments on six question-answering benchmarks demonstrate that MAPS consistently outperforms representative RAG methods across different backbone models. All code and resources are available at https://github.com/NEUIR/MAPS.

</details>


### [72] [MemoryTax: The Bitter Lesson of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29823)
*Zedong Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究对比了设计记忆系统与编码代理在长对话问答中的表现，发现设计记忆仅在弱模型下有效且成本高昂，强模型下编码代理凭借原始历史搜索能力更优且更省成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当模型具备强大搜索能力时，预先设计的记忆系统（如Mem0、Zep）是否仍能带来优势，以及不同记忆方法与模型强弱的交互效应。

Method: 在单一LoCoMo对话（419轮、152问题）上，测试四种记忆方法（完整上下文、BM25、Mem0、Zep）与三种编码代理（Pi、Codex、Claude Code），分别搭配三种模型（GPT-4o-mini、GPT-6 Luna、Claude Opus 5.5），记录每次推理的成本与准确率。

Result: Mem0仅在GPT-4o-mini下优于完整上下文，且其构建成本超过一次完整上下文推理；记忆系统性能上限由写入模型决定，强读模型无法弥补弱写模型的信息丢失。Claude Opus 5.5下Pi代理以不足一半成本达99%准确率，领先优势部分源于仅代理可读的图片说明。

Conclusion: 设计记忆是弱模型的昂贵补丁，强模型下保留原始历史并让模型自主搜索更高效；记忆系统的写入质量决定了性能上限，且多模态证据（如图片说明）可能成为代理的关键优势。

Abstract: Agent memory systems such as Mem0 and Zep decide what to keep before any question arrives; coding agents keep the raw history and let the model search it with its own tools. We ask whether designed memory still helps once the model is strong enough to search for itself. On one LoCoMo conversation (419 turns, 152 questions), we run four memory methods (full context, BM25, Mem0, Zep) and three coding agents (Pi, Codex, Claude Code), each with GPT-4o-mini, GPT-6 Luna and Claude Opus 5.5, and record what every rollout costs. This is a short empirical study, with one conversation and one run per cell. Agent memory is a costly patch for a weak model: Mem0 beats full context only with GPT-4o-mini, and its build alone costs more than a whole full-context rollout. Agent memory also has a ceiling, set by the model that writes it: a stronger reader does not recover what a weaker writer left out. With Claude Opus 5.5, Pi tops the cost-accuracy frontier, scoring 99% for less than half the cost of full context; its one miss is a wrong answer key. Part of that lead comes from photo captions that only the agents could read: on the 88 questions whose evidence has no photo, Pi and full context differ by one answer.

</details>


### [73] [S-EDL: Eliciting Self-Evidence from Sequence Likelihoods for Semantic Calibration of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29832)
*Yawei Li,Jiazheng Li,David Rügamer,Bernd Bischl,Yulan He,Mina Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出S-EDL方法，利用模型自身序列似然构建可微分Dirichlet先验，针对开放式生成的语义校准进行优化，无需固定多选选项，实验显示显著降低语义校准误差并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式生成中语义校准极具挑战性，因为多个不同token序列可表达相同意义。现有校准技术仅针对固定集合分类置信度或单字符串二元正确性，不适合动态、多字符串的开放式语义结果。

Method: S-EDL是一种新型证据学习方法：从模型自身序列似然中获取证据，构建针对提示词特定语义类别的可微分Dirichlet先验；优化证据损失产生校准感知训练信号，适用于可变语义类别，完全绕过固定多选选项的需求。

Result: 在四个开放式基准和三个模型上评估，S-EDL显著降低语义校准误差，同时提高准确率。

Conclusion: 通过将生成似然与语义正确性对齐，为在复杂开放式应用中部署可信大语言模型建立了框架。

Abstract: Calibrating large language models (LLMs) in open-ended generation is uniquely challenging because many distinct token sequences can express the same underlying meaning. Prior calibration techniques target either fixed-set classification confidence or the binary correctness of individual strings, making them inherently ill-suited for the dynamic, multi-string nature of open-ended semantic outcomes. To bridge this gap, we introduce S-EDL, a novel evidential method that natively optimizes LLMs for semantic calibration. By eliciting evidence from the sequence likelihoods of the model itself, S-EDL constructs a differentiable Dirichlet prior over prompt-specific semantic classes. Optimizing the evidential loss under this prior yields a calibration-aware training signal over variable semantic classes, completely bypassing the need for fixed multiple-choice options. Evaluations across four open-ended benchmarks and three models demonstrate that S-EDL substantially reduces semantic calibration error while improving accuracy. By aligning generative likelihoods with semantic correctness, this work establishes a framework for deploying trustworthy LLMs in complex, open-ended applications. Code is available at: https://github.com/sandylaker/s-edl.

</details>


### [74] [Every Part Has Its Role: Part-of-Speech Effects in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29843)
*Laya Iyer,Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了概念增强在大语言模型训练中的效果，复现并扩展了先前工作（从名词扩展到动词和形容词），探讨词性（POS）对困惑度和下游任务性能的影响。发现：概念增强在新闻领域最稳定地降低困惑度，与词性无关；动词提供最可靠的下游任务增益；但表示学习分析显示，带来最大下游增益的条件不一定是最改变模型内部表示的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常用下一词预测（NTP）训练，该目标奖励预测特定token而非显式建模词汇间的语义相似性。先前工作通过用概念相关的替代词替换目标词来增强训练数据，但尚不清楚概念在句子中的位置（即词性）是否影响增强效果。

Method: 复现并扩展概念增强方法：将名词扩展到动词和形容词，生成最小变化句子（如 'The tired coach ran home' → 'The tired trainer/instructor ran home'）。在困惑度（新闻、维基百科等）和下游任务上评估不同词性的增强效果；使用CKA和线性探测分析模型内部表示变化。

Result: 1) 概念增强在新闻领域最稳定降低困惑度，与词性无关；2) 动词增强提供最可靠的下游任务性能提升；3) CKA和线性探测显示，下游增益最大的条件不一定是最改变内部表示的条件。

Conclusion: 概念增强的有效性取决于概念信息在训练中引入的位置和方式；困惑度改善与下游性能提升的驱动机制可能不同，内部表示变化程度不直接决定下游表现。

Abstract: Large language models are typically trained with next-token prediction (NTP), which rewards predicting specific tokens rather than explicitly accounting for the semantic similarities between different words or expressions. Prior work has addressed this limitation by augmenting training data with minimally varying sentences that replace a target word with conceptually related alternatives, e.g., "The tired coach ran home" $\rightarrow$ {"The tired trainer ran home", "The tired instructor ran home", ...}. Such concept augmentation improves performance, but it remains unclear whether its effectiveness depends on where in the sentence the concept is expressed. We reproduce and extend prior work $\textit{beyond nouns}$ to verbs and adjectives, asking whether the part of speech (POS) carrying the concept signal affects perplexity and downstream performance. We find different patterns across evaluation settings: concept augmentation improves perplexity most consistently on news, independent of the POS used, whereas verbs provide the most reliable downstream gains. Despite these behavioral differences, representational analyses using CKA and linear probing show that the conditions yielding the largest downstream gains are not necessarily those that most alter the model's internal representations. Together, these results suggest that the effectiveness of concept augmentation depends on where and how conceptual information is introduced during training.

</details>


### [75] [Investigating the Impact of Learner Errors on Social Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29896)
*Federico Marcuzzi,Martin Christoph,Iryna Gurevych,Alla Rozovskays*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了非母语英语使用者的语法错误如何影响大语言模型的社会偏见评估，发现语法错误会增加偏见，且错误率越高偏见增大越明显；介词错误影响最大。在含错误文本上微调可缓解该问题。现有仅基于流利英语的偏见基准可能低估非母语用户面临的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会偏见基准主要基于流利、规范的母语英语评估大语言模型，忽略了非母语写作者的使用场景，导致评估结果无法代表真实用户群体，尤其是语法错误可能改变模型行为这一问题未被探究。

Method: 构建了一个受控框架，将四类常见学习者语法错误（介词、冠词、动词形式、名词数）以不同比例注入提示词，并应用于三个涵盖性别、种族、宗教的刻板印象一致性和人口统计偏好基准，系统测量错误率与错误类型对偏见的影响；同时实验了在含错误文本上微调模型的效果。

Result: 语法错误显著增加模型偏见，且偏见随错误率升高而增强。四类错误中，介词错误导致刻板印象一致性增加最显著且最一致。分析表明错误在干扰理解时更易放大偏见。在含错误文本上微调可有效降低错误引发的偏见增幅。

Conclusion: 仅基于规范英语的偏见评估会低估非母语用户实际遭遇的偏见水平。引入真实语法错误的评估框架更能反映模型在多语言、多能力用户群体中的公平性表现，且通过错误文本微调可提升模型对非规范输入的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Existing social bias benchmarks evaluate large language models mostly on fluent, well-formed English, reflecting native speaker usage and leaving non-native writers unrepresented. To address this gap, we investigate how grammatical errors by non-native writers affect social bias. We develop a controlled framework that injects common learner errors into prompts and apply it to three bias benchmarks covering stereotype alignment and demographic preference across gender, race, and religion. We find that grammatical errors increase bias, and the effect grows with the error rate. Among the four common error types we consider, preposition errors produce the largest and most consistent increase in stereotype alignment. Our analysis suggests that errors increase bias when they are more likely to interfere with comprehension. Finally, fine-tuning on erroneous texts reduces the bias increase caused by grammatical errors. Our findings show that bias evaluations based exclusively on well-formed prompts may underestimate the bias experienced by users whose English contains grammatical errors.

</details>


### [76] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Detecting Satirical Code-Mixed Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29907)
*Răzvan-Alexandru Smădu,Ioana Buhnila,Daniela Tanase,Dumitru-Clementin Cercel,Florin Pop,Mihaela-Claudia Cercel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究罗马尼亚语代码切换对讽刺检测的影响，提出四种合成代码混合数据生成方法，构建包含60万+样本的CS-SeLeRoSa数据集，评估多语言大模型在零样本、思维链和微调设置下的表现。新模型如Gemma 4 31B零样本达68.1%，微调后在合成集达77.5%、金标准集达72.6%，超越前沿模型零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码切换是自然语言现象，但其对高层推理任务（如讽刺检测）在低资源语言（如罗马尼亚语）中的影响鲜有探索。讽刺检测高度依赖细微文化和句法线索，大语言模型难以捕捉。

Method: 基于现有罗马尼亚语讽刺检测数据集SeLeRoSa，采用四种方法生成合成代码混合句子：随机替换、短语翻译、依存句法分析和大语言模型生成。构建CS-SeLeRoSa（60万+样本）和人工裁决的CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold基准。在零样本、思维链、微调三种范式下评估多语言大模型。

Result: 旧开源模型性能大幅下降（37%-55% macro-F1）。新模型Gemma 4 31B零样本达68.1%，接近GPT-5 mini的69.5%。微调显著减少格式错误并提升性能：Gemma 4 31B在CS-SeLeRoSa达77.5%，在金标准集达72.6%，超越前沿模型零样本基线。

Conclusion: 合成代码混合数据生成对低资源语言有效；新一代多语言大模型能较好处理代码切换；微调能显著提升讽刺检测任务上的代码切换鲁棒性，性能超越未微调的前沿模型。

Abstract: Code-switching is a natural linguistic phenomenon, yet its impact on high-level reasoning tasks remains largely unexplored in under-resourced languages like Romanian. Natural language processing tasks, such as satire detection, rely heavily on nuanced cultural and syntactic cues that large language models struggle to capture. In this paper, we investigate four methods (i.e., random replacement, phrase translation, dependency parsing, and large language models) for generating synthetic code-mixed Romanian sentences with English, German, French, and Italian. We start with an existing dataset for satire detection, compiled from Romanian news articles and annotated at the sentence level by humans, and augment it with over 600k samples, namely CS-SeLeRoSa. We provide an in-depth analysis of the dataset generation process. Then, we evaluate several multilingual large language models across zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and fine-tuning regimes on both the synthetic corpus and a human-adjudicated gold benchmark, namely CS-SeLeRoSa-Gold. While older open models suffer substantial degradation (dropping to 37%-55% macro-F1), newer models like Gemma 4 31B achieve 68.1% zero-shot, rivaling frontier models like GPT-5 mini (69.5%). Furthermore, fine-tuning significantly reduces formatting errors and improves the results, increasing Gemma 4 31B to 77.5% on CS-SeLeRoSa and 72.6% on the gold standard, outperforming frontier zero-shot baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Unintended Side Effects of Value Training in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29922)
*Siddhesh Pawar,Chen Cecilia Liu,Anna Korhonen,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型（LLM）在后训练阶段的价值对齐问题，发现针对特定霍夫斯泰德价值维度的微调会产生意外副作用，影响未训练价值、风险偏好、社会认知和安全特性，建议引入更广泛的行为评估并探索价值训练在流水线中的最佳位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在价值敏感场景的部署增加，价值对齐成为后训练目标。但当前评估狭窄，价值之间相互关联，不清楚针对特定价值的微调如何改变无关能力和行为，因此需要系统研究跨价值迁移和更广泛行为影响。

Method: 选取四个指令微调的LLM，分别在霍夫斯泰德文化价值维度的单个维度上进行适配训练，随后在跨价值迁移和更广泛行为（未训练价值、风险偏好、社会认知、安全特性）上进行评估。

Result: 特定价值训练会产生意外副作用：显著改变未训练价值维度的表现，并影响风险偏好、社会认知能力和安全相关特质，表明价值对齐存在不可忽视的溢出效应。

Conclusion: 后训练阶段的价值对齐应纳入更广泛的行为评估体系，而非仅关注目标价值；同时需进一步研究额外价值训练在训练流水线中的最佳插入位置，以减少负面副作用。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed in value-sensitive contexts, aligning them with human values has emerged as a post-training objective in NLP, aimed at instilling additional values and guiding model behaviour. However, current evaluations remain narrow. Because values are interconnected, it is unclear how fine-tuning for specific values alters unrelated capabilities and behaviours. In this work, we adapt four instruction-tuned LLMs across individual Hofstede's value dimensions and evaluate them on both cross-value transfer and broader behaviours. We find that value-specific training produces unintended side effects on untrained values, risk preferences, social cognition, and safety traits. Consequently, we suggest that value alignment after post-training should incorporate broader behavioral evaluations, and we highlight the need to investigate where additional value training might best fit into the pipeline.

</details>


### [78] [Task-Preserving Multilingual Instruction Tuning for Open-Ended QA in WMT 2026 MIST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29926)
*Aleix Sant,Jordi Luque,Carlos Escolano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了提交到 WMT 2026 多语言指令共享任务的系统：使用 LoRA 微调 Qwen3-8B，结合组织者提供的数据、EuroAlpaca（任务保留本地化构建的多语言指令数据集）和 CrossEuroAlpaca（跨语言增强数据），针对十种目标语言的开放式问答（QA-OEG）。在匹配解码下，适配后模型在目标语言 QA-OEG 上的 Gemma-4 评分平均提升 7.8%；在 Aya 评估套件上，宏平均 ROUGE-L 提升 41.5%，BERTScore-F1 提升 2.9%，目标语言增益更大。但提升具有任务和语言特异性：目标语言上 QA-OEG 和摘要任务提升，但上下文依赖问答下降；非目标语言上三个任务均下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过高效参数微调（LoRA）和多语言指令数据增强，提升大语言模型在多语言开放式问答任务上的表现，同时探究跨语言数据增强对不同任务和语言的影响。

Method: 基于 Qwen3-8B 应用 LoRA 微调，训练数据包括组织者提供的共享任务数据、EuroAlpaca（任务保留本地化生成的多语言指令数据）和 CrossEuroAlpaca（将指令、上下文和回答分配不同源/目标语言的跨语言增强数据）。采用匹配解码进行推理。

Result: 目标语言 QA-OEG 的 Gemma-4 评分相对提升 7.8%。Aya 评估套件全语言宏平均 ROUGE-L 提升 41.5%，BERTScore-F1 提升 2.9%，目标语言增益更大。但任务和语言差异显著：目标语言上 QA-OEG 和摘要提升，上下文依赖问答下降；非目标语言上三任务均下降。

Conclusion: LoRA 结合多语言指令数据和跨语言增强能显著提升目标语言的开放式问答性能，但收益具有任务和语言特异性，可能损害上下文依赖问答和非目标语言表现，需权衡任务覆盖与语言泛化。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the WMT 2026 Multilingual Instruction Shared Task. We adapt Qwen3-8B with LoRA using organiser-provided data, EuroAlpaca, a multilingual instruction dataset created through task-preserving localisation, and CrossEuroAlpaca, a cross-lingual augmentation that assigns different source and target languages to instructions, contexts and responses. Our system targets open-ended question answering (QA-OEG) in ten shared-task languages represented in the auxiliary data. Under matched decoding, adaptation improves the mean Gemma-4 judge score on target-language QA-OEG by 7.8% relative to the base model. On an additional evaluation using the Aya Evaluation Suite across all shared-task languages, it yields relative gains of 41.5% in macro-averaged ROUGE-L and 2.9% in BERTScore-F1, with larger improvements in target languages. However, these gains are task- and language-specific. Gemma-4 scores improve for QA-OEG and summarisation in the target languages but decline for context-grounded QA there and across all three tasks in the non-target languages.

</details>


### [79] [Graph Topology Meets Energy-Driven Pathfinding: Preserving Contextual Consistency in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29933)
*Yufeng Li,Zaijing Li,Fuchen Zhang,Zongzong Wu,Ming Zhao,Fengxiao Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ThreadRAG，一种基于语义拓扑的RAG框架，通过语义拓扑映射器（STM）构建保留上下文的语义节点和语义拓扑图（STG），并引入能量驱动路径搜索（EDP）进行查询相关子图识别与线性化，解决了知识图谱增强RAG中的语义丢失和模态错位问题，在多跳问答基准上显著超越基线并大幅降低Token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG结合知识图谱（KG）进行多跳问答时，面临两大瓶颈：(i) 严重的语义损失：将连续文本离散化为符号会剥离上下文细微差别和叙事连贯性；(ii) 显著的模态错位：碎片化的图结构与LLM预训练的连续文本模态冲突，而将其重写为自然语言又会引入生成噪声并遗漏信息。

Method: ThreadRAG包含两大核心组件：(1) 语义拓扑映射器（STM）：将检索文档组织为保留上下文的语义节点，并通过叙事边、语义边、词汇边构建语义拓扑图（STG）；(2) 能量驱动路径搜索（EDP）：利用能量扩散识别查询相关子图，并确定性地将其线性化为连贯的文本证据路径。

Result: 在三个多跳问答基准上实验表明，ThreadRAG持续优于强基线。在HotpotQA上较以文本为中心的方法提升14.4% EM；在2WikiMultihop上超越最佳基线5.8% EM；Token成本仅为最强基线的23.3%。

Conclusion: ThreadRAG通过语义拓扑建模和能量驱动路径推理，有效缓解了KG增强RAG中的语义丢失与模态错位，实现了上下文保真、模态原生的证据推理，显著提升多跳问答性能并大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frequently integrates knowledge graphs (KGs) for complex multi-hop question answering. However, their performance is increasingly bottlenecked by the knowledge structuring and reasoning processes. (i) Severe semantic loss: Distilling continuous texts into discrete symbols strips away contextual nuances and narrative coherence. (ii) Pronounced modality mismatch: Fragmented graph structures clash with the continuous texts LLMs are pre-trained on, while rewriting them into natural language introduces generative noise and omits information. To address these limitations, we propose ThreadRAG, a semantic-topology-based RAG framework for context-preserving knowledge structuring and modality-native evidence reasoning. ThreadRAG introduces a Semantic Topology Mapper (STM), which organizes retrieved documents into context-intact Semantic Nodes and constructs a Semantic Topology Graph (STG) through narrative, semantic, and lexical edges. It further incorporates Energy-Driven Pathfinding (EDP), which identifies query-relevant subgraphs via energy diffusion and deterministically linearizes them into coherent textual evidence paths. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ThreadRAG consistently outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it achieves a 14.4% EM improvement over text-centric methods on HotpotQA and surpasses the best baseline by 5.8% EM on 2WikiMultihop, while reducing token cost to 23.3% of the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [80] [Towards English - Mizo Legal Machine Translation: Corpus Creation, Analysis, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29943)
*Telem Joyson Singh,Sanabam Boyshana,Sanasam Ranbir Singh,Priyankoo Sarmah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个英语-米佐语印度法院判决平行语料库（13,168句对），由专业母语译者翻译并人工对齐，发现法律术语借用英语而日常词汇用本族语，变音符号使用不一致；微调NLLB-200 1.3B模型后BLEU提升7.16至27.47，但模型仍过度使用英语词汇（约为人工3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度法庭以英语审理，导致缺乏翻译技术的语言使用者难以获取司法文件；米佐语为低资源语言，缺乏法律领域平行语料，阻碍机器翻译系统的开发与评测。

Method: 从 Gauhati 高等法院和印度最高法院判决中收集文档，聘请专业米佐语母语译者逐文档翻译，人工进行句级对齐，并由两名独立评审员复核；构建训练/测试集，统计词汇借用模式与变音符号一致性；在 NLLB-200 1.3B 基座模型上微调并评估 BLEU 与英语词保留率。

Result: 微调模型在 1,000 句平衡法律测试集上达到 27.47 BLEU，较基座模型提升 7.16 BLEU；语料分析显示官方/法律术语几乎全保留英语，动作/情境/结果等日常词汇多用米佐语本族词，变音符号使用不一致；模型生成的英语词频率约为人工翻译的三倍。

Conclusion: 高质量人工翻译与对齐的法律平行语料能显著提升低资源语言法律机器翻译质量，但模型仍存在过度借用源语词汇的倾向，需进一步研究词汇选择控制与变音符号规范化以逼近人工翻译风格。

Abstract: Indian court proceedings are held in English, so speakers of languages with limited translation technology have limited access to them. We present the first English Mizo parallel dataset of Indian court judgments. It contains 13,168 sentence pairs from the Gauhati High Court and the Supreme Court of India. Professional native Mizo translators translated complete documents; sentence pairs were manually aligned, and two independent reviewers checked the results. Our study finds that English words are borrowed in a very selective way: official and legal terms are almost always kept in English, but words for everyday things like actions, situations, and results are usually expressed in native Mizo. Mizo diacritics are also used inconsistently across the corpus. When we fine tune the NLLB 200 1.3B model on our training data, it scores 27.47 BLEU on a balanced legal test set of 1,000 sentence pairs, an improvement of 7.16 BLEU over the base model. However, it still uses English words about three times more often than a human translator does.

</details>


### [81] [CulturalRiddles: A Multicultural Benchmark of Riddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29968)
*My Chiffon Nguyen,Tianyi Hu,Vinod Anbalagan,Henry Gagnier,Pouya Sadeghi,Farah Abdou,Tidor-Vlad Pricope,Diego MMS Oliveira,Jocelyn K D'Arcy,Madeline Smith,Oluwaseun A Ajayi,Yusser Al Ghussin,Zena Al-Khalili,Micol Altomare,Shayan Amir,Ahanaf Aziz,Bryan Chen,Ruchira Dhar,Abdellah El Mekki,Rawan Mohammed Elframawy,Akhil Eppa,Silvia Fernandez,Alex Gimei,Arya Hariharan,Christian Hoang,RamMohanRao Kadiyala,Diana Kozachek,Peerat Limkonchotiwat,Ankita Maity,Di Mo,Ayush Munot,Aylin Naebzadeh,Anastasiia Nikitina,Dina Pisarevskaya,Divas Poudel,Jebish Purbey,Md Rabius Sany Apu,Ahmad Mufli Ramadhan,Ralitza Nikolova Rizova,Diogo M. Serrano-Miranda,Vivek Gopalkrishna Silimkhan,Luis Fernando Solis Navarro,Kaiser Sun,Arya Suneesh,Manaswitha Sunkara,Tejaswi Sunkara,Jonathan E Swindell,Hanna Yukhymenko,Jingyi Zheng,Esther Adenuga,Ilia Afanasev,Wafa Aissa,Lisa Alazraki,Lauren C Altomare,Priscilla Amondi Amuok,Emile Anand,Gabriel Assis,José Matías Avalos,Silvia Lorena Avila,Zakaria Baannou,Niyati Bafna,Yash Bagla,Sarah Bahrami,Bontu F Balcha,Vanya Bannihatti Kumar,Irene Baucells,Abdumutalib Begmuratov,Naman Bhatia,Souvik Bhattacharyya,Isabel Blanco Ferreiro,Felipe Bochini,Sayuru Bopitiya,Angana Borah,Tulika Bose,Olena Bugaiova,Juliana Carvalho,Nancy F Chen,Stephy Chen,Rochelle Choenni,Jayanth Krishna Chundru,Leandro A Cossio,Nebojša Damnjanović,Ona de Gibert,Marco De Santis,Pedro Delgado,Kaustubh Dholé,Adnan El Assadi,Wesam El-Sayed,Nadine M Elframawy,Oumayma Essarhi,Naome A Etori,Reuben Chagas Fernandes,Saanidhi Pravin Gade,Javier García Gilabert,Álvaro González-Escapa,Bin Hu,Kaiyu Huang,Abderrahmane Issam,Danka Jokić,Ulugbek Khalilov,Dominik Krzemiński,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Haichang Li,Chen Cecilia Liu,Zhengyuan Liu,Cedric Lothritz,Houman Mehrafarin,Antonio Miranda-Escalada,Fengran Mo,Mehrnaz Mofakhami,Wafaa Mohammed,Felipe Moreno-Vera,Prahitha Movva,Jannatul Nayem,Van Ngo,Joy Olusanya,Marc Pàmies,Avinash Kuney Pandey,Yurii Paniv,Fred Philippy,Vladislav Poritski,Dikshya Poudel,Joanna Radoła,Sara Rajaee,Swati Rajwal,Prerana Rane,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Lucas Resck,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Ananya Sahu,Eduardo Sánchez,Borja Sánchez Clemente,Aleix Sant,Nobin Sarwar,Istiak Shihab,Santhoshini Pranathi Singaraju,Jane Smith,David Stap,Emily Z Su,Ananth K Suresh,Sajag Swami,Ruiyang Tan,Nazia Tasnim,Khalid Tounoussi,Patteera Triamamornwooth,Muhammad Saad Umar,Shankar Venkitachalam,Oksana Volchek,Lingbo Wang,Debela Desalegn Yadeta,Rushikesh Zawar,Jinghan Zhang,Mike Zhang,Weihua Zheng,Marzieh Fadaee,Akhil Arora,Károly Boczka,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CulturalRiddles 基准，包含 62 种文化（45 国、51 语言）的原创谜语，通过社区参与式方式由人类创建，用于评估大语言模型在多元文化背景下的理解与推理能力。实验显示即使是最先进的模型在某些社区中准确率也仅 22% 左右，凸显了当前模型在文化知识应用上的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型语言覆盖范围扩大，评估其对多元文化的理解与表征能力变得至关重要。现有基准多以英语为中心，文化覆盖有限，且侧重事实知识而非在真实文化语境中的应用。

Method: 构建 CulturalRiddles 基准：通过参与式社区努力，由人类创作深植于各自文化的原创谜语，涵盖 62 种文化（45 国家、51 语言）。这些谜语要求溯因推理、组合推理、详细事实与社会知识、语言技能及文化特定联想理解。对 28 种 LLM 配置进行自动评估与大规模人工评估。

Result: 即使是前沿模型，在某些社区中的准确率也仅达到 22% 左右，显示出模型在文化知识应用与推理上的显著不足。

Conclusion: CulturalRiddles 揭示了当前大语言模型在多元文化理解与推理上的严重局限。作者公开发布所有工件并建立开放贡献流程，以持续扩展基准的语言与文化覆盖范围。

Abstract: As LLMs expand their language coverage, evaluating how well they understand and represent diverse cultures becomes increasingly important. However, many benchmarks remain English-centric, with limited cultural coverage and a focus on factual knowledge over its application in real-world cultural contexts. We propose CulturalRiddles, a benchmark of original, culturally grounded riddles created entirely by humans through a participatory community effort, spanning 62 cultures (45 countries, 51 languages). These riddles are deeply rooted in their respective cultures and require abductive and compositional reasoning, alongside detailed factual and social knowledge, language skills, and an understanding of culturally specific associations. We benchmark 28 LLM configurations using both automatic and large-scale human evaluation. Even frontier models achieve at most 22% accuracy in some communities. We publicly release all artifacts alongside an open contribution process to expand the benchmark's linguistic and cultural coverage.

</details>


### [82] [Remembering Through Change: Provenance-Preserving Hypergraph Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29995)
*Jielong Tang,Ling-An Zeng,Xujie Yuan,Xiao Dong,Zhiqian Xia,Qingfeng Li,Shixiong Kai,Kaichao Liang,Rong Yao,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MHG-Mem，一种多模态超图记忆架构，通过超图结构联合组织实体身份、状态演化与视觉证据溯源，解决长期多模态代理在保留细粒度视觉细节与跨会话实体追踪之间的矛盾，在三个基准上比最强基线提升 8.20-9.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆方案面临两难：基于抽象的记忆将图像压缩为文本，丢失视觉判别细节；基于像素保留的记忆虽保留原始观测，但难以可靠关联多样观测与持久实体身份及其演化状态。需要一种机制同时保留细粒度视觉证据并支持跨会话实体身份与状态演化的追踪。

Method: MHG-Mem 采用三层超图结构：(1) 发生超边 将身份一致的实体提及与观测事实聚类为节点组；(2) 演化超边 从分组观测合成方面特定的演化状态，并链接至支撑实体、事实；(3) 证据超边 将演化状态追溯至原始视觉观测。引入基于超图的代理式搜索：自适应选择入口层、遍历类型化超边跨层导航、在需要细粒度验证时回溯源观测。

Result: 在三个长期多模态基准上进行大量实验，MHG-Mem 相对最强基线提升 8.20%-9.98%，验证了其在细粒度视觉验证与跨会话状态推理任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MHG-Mem 通过超图联合建模实体身份、状态演化与视觉溯源，配合代理式超图搜索，成功兼顾了视觉细节保留与跨会话实体演化推理，为长期多模态代理提供了可扩展且可验证的记忆范式。

Abstract: Long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging, as it must preserve fine-grained visual evidence while tracking entity evolution across sessions. Existing abstraction-based memories compress images into text, often discarding visually discriminative details, whereas pixel-preserving memories retain raw observations but struggle to reliably associate diverse observations with persistent entity identities and track their evolving states. To address these limitations, we propose MHG-Mem, a multimodal hypergraph memory that jointly organizes entity identity, state evolution, and provenance to visual evidence. Specifically, MHG-Mem represents distinct entity mentions and their observed facts as individual nodes, grouping identity-consistent mentions and facts through occurrence hyperedges. It then synthesizes aspect-specific evolution states from these grouped observations and links them to supporting entities, facts, and source evidence through evolutionary and evidential hyperedges. To navigate this structured memory, we introduce hypergraph-based agentic search, which adaptively selects an entry layer, traverses typed hyperedges across memory levels, and inspects source observations when fine-grained verification is required. These designs support both detailed visual verification and reasoning over cross-session state changes. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of MHG-Mem, improving performance by 8.20-9.98% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [83] [Building Auditable AI Text Detectors with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30006)
*Liam Dugan,Callum McDougall,Matthew Denton,Philip Pham,Christine Kaeser-Chen,Chris Callison-Burch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流程，将不透明的微调神经AI文本检测器转换为基于人类可读概念的可解释分类器：通过在检测器的残差流上训练稀疏自编码器(SAE)，自动解释每个SAE特征，仅保留通过全局保真度阈值的特征，再训练轻量级分类器。该方法恢复了对应AI文本特定语言“特征”的潜在概念，实现了高可解释性且在分布外数据上优于神经网络和文体统计两类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本检测器已广泛部署于同行评审、教育、招生、新闻、内容审核等高风险场景，其输出直接影响个人。现有最强检测器为微调神经分类器，决策不透明；透明替代方案（文体/语言学特征分类器）性能大幅落后。亟需兼具可解释性与高性能的检测器。

Method: 1) 在预训练检测器的残差流上训练稀疏自编码器(SAE)；2) 为每个SAE特征生成自动解释标签；3) 仅保留通过全局保真度阈值的可解释特征；4) 在筛选后的特征上训练轻量级分类器。

Result: SAE成功恢复了对应AI生成文本高度特定且具预测性的语言“特征”潜在概念。基于这些提取特征的分类器不仅高度可解释，在分布外文本上表现优于神经网络检测器和文体统计检测器。

Conclusion: 该流程成功弥合了AI文本检测中可解释性与性能的鸿沟，使透明、高性能的检测器能够部署于高风险场景。

Abstract: AI-generated text detectors are increasingly deployed in consequential settings---peer review, education, admissions, journalism, and content moderation---where their outputs directly affect individuals. Yet the strongest detectors are fine-tuned neural classifiers whose decisions are opaque, while transparent alternatives (stylometric and linguistic-feature classifiers) underperform them by wide margins. We present a pipeline that converts a fine-tuned neural detector into a classifier that operates on human-readable concepts. We accomplish this by training sparse autoencoders (SAEs) directly on a detector's residual stream, assigning auto-interpretation labels to each SAE feature, and retaining only features whose generated descriptions pass a global fidelity threshold. We then train a lightweight classifier on just the interpretable features. We find that our SAEs recover latent concepts that correspond to highly specific and predictive linguistic "tells" of AI-generated text. Classifiers built on these extracted features are not only highly interpretable but exhibit stronger performance on out-of-distribution texts than either neural or stylometric alternatives.

</details>


### [84] [Looped Uniform Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30066)
*Saiteja Utpala,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Yuki Mitsufuji,Praneeth Vepakomma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 统一扩散语言模型（UDLMs）引入选择性循环机制，在保持训练目标和采样器不变的情况下，显著提升训练效率和生成质量。循环UDLMs在OpenWebText上用2.8倍更少FLOPs、FineWeb-Edu上用3.3倍更少FLOPs达到相同验证NLL；在相同NFE预算下生成困惑度降低达9.8%；非均匀循环计数采样策略使推理时循环次数减少12%且不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UDLMs虽比掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）训练更耗费FLOPs，但下游推理性能更强。选择性循环已在MDLMs中证明能提升iso-FLOP预算下的训练效率，因此研究其在UDLMs中的效果。

Method: 在UDLMs中应用选择性循环——在每个去噪步骤中递归应用早期Transformer层，保持训练目标和采样器不变。实验对比不同循环计数分布（均匀分布 vs 偏向小循环计数的分布），在OpenWebText和FineWeb-Edu上评估。

Result: 循环UDLMs以2.8倍（OpenWebText）和3.3倍（FineWeb-Edu）更少的训练FLOPs达到非循环基线的验证NLL；相同NFE预算下生成困惑度最多降低9.8%，中等循环次数收益最大；训练时更频繁采样小循环计数，推理时循环次数减少12%且下游任务准确率不降。

Conclusion: 选择性循环能大幅提升UDLMs的训练效率与生成质量，且通过调整循环计数采样分布可进一步优化推理效率，为扩散语言模型的实用化部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising sequences of ordinary vocabulary tokens, any of which may be corrupted and subsequently revised. During training, UDLMs must denoise from a context that may itself be unreliable, whereas masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) always condition on a clean context. This challenging training objective makes UDLMs more FLOP-intensive to train than MDLMs, but it yields stronger downstream reasoning performance. Recently, selective looping, which recursively applies a few early Transformer layers within each denoising step, has been shown to improve training efficiency in MDLMs under an iso-FLOP budget. We investigate looping in UDLMs while keeping the training objective and sampler unchanged. We find that looping significantly improves training efficiency: looped UDLMs reach the validation NLL of a non-looped UDLM trained on about 0.5 trillion tokens with up to 2.8x fewer training FLOPs on OpenWebText and 3.3x fewer on FineWeb-Edu. We also find that, at a matched NFE budget, looped UDLMs reach up to 9.8% lower generation perplexity than the non-looped UDLM, with most gains achieved at moderate loop counts. Finally, we study different loop-count distributions and find that sampling smaller loop counts more frequently during training improves inference efficiency: on downstream tasks, this strategy uses 12% fewer loops than uniform loop-count sampling without degrading accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [GoiBERT: Compact Vietnamese Language Models via Two-Stage Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30067)
*Quang Anh Kiet Tran,Nam Anh Tran,Manh Hung Trinh,Thanh Danh Le,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Huynh Dai Nhan Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GoiBERT，一系列紧凑的越南语编码器（26M和49M参数），直接在原始文本上运行，无需外部分词。通过双教师蒸馏（同分词器越南语编码器教通用表示，大模型教任务级软标签），在八个基准上取得最优非教师模型平均分，参数量减半且吞吐率翻倍。GoiBERT-base保留99%基线性能且体积仅为四分之一。压缩对情感和分类任务近乎无损，但对推理和实体识别仍有损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最强越南语预训练编码器多为大型多语言词表模型或依赖外部分词，部署成本高。需要轻量、直接处理原始文本且性能强劲的越南语编码器。

Method: 采用双教师知识蒸馏：1) 同分词器越南语编码器作为教师提供通用表示蒸馏；2) 更大规模教师模型生成软标签提供任务级指导，且无需标注其自身训练数据。学生模型为两个紧凑编码器（GoiBERT-base 26M，GoiBERT-large 49M），直接在原始文本上操作。

Result: 在文本分类、基于方面的情感分析、自然语言推理、命名实体识别八个基准上，GoiBERT-large在非教师模型中平均分最高，参数量不足最强紧凑基线一半，吞吐率提升两倍。GoiBERT-base以四分之一参数量保留基线99%性能。分析显示压缩对情感和分类任务近乎无损，但对推理和实体识别仍有显著损失。

Conclusion: GoiBERT证明了通过双教师蒸馏可训练出极紧凑且高性能的越南语编码器，大幅降低部署成本。未来工作方向包括改进推理和实体识别任务上的压缩鲁棒性。代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Vietnamese pre-trained encoders have improved rapidly, but the strongest ones are large, multilingual-vocabulary models or depend on external word segmentation, which makes them costly to deploy. We present GoiBERT, a family of two compact Vietnamese encoders with 26M and 49M parameters that operate directly on raw text. GoiBERT learns from two complementary teachers. A same-tokenizer Vietnamese encoder first teaches general-purpose representations. A much larger teacher then provides task-level guidance through soft labels that are generated without ever labeling its own training data. Across eight benchmarks covering text classification, aspect-based sentiment analysis, natural language inference, and named entity recognition, GoiBERT-large achieves the highest average score among non-teacher models. It does so with fewer than half the parameters of the strongest compact baseline and twice its throughput. GoiBERT-base retains 99% of that baseline's performance at a quarter of its size. Our analysis shows that compression is nearly lossless for sentiment and classification tasks but remains costly for inference and entity recognition, which points to clear directions for future work. Our code and models are available at: https://github.com/Nhantran65/GoiBERT.git

</details>


### [86] [Did the Problem Actually Get Harder? Testing LLMs on Paired P and NP-Complete Problems Hardened by Adversarial Multi-Agent Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30103)
*Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Zabir Al Nazi,Mahbub E Sobhani,Shubhashis Roy Dipta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了P2NPCBench基准，包含12对同表面P/NP完全问题，通过AgentGAN对抗生成消除教科书线索的硬化题目，评测显示所有模型在NPC问题上显著弱于P孪生问题（35.4% vs 63.8%），且延长时间限制难以改善，揭示了模型对复杂度类边界的推理能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么固定任务措辞，要么忽略复杂度类别，无法区分模型是靠记忆教科书模式还是真正推理复杂度边界。需要一个能隔离单一结构差异（如3-着色vs2-着色）且消除表层线索的基准，来测试模型是否真正理解问题的复杂度类归属。

Method: 构建P2NPCBench：1) 选取12对P/NPC孪生问题族；2) 设计AgentGAN对抗框架——生成器重写题目直到冻结的代码模型面板失效，同时可执行有效性门控保证答案与类别不变；3) 产出2114条硬化陈述并经人工验证参考解；4) 对12个专有/开源模型进行pass@1评测，并测试十倍时间限制的影响。

Result: 所有模型在NPC陈述上均低于P孪生（35.4% vs 63.8% pass@1），95.1%模型-族对在NPC侧失败更多。十倍时间限制仅将NPC准确率从28.5%提至31.9%，失败源于程序错误/崩溃而非超时。硬化过程将类别差距从24.8点缩至14.6点，表明部分原始差距源于教科书措辞熟悉度而非复杂度类本身。

Conclusion: 当前大模型难以通过推理识别P/NPC边界，主要依赖表层模式匹配。P2NPCBench为评估真正的复杂度类推理能力提供了严格基准，揭示了模型在结构性复杂度判断上的根本短板。

Abstract: Many NP-complete (NPC) problems have a polynomial-time (P) twin that differs in a single structural detail, such as 3- versus 2-colouring a graph. A model that reasons rather than recalls should notice which side of this boundary a problem falls on, yet existing benchmarks either fix the wording of each task or ignore its complexity class, so they cannot separate the two. We introduce P2NPCBench, a benchmark of 12 problem families in which every NPC problem is paired with a P twin posed over the same surface. To remove the textbook cues that enable recall, we build it with AgentGAN, an adversarial multi-agent framework in which a generator rewrites each statement until a panel of frozen code models fails, while an executable validity gate keeps every rewrite answer- and class-preserving. The result is 2,114 hardened statements with hand-verified reference human solvers. Evaluating 12 proprietary and open-source models, we find that every model scores lower on NPC statements than on their P twins (35.4% vs. 63.8% pass@1), and that 95.1% of model-family pairs fail more often on the NPC side. A tenfold time limit raises NPC accuracy only from 28.5% to 31.9%, so failures stem from wrong or crashing programs rather than slow ones. Hardening also narrows the class gap from 24.8 to 14.6 points, showing that part of the apparent gap reflects familiarity with textbook phrasing rather than complexity class.

</details>


### [87] [Quant-Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Quantitative Trading Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30113)
*Houde Qian,Zheng Jiang,Wenyi Li,Chenxi Xu,Xinjie Lin,Yuzhao Peng,Ruoyu Han,Youjie Zheng,Kaisen Yang,Qinhuai Na,You Fu,Calvin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Quant-Arena，一个面向真实 A 股数据的端到端量化研究基准与公开排行榜，要求模型在 2022-2023 年构建特征与选股代码，冻结策略后在封存的 2024 年进行实盘约束评估，综合超额收益、信号质量、特征多样性打分。测试九个前沿模型，GPT-6 Astra 得分最高，但均未跑赢 2024 年等权市场基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估单一公式、代码或交易决策，无法验证大语言模型能否完成从假设生成、代码实现、历史回测到策略筛选的完整量化研究闭环，并在样本外时期产生可行策略。

Method: 构建 Quant-Arena 基准：模型在 2022-2023 年训练期迭代生成特征工程与评分代码，仅接收该期间反馈；随后冻结单一策略，在封存的 2024 年测试期由现金账户评估器执行实盘约束（含基于模拟订单簿的市场冲击成本）；评分以实现超额收益为主，辅以信号质量与特征多样性指标。

Result: 评估九个前沿模型，GPT-6 Astra 综合得分最高；然而所有模型在 2024 年测试期均未能战胜等权市场组合，显示端到端量化研究在样本外仍面临显著挑战。

Conclusion: Quant-Arena 为端到端量化研究提供了可复现、可扩展的公开评测平台，揭示了当前最强模型在真实市场约束下的局限性，未来工作需聚焦提升样本外泛化能力与风险调整后收益。

Abstract: Quantitative research is an iterative loop: a researcher proposes a market hypothesis, implements it as an executable signal, tests it on historical data, and decides what to keep. Existing benchmarks score formulas, code, or individual trading decisions, leaving open whether large language models (LLMs) can complete this loop and produce strategies that work beyond the research period. We introduce Quant-Arena, a benchmark and public leaderboard for end-to-end quantitative research on real A-share market data. A model constructs features and stock-scoring code, receives feedback only on 2022--2023, and freezes one strategy for assessment on the sealed year 2024. A cash-account evaluator applies trading constraints and costs, including market impact estimated from a simulated order book. Scores are dominated by realized excess return and complemented by signal quality and the diversity of features used by the strategy. We evaluate nine frontier models, among which GPT-6 Astra scores highest; yet no model beats the equal-weight market in 2024. Quant-Arena is open to submissions from any model or agent harness.

</details>


### [88] [Environment Duels: A Microcosm of LLM Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30116)
*Shibo Jin,Zhiqiu Xu,Yiling Zou,Shangyu Gong,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“环境决斗”，一种动态智能体基准，用于评估大语言模型作为环境创建者（作者）和解题者的双重能力。实验显示解题与创作是不同技能，强解题者不一定是强作者；小模型从他人交互中学习更多，能设计更难环境和更有用提示。配对交互形成微观生态：胜负关系具有传递性、独立作者趋同设计、RL实验显示提示提升与训练收益相关。这些模式映射了模型间真实教学、挑战与学习动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在真实生态中通过合成训练管线和开放网络数据相互吸收彼此产出，但缺乏系统评估模型作为创造者和解题者的双重角色及其相互影响。需要一种受控基准来量化模型间教学、挑战与学习动态。

Method: 构建Environment Duels基准：九个前沿LLM各自编写交互式环境并附带特权提示，目标是让强解题者在无提示时失败、有提示时通过。采用配对决斗评估解题与作者能力，分析胜负传递性、作者设计趋同性，并用Qwen3.8-27B进行强化学习实验，考察提示提升与训练迁移收益的关系。

Result: 1. 解题与作者能力解耦：最强解题模型非最强作者模型。2. 小模型从他人交互学习更多，产出最难环境和最有用提示。3. 头对头结果具有传递性，形成稳定排名。4. 独立作者趋同于相似环境设计。5. RL实验中，学习者在作者环境上的提示提升，能预测其在保留推理与智能体基准上的训练提升，无论环境来自自身或他模型。

Conclusion: Environment Duels在受控条件下复现了LLM生态中的教学、挑战与相互学习模式，揭示了解题与创作技能的分离、模型间交互的传递性与趋同性，以及提示质量与训练迁移收益的对应关系。该基准为理解模型共同进化动态提供了窗口。

Abstract: Through a tangle of real-world interactions, from synthetic training pipelines to data shared on the open web, frontier LLMs absorb what their predecessors and competitors create. How well does each model play its role in this ecosystem, as a creator and as a solver? We introduce Environment Duels, a dynamic agentic benchmark that evaluates both capabilities on interactive environments. Each model writes environments, each with a privileged hint, aiming for tasks that capable solvers fail without the hint and pass with it. Across nine frontier LLMs, solving and authoring prove to be distinct skills: the strongest solvers are not the strongest authors, while smaller models learn more from how others played and write the hardest environments and the most useful hints. Beyond the scores, the pairwise interactions form a microcosm of how models affect one another. Head-to-head outcomes are transitive and so yield a stable ranking; independent authors converge on the same designs; and in a reinforcement-learning study with Qwen3.8-27B, the learner's hint lift on an author's environments tracks how much training on those environments improves it on held-out reasoning and agentic benchmarks, whether the environments are its own or another model's. Each of these patterns has a counterpart in how models shape one another outside the duel. By staging these interactions under controlled conditions, Environment Duels offers a window onto how LLMs teach, challenge and learn from one another. Code is available at https://github.com/LLM-Duels/EnvDuels.

</details>


### [89] [LongZero: Enhancing Long-Context Reasoning in LLMs from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30132)
*Zhennan Wan,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongZero 是一种无需外部数据的自我强化学习方法，让大语言模型通过自发生成结构化问答及证据，再扩展为长多文档上下文，结合多跳推理与多轮检索任务，利用 GRPO 训练，显著提升长上下文推理能力并泛化至真实任务，超越基于外部语料合成数据的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习提升长上下文推理需要长、多样、难、可验证的训练数据，但人工标注成本高、自然数据稀缺、现有合成方法依赖外部语料或更强教师模型，且随着模型变强难以获取。零数据 RL 在短上下文任务有效，但长上下文场景尚未探索。

Method: LongZero 让模型先生成问题、答案及支撑证据的紧凑结构化形式，再将证据单独扩展为长多文档上下文。将自编的多跳推理任务与多轮检索任务结合，采用 GRPO（组相对策略优化）进行训练，完全摆脱外部数据依赖。

Result: 在两个 LLMs 上跨五个长上下文推理基准评测，对比基于外部语料合成数据的 RL 基线。实验显示 LongZero 能泛化至真实任务，且性能优于两种基线方法。

Conclusion: LongZero 验证了无需外部数据即可通过自我进化提升 LLM 长上下文推理的可行性，为后续研究提供了极具前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is an effective way to strengthen the long-context reasoning of large language models (LLMs), but it needs training data that are long, diverse, challenging, and verifiable. Human annotation of such data is expensive, naturally occurring data are too scarce to sustain RL, and existing synthesis methods depend on external corpora or stronger teacher models, which become harder to obtain as the trained models get stronger. Recently, RL from zero data, in which a model learns from tasks it proposes itself, has achieved excellent performance, but existing methods target short-context tasks. Therefore, we propose $\textbf{LongZero}$, a method that lets an LLM improve its own long-context reasoning without any external data. Instead of writing questions and answers over existing documents, LongZero first has the model synthesize the question and answer with its supporting evidence in a compact structured form, and only then separately expands the evidence into a long multi-document context. LongZero combines these self-written multi-hop reasoning tasks with self-written multi-round retrieval tasks and trains the model with GRPO. We evaluate LongZero on two LLMs across five long-context reasoning benchmarks and compare it with RL on data synthesized from external corpora. Experimental results show that LongZero can generalize to real tasks, and outperforms both baselines, suggesting a viable and promising path to improving long-context reasoning of LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

### [90] [RESEARCHHUB: A Personalized Literature Discovery System for Biomedical Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28636)
*Viswonathan Manoranjan,Taeyoon Kim,Nicholas Do,Sang Woo Kim,Dawei Li,Zhen Tan,Tianlong Chen,Yunmei Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ResearchHub 是一个面向生物医学预印本的个性化文献发现交互系统，核心是 prism 双编码器检索器，结合 GPT-4o 生成的自然语言查询与混合损失训练，在 MedRxiv 语料上显著超越现有密集检索基线，并提供新颖性、多样性、课程化排序等可控推荐功能，端到端低延迟可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术推荐系统常陷入近似重复推荐，难以满足研究者对新颖性、多样性及从主流到专业的渐进式阅读需求；且通用密集检索器在生物医学预印本领域表现不足，缺乏领域适配的检索模型与交互式发现工具。

Method: 1. 构建 prism 双编码器检索器：利用 GPT-4o 生成自然语言查询扩充训练数据，采用混合 Multiple Negatives Ranking 与 MarginMSE 损失，并引入交叉编码器软标签蒸馏。2. 系统层面：基于用户阅读历史的前沿检索、DPP 多样性重排序、局部固有维度（LID）课程化排序（从主流到专业）、轻量级归因卡片解释。3. 在 44,937 篇 2023–2025 年 MedRxiv 论文上离线评估与在线部署。

Result: prism 在 Recall@10 上比强基线 SPECTER2-Search 提升 12.4 个百分点；系统支持可控新颖性、多样性及课程化排序，端到端延迟低，适合实时交互。

Conclusion: ResearchHub 通过领域适配的检索模型与多维度可控推荐机制，有效解决了生物医学预印本发现中的重复推荐问题，为研究者提供了高效、可解释的个性化文献探索工具，展示了大模型辅助训练与检索增强推荐相结合的实用价值。

Abstract: We present ResearchHub, an interactive system for personalized literature discovery over biomedical preprints. Given a researcher profile or a free-form query, ResearchHub retrieves relevant papers from a domain-specific MedRxiv corpus and helps users move beyond near-duplicate recommendations through controllable novelty, diversity, and curriculum ordering. At the core of the system is prism, a bi-encoder retriever adapted to biomedical preprints using GPT-4o-generated natural language queries and a hybrid Multiple Negatives Ranking plus MarginMSE objective with cross-encoder soft labels. On 44,937 MedRxiv papers from 2023--2025, prism outperforms strong off-the-shelf dense retrievers, improving Recall@10 over SPECTER2-Search by 12.4 percentage points. ResearchHub further supports frontier retrieval based on a user's reading history, DPP-based diversity re-ranking, Local Intrinsic Dimensionality ordering from mainstream to specialized papers, and lightweight attribution cards for result explanation. The full pipeline runs with low latency, making the system suitable

</details>


### [91] [TAGR: Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval across Heterogeneous Urban Delivery Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28638)
*Zhi Liu,Yurun Cai,Xiao Han,Zhi Zhen,Yuran Li,Guojiang Shen,Xiangjie Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TAGR（Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval）框架，针对城市即时配送中的需求预测、到达时间预测和下一站点预测三种异构任务，构建基于LLM的地理语义共享先验和任务特定检索库，实现区域知识共享同时保留任务特有证据，并引入任务自适应检索增强模块解决异构输出结构融合难题，在三个城市数据集上持续超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市居民对高质量即时配送需求增加，使得需求预测、ETA预测和下一站点预测成为关键任务。然而这三类任务依赖的历史证据在结构和粒度上差异巨大，且预测目标的输出形式不同，难以通过单一融合机制整合证据而不引发错配或负迁移，导致预测误差增加，进而造成资源分配不均衡，降低整体效率和服务质量。

Method: TAGR包含两大核心组件：1) 基于LLM的地理语义共享先验与任务特定检索库：利用LLM生成的区域表示编码地理上下文和区域间语义关系，提供可迁移的共享知识，无需在线LLM推理；同时为每个任务维护专用检索库以保留异构任务特有证据。2) 任务自适应检索增强模块：针对不同任务的异构输出结构动态调整检索融合策略，缓解统一检索融合策略导致的信息错配和负迁移。

Result: 在三个城市数据集上的实验表明，TAGR在需求预测、ETA预测和下一站点预测三个任务上均持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: TAGR通过LLM衍生的地理语义表示实现跨任务知识共享，并通过任务自适应检索增强有效处理异构输出结构，解决了城市配送多任务预测中的证据异构、融合错配和负迁移问题，显著提升了预测精度和资源分配效率。

Abstract: As urban residents demand higher-quality instant delivery services, demand forecasting, estimated time of arrival prediction, and next-stop prediction have become critical tasks in urban delivery systems. However, the three tasks rely on historical evidence with substantially different structures and granularities. Meanwhile, their prediction targets differ in output form, making it difficult to integrate retrieved evidence through a single fusion mechanism without causing mismatch or negative transfer. These issues can increase prediction errors and subsequently lead to imbalanced resource allocation, where some regions experience unmet demand. In contrast, others suffer from underutilization, ultimately degrading overall efficiency and service quality. To address these challenges, we propose TAGR, a Task-Adaptive Geo-Semantic Retrieval framework across heterogeneous urban delivery tasks. TAGR constructs an LLM-based geo-semantic shared prior and task-specific retrieval banks to share regional knowledge while preserving heterogeneous task-specific evidence. The LLM-derived regional representations encode geographic context and semantic relations among regions, providing transferable knowledge for all three tasks without requiring online LLM inference. It further introduces a task-adaptive retrieval augmentation module to accommodate heterogeneous output structures, thereby mitigating information mismatch and negative transfer caused by a uniform retrieval-fusion strategy. Experiments on three city datasets demonstrate that TAGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across multiple tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Can AI Chat Replace Traditional Search? Evidence from a RAG-based Live Events Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28639)
*Rakesh Allu,Evgeny Kagan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文通过预注册的激励在线实验，比较了经典过滤界面（Filter）、生成式AI聊天界面（Chat）及两者结合的混合界面（Hybrid）在现场活动目录搜索中的表现。主要发现：纯聊天界面效果最差，且跨多个AI模型稳健；混合界面与纯过滤界面无显著差异。揭示了两个机制：回声室效应（用户因无可见菜单而基于假设构建查询，导致搜索不可用事件类型且过度狭窄）和提示疲劳（精力转向提示而非评估输出，致使用户倾向选择首个建议）。结论建议AI作为过滤搜索的补充工具而非替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI工具正日益集成到在线搜索和浏览界面中，亟需了解这类工具如何影响用户行为和搜索结果。

Method: 开展一项预注册的激励在线实验，参与者从主要现场活动平台抓取的数千个事件目录中进行搜索。对比三种界面：经典基于属性的过滤界面（Filter）、生成式AI聊天界面（Chat）以及两者结合的混合界面（Hybrid）。AI输出均通过检索增强生成（RAG）进行限制。

Result: Chat条件在各项指标上表现最差，效果跨GPT-4.1-mini、GPT-5.2和GPT-5.4等模型稳健，主要由开放式探索型用户驱动。Filter与Hybrid之间无显著差异。发现两个机制：1) 回声室效应：无可见菜单导致用户基于对目录内容的假设构建查询，频繁搜索目录中不存在的事件类型，且搜索范围过窄。2) 提示疲劳：精力从检查评估输出转向提示编写，致使用户倾向选择首个显示的建议而非仔细浏览列表。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，AI最好作为现有基于过滤搜索的补充工具，而非替代品。

Abstract: Conversational AI tools are being increasingly integrated into online search and browsing interfaces. To understand how such tools affect user behavior and search outcomes, we run a pre-registered, incentivized online experiment in which participants search a catalog of several thousand events scraped from a major live-events platform. We compare three interfaces: a classic, attribute-based filter interface (Filter), a GenAI chat interface (Chat), and a combined interface offering both (Hybrid). In both AI conditions, AI output is restricted via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Our main result is that, relative to the other two conditions, the Chat condition performs the worst. The effect is robust across AI models (gpt-4.1-mini, gpt-5.2 and gpt-5.4) and is driven mainly by users who perform open-ended exploration (as opposed to users searching for a specific artist, venue or sports team). Furthermore, there are no significant differences on any metrics between Filter and Hybrid. We also document two mechanisms. First, Chat creates an echo chamber: without a visible menu, users construct queries based on what they think is contained in the catalog. As a result, they frequently search for event types that are not available in the catalog, and they search too narrowly. Second, Chat leads to prompting fatigue: it shifts effort towards prompting, and away from inspecting and evaluating the output. As a result, users often pick one of the first displayed suggestions rather than examining the list more carefully. Taken together, our results suggest that AI is best used as a complementary tool to existing filter-based search rather than as a replacement.

</details>


### [93] [Transaction Foundation Models for Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Financial Transaction Data: A Comparative Evaluation with Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28641)
*Snehal Pal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个基于IBM合成反洗钱交易数据集的1,026问QA基准，对比了NVIDIA的29M参数交易基础模型（TFM）与五个通用/金融领域文本嵌入模型及人工聚合摘要基线在检索增强生成（RAG）任务中的表现。TFM在检索召回率和端到端答案准确率上显著优于同规模通用嵌入器，但简单的“人工聚合摘要+通用句子编码器”管道在整体上匹配或超越TFM，且在低基数实体类别（银行、货币、支付格式）上占优。结论：交易原生基础模型的优势取决于实体类别，精心设计的特征聚合可在多个实际场景中替代领域特定预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域特定的表格和交易基础模型被作为金融应用的即插即用表征学习器提出，但其相对于现成的文本嵌入模型在结构化数据上的检索增强生成（RAG）价值尚未充分研究。论文旨在系统评估交易原生基础模型与通用文本嵌入模型在金融结构化数据RAG中的相对优劣，并探究特征聚合是否能替代领域预训练。

Method: 1. 基于IBM合成AML交易数据集，通过确定性模板生成1,026个带检索级金标文档标注的问答基准。2. 选取NVIDIA 29M参数TFM与五个规模和数据匹配的通用/金融文本嵌入模型，以及人工聚合摘要+通用句子编码器基线。3. 在统一的检索设置下比较Recall@5和端到端答案准确率，并按实体类别（如银行、货币、支付格式）细分分析。

Result: TFM在检索召回率上显著领先（Recall@5 0.81 vs 0.25-0.39），端到端答案准确率66.7% vs 27.5%。然而，人工聚合摘要+通用句子编码器管道在整体上匹配或超越TFM，并在低基数实体类别（银行、货币、支付格式）上占优。表明交易原生模型的优势呈类别依赖性，而非一致性。

Conclusion: 交易原生基础模型相对于通用文本嵌入的优势是类别依赖的，而非普遍的。在多个实际相关场景中，精心设计的特征聚合（如人工工程化摘要）结合通用句子编码器，可以替代领域特定的预训练。这提示在构建金融RAG系统时，应根据目标实体类别权衡模型选择与特征工程。

Abstract: Domain-specific tabular and transaction foundation models are increasingly proposed as drop-in representation learners for financial applications, but their value relative to off-the-shelf text embedding models for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over structured data remains underexamined. We construct a deterministic, template-generated question-answering benchmark of 1,026 questions with retrieval-level gold-document annotations over IBM's synthetic Anti-Money-Laundering (AML) transaction dataset, and compare NVIDIA's 29M-parameter Transaction Foundation Model (TFM) against five general-purpose and financial-domain text embedding models under a size- and data-matched retrieval setup, as well as against a hand-aggregated summary-text baseline. TFM substantially outperforms every size-matched generic embedder on both retrieval (Recall@5: 0.81 vs. 0.25-0.39) and end-to-end answer accuracy (66.7% vs. 27.5%). However, a simple pipeline that embeds a human-engineered aggregated summary with a generic sentence encoder matches or exceeds TFM overall, and dominates it on several low-cardinality entity categories (bank, currency, payment format). This suggests that the benefit of a transaction-native foundation model over generic text embeddings is category-dependent rather than uniform, and that careful feature aggregation can substitute for domain-specific pretraining in several practically relevant regimes. We release the benchmark generation and evaluation code.

</details>


### [94] [Controllable Generative Recommendation via Guided Token Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28642)
*Haichao Zhang,Zhixuan Liang,Chong Zhang,Zixi Chen,Wen Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReGen 提出并行迭代优化框架，将语义 ID 生成重构为掩码填充任务，实现无需重训练的实时可控推荐，并在准确性-多样性权衡与生成有效性上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归方法按固定左到右顺序生成离散语义 ID，早期决策锁定导致整体规划困难，直接干预易破坏语义结构生成非法项，难以满足实时可控性（如准确性与多样性平衡）需求。

Method: ReGen 将生成重构为并行掩码填充任务，同时预测所有 token，引入拓扑约束树在 beam search 中迭代修正低置信度预测并强制合法码本路径，支持无需重训练直接施加高层语义控制信号。

Result: 实验表明 ReGen 在中等引导强度下实现有利的准确性-多样性权衡，且保持生成语义 ID 的高有效性。

Conclusion: ReGen 通过并行迭代优化克服自回归局限，为生成式推荐提供实时可控、语义有效的新范式。

Abstract: Generative recommendation over discrete Semantic IDs has become a powerful paradigm for item retrieval. Real-world systems, however, frequently require real-time controllability to balance accuracy against objectives such as diversity. Existing autoregressive methods generate tokens strictly from left to right. This fixed direction locks in early decisions, preventing holistic planning and making control difficult. Consequently, direct interventions often break the semantic structure, producing non-existent items. To address this, we propose ReGen, a framework that shifts from sequential decoding to parallel iterative refinement. ReGen reformulates generation as a mask-filling task, predicting all tokens simultaneously rather than one by one. This global view allows us to apply control signals directly to tokens with high-level semantics without retraining the model. To ensure that decoded candidates map to real items, we introduce a topology-constrained tree that iteratively corrects low-confidence predictions while strictly enforcing valid codebook paths during beam-based inference. Experiments show that ReGen provides a favorable accuracy-diversity trade-off under moderate guidance while maintaining high validity of generated Semantic IDs. The source code is available at: https://github.com/zhang-haichao/ReGen.

</details>


### [95] [Constrained Tagging for Span Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28922)
*Radek Štulc,Jan Drchal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出约束标注，一种无需训练的方法，使生成式LLM能精确地在输入文本中标注跨度，解决了模型输出跨度可能不在输入中或出现多次的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式LLM虽能在无任务特定训练下标注跨度，但其输出未绑定输入位置：返回的跨度可能不在输入中或重复出现，导致定位不可靠。

Method: 约束标注让模型复现输入并用内联标记包裹每个跨度。采用基于字节级Token Trie和有限状态机的logits处理器，仅允许延续原文或形成合法标签的token，保证输出完全复现输入且标签来自给定集合，跨度可在任意字符边界开始/结束。两阶段解码让推理模型先推理再启用约束。

Result: 在6个数据集和6个开放权重模型上，255,312次约束生成中仅0.022%失败（均因推理模型耗尽token预算或未结束推理），约束无额外开销。对比基于上下文的提示丢失8.5%跨度，约束标注在所有数据集上定位其提出的每个跨度；在2个数据集上准确率相当，其余4个数据集的错误仅在于选择哪些跨度而非位置。

Conclusion: 约束标注以零训练成本、零推理开销实现了跨度级定位的强保证，将跨度抽取的错误源从“位置幻觉”收敛为“选择判断”，显著提升了生成式LLM在结构化标注任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: Generative LLMs can label spans without task-specific training, but their output is not tied to positions in the input: a returned span may be absent from the input or occur several times. We present constrained tagging, a training-free method in which the model reproduces the input and wraps each span in labeled inline markup. A logits processor using a byte-level token trie and a finite-state machine admits only tokens that continue the input verbatim or form a valid tag, so the output reproduces the input exactly with labels from the given set, and spans may begin or end at any character boundary, even inside a token. Two-phase decoding lets reasoning models reason before the constraint engages. On six datasets and six open-weight models, the guarantee holds in all but 0.022% of 255,312 constrained generations, each failure a reasoning model exhausting its token budget or never closing its reasoning, and the constraint adds no measurable cost. Unlike context-based prompting, which loses 8.5% of its spans, constrained tagging locates every span it proposes on every dataset; it is as accurate on two datasets, and on the other four its errors lie in which spans the model chooses, not where they are.

</details>


### [96] [Progressive Learning from Content to Relevance to Personalization for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29138)
*Zhiguang Yang,Yulin Yang,Fuyu Lv,Dan Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 FineSID，一种用于多模态点击率预测的渐进式学习框架，通过细粒度语义 ID 统一内容标记化、查询-项目语义预训练和个性化后训练，显著提升 CTR 预测和业务指标，并增强创意辨别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 CTR 预测方法难以有效融合细粒度异构内容与基于 ID 的预测器，需一种统一框架保留 token 级内容访问并兼顾语义匹配与个性化偏好。

Method: 三阶段渐进式学习：1) 使用预训练多尺度视觉分词器和文本分词器将异构内容转为细粒度语义 ID（SID），显式对齐 ID 特征；2) 在查询-项目相关性监督下全参数语义预训练，建立稳健匹配先验，联合优化多模态 SID 与常规字段交互；3) 直接用点击监督微调预测器捕捉隐式个性化偏好。

Result: 大量离线和在线实验显示，FineSID 在标准 CTR 预测和核心业务指标上均取得显著提升；且改善了同产品内创意辨别，展现出超越传统 ID 记忆的语义泛化能力。

Conclusion: FineSID 通过细粒度离散表示统一多模态内容建模、语义预训练与个性化微调，有效解决了异构内容融合难题，实现了语义泛化与个性化预测的双重提升，为工业级搜索推荐系统提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal click-through rate (CTR) prediction is central to search systems, where ranking depends on item content, query relevance, and user preference. Existing approaches incorporate content through dense embeddings or coarse discrete IDs, yet effectively integrating fine-grained heterogeneous content with ID-based predictors remains challenging. We propose FineSID, a progressive learning framework that unifies content tokenization, query--item semantic pretraining, and personalized post-training via a shared fine-grained discrete representation. Each stage optimizes an explicit objective while preserving token-level access to content. First, a pretrained multi-scale visual tokenizer and text tokenizers convert heterogeneous content into fine-grained Semantic IDs (SIDs), explicitly aligning them with the ID features. Second, full-parameter semantic pretraining under query--item relevance supervision establishes a robust matching prior, jointly optimizing the interactions of multimodal SIDs and conventional fields within a shared predictor. Third, this initialized predictor is directly fine-tuned with user click supervision to capture implicit personalized preferences. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate substantial gains in both standard CTR prediction and core business metrics. Furthermore, FineSID improves within-product creative discrimination, demonstrating its semantic generalization beyond conventional ID memorization.

</details>


### [97] [Reasoning-Search-Augmented LLMs: An Operational Taxonomy, Benchmark Analysis and Controlled Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29189)
*Biswas Poudel,Nilson Chapagain,Amit Kumar,Xianshun Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文综述了将检索作为迭代智能体循环中动作的推理-搜索增强大语言模型框架，从检索耦合、控制器设计、知识来源、落地严格性四大架构轴及学习信号等维度系统分析，并通过受控消融实验厘清设计选择对性能与成本的影响，旨在为该领域提供统一设计视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽展现涌现推理能力，但受限于固定参数与有限上下文窗口；即使结合静态检索，仍会产生无证据支持甚至与检索证据矛盾的陈述，导致幻觉、难以融入最新信息及无法正确协调多源证据。近期工作将检索视为迭代智能体循环中的动作，紧耦合逐步推理与交互式搜索，以解决上述局限。

Method: 从四大架构轴——检索耦合、控制器设计、知识来源、落地严格性——系统分析现有推理-搜索循环框架；将学习信号（提示、监督、偏好优化、强化学习）视为横向维度；区分基准难度与 RSA 循环需求；综合受控组内消融实验与质量-成本增量，避免跨异构模型主干、检索器、语料库及评测协议的无效池化。

Result: 梳理了推理-搜索增强 LLM 的全景图谱，揭示了不同架构轴与学习信号对搜索行为、停止策略及落地行为的实质性影响，并量化了各设计选择带来的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 通过统一的架构视角与受控实证分析，为未来推理-搜索增强 LLM 的设计选择提供了清晰的指导框架，推动该领域从碎片化探索走向系统化研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive emergent reasoning capabilities, yet they remain constrained by fixed model parameters and finite context windows. Even when augmented with static retrieval, they can produce statements that are unsupported by, or even contradictory to, retrieved evidence. This leads to hallucinations, difficulty incorporating up-to-date information, and failures to correctly interpret or reconcile sources. Recent work addresses these limitations by tightly coupling step-by-step reasoning with interactive search, treating retrieval as an action within an iterative agentic loop rather than a one-shot preprocessing step. This study reviews frameworks that implement such reasoning-search loops by interleaving planning, querying, retrieval, integration, verification, and stopping. We analyze these systems through four architectural axes: retrieval coupling, controller design, knowledge source, and grounding strictness. We separately treat the learning signal as a cross-cutting dimension because prompting, supervision, preference optimization, and reinforcement learning can instantiate the same architecture while producing materially different search, stopping, and grounding behavior. We further distinguish benchmark difficulty from RSA-loop demand and synthesize controlled within-study ablations and quality-cost deltas, avoiding invalid pooling across heterogeneous model backbones, retrievers, corpora, and evaluation protocols. Our goal is to clarify the landscape of reasoning-search-augmented LLMs and outline a unified perspective on the design choices shaping future research.

</details>


### [98] [Preference Is Not Permission: Personalizing the Auto-Purchase Line in Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29216)
*Ryotaro Shimizu,Sai HtaungKham,Mo Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出学习个体“自动购买界限”任务，即代理在无需用户确认下可自主购买的程度边界。评估16个指令微调模型显示普遍存在越权行为；基于个体少量标注的浅层学习器能以中位数0.79准确率预测个性化边界，显著优于所有提示词×模型配置，且边界不跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化购物基准仅关注商品与用户的匹配度，忽视了用户授权代理自主决策的边界条件。实际委托场景中，是否自动购买取决于证据充分性、可逆性、价格偏好、紧急度等多因素，且因人而异。需要研究如何学习个体的授权边界以对齐代理行为。

Method: 构建反事实基准，收集15位标注员基于用户画像扮演委托主体，对各场景标注三值授权决策（不买/确认后买/自动买）。评估16个指令微调模型在零样本/少样本提示下的表现。训练浅层学习器（如逻辑回归）利用个体少量历史标签预测其保留数据上的授权边界，对比多种提示词×模型配置及逐标注员预言机基线。

Result: 所有评估模型均表现出显著越权：即使匹配度低或自身证据标志显示依据不足也自动购买。标注员间存在分歧但分歧有结构且可部分预测。个体化浅层学习器在30-40个标签后达中位数0.79准确率，超越所有80种提示词×模型配置及逐标注员预言机。学习到的边界不跨域迁移。

Conclusion: 代理自主决策边界高度个性化，通用模型难以零样本对齐；利用个体少量偏好数据训练轻量级预测器是有效路径。发布的基准、人工授权参考及标注协议将推动个性化代理对齐研究。

Abstract: Personalized shopping benchmarks ask which item fits the user; we ask how far an agent may commit on that item, and pose learning the individual's auto-purchase line as a task. The boundary is evidence-conditioned and three-valued: whether committing is appropriate depends on the sufficiency of product information (warrant), reversibility, price against the user's norm, and urgency, not only on fit; and the natural output is don't buy, confirm first, or buy automatically. Across sixteen instruction-tuned models, autonomous commitment is misaligned with a human authorization reference in which fifteen annotators, role-playing the delegating principal from a given profile, choose the furthest action they would allow without confirmation: models commit even when fit is low and when their own evidence flag reports the warrant missing. This is overreach. The line is individual: these raters disagree, yet the disagreement is structured and partly predictable from a person's own other labels. A shallow learner on a few dozen of a person's own labels predicts their held-out auto-purchase line at a median of 0.79 by 30-40 labels, outperforming every evaluated prompt$\times$model configuration, including a per-annotator oracle over 80 configurations; the learned boundary does not transfer across domains. We release the counterfactual benchmark, human authorization reference, and annotation protocol after acceptance.

</details>


### [99] [Learning Marginal Viewpoint Utility for Budgeted Evidence Selection in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29234)
*Yiyang Duan,Ziqi Yu,Tiantong Wu,Yebo Wu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LMVU 是一种轻量级排序器，用于多视角查询的检索增强生成，通过学习边际视角覆盖来优先选择候选文档，在固定预算下提升视角覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多视角查询的检索增强生成中，需要在固定上下文预算下保留来自检索证据的不同答案和立场，现有方法难以有效优先选择能增加新视角覆盖的候选文档。

Method: 提出 LMVU，一种轻量级排序器，利用开发集的视角标注训练，学习在相同选择状态下比较候选文档的边际视角覆盖增益；推理时无需标注，随上下文增长反复对预算可行的候选文档打分。

Result: 在四个视角标注基准上，1024 token 预算下，LMVU 在 RVC1 和 RVC2 指标上分别比最强非 oracle 基线最高提升 0.047 和 0.052，并在两个基准上达到精确视角广度上限。

Conclusion: LMVU 能有效提升多视角检索增强生成的视角覆盖度，在固定预算下接近甚至达到理论上限，证明了基于边际视角覆盖排序的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over multi-viewpoint queries must preserve distinct answers and positions from the retrieved evidence while operating under a fixed context budget. We introduce LMVU, a lightweight ranker that learns to prioritize candidates by the marginal viewpoint coverage they add to the current selection. Training uses viewpoint annotations from each benchmark's development split to compare candidates within the same selection state. At inference, LMVU repeatedly scores budget-feasible candidates as the context grows, without accessing these annotations. We evaluate two forms of recovered viewpoint coverage: RVC$_1$ assigns each viewpoint full credit for at least one supporting source unit, while RVC$_2$ assigns half credit for one and full credit for two or more distinct source units. Exact oracles measure achievable coverage under the same evidence pool and budget. Across four viewpoint-annotated benchmarks at a 1,024-token budget, LMVU achieves gains of up to 0.047 and 0.052 points in RVC$_1$ and RVC$_2$, respectively, over the strongest non-oracle baseline for each benchmark and metric, and reaches the exact viewpoint-breadth ceiling on two benchmarks.

</details>


### [100] [Generative Recommenders Forget the Long Tail: Restoring Memorization in Retrieval and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29298)
*Xu Huang,Jiancan Wu,Hongzheng Qin,Jiawei Feng,Can Ye,Wencong Zeng,Fengze Li,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文指出生成式推荐模型在检索和排序两个阶段都会遗忘长尾物品，提出无需训练的CAPE插件，利用共现证据在检索阶段召回长尾物品并在排序阶段融合生成概率与共现证据，显著提升长尾准确率且保持整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型用单一模型替代传统召回-排序流水线，但共享的生成概率导致长尾物品被双重遗忘：检索时束搜索按子树概率质量保留前缀，导致长尾物品早期被剪枝；排序时似然概率将长尾物品排在头部物品之下。这反映了缺乏物品级记忆（即直接回忆共现物品），而协同过滤具备这种能力。

Method: 提出CAPE（Co-occurrence-Aware Pool Expansion），免训练插件：(i) 记忆通道利用共现证据在束搜索外部召回长尾物品，再用冻结生成器打分；(ii) 记忆校准排序将生成器似然与共现证据融合，同时保留生成器的首位推荐。

Result: 在两个生成式骨干模型和产品、音乐两个领域实验中，CAPE显著提升长尾准确率，优于共享候选集的重排基线，保持或提升整体准确率，仅增加少量延迟。

Conclusion: 生成式推荐的长尾遗忘源于缺乏物品级共现记忆，CAPE通过外部记忆通道和校准排序在不重新训练的情况下有效缓解该问题，为生成式推荐的长尾增强提供了轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Generative recommenders replace the retrieve-then-rank cascade with a single model: beam search over semantic item identifiers performs retrieval, and the same likelihood performs ranking. We show that this shared score makes them forget long-tail items twice. In retrieval, beam search keeps identifier prefixes by the probability mass of their subtrees, so tail items are pruned early, and a wider beam admits few more of them. In ranking, the likelihood places tail items below head items even when they are admitted, so expanding retrieval alone brings little or no gain. Both failures reflect missing item-level memorization (i.e., direct recall of which items co-occur), which collaborative filtering provides and shared-prefix generation lacks. To this end, we propose CAPE (Co-occurrence-Aware Pool Expansion), a training-free plug-in that restores memorization at both stages: (i) a memorization channel retrieves co-occurrence-supported tail items outside the beam and scores them with the frozen generator, and (ii) memorization-calibrated ranking fuses the generator likelihood with co-occurrence evidence while keeping the generator's first item. Experiments with two generative backbones on product and music domains show that CAPE significantly improves long-tail accuracy over the backbones and over re-ranking baselines that share its candidates, preserves or improves overall accuracy, and adds a small fraction of the latency.

</details>


### [101] [Directional Posterior Feedback Fusion for Query Reformulation in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29464)
*Yiteng Tu,Qingyi Pan,Weihang Su,Jiaxin Mao,Yiqun Liu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DIFF (Directional Posterior Feedback Fusion)，一种将反馈建模为方向性证据而非目标点的查询重构框架，在不重训练稠密检索器的前提下实现了最优的整体检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索严重依赖查询嵌入捕捉用户信息需求，但短、歧义或欠指定查询难以得到充分表达。现有基于嵌入空间的伪相关反馈方法通过聚合或插值反馈向量，隐式地将其视为完整的目标表示，这种逐点融合会抑制单个反馈项未显式表达但由互补证据支持的查询修正方向。

Method: DIFF 在原始查询的切空间中将反馈表示为置信度加权的方向性证据，采用先验正则化的后验融合机制联合推断可控的查询更新，无需重训练底层稠密检索器。

Result: 跨多个稠密检索器和不同反馈源的实验表明，DIFF 实现了最佳的整体检索性能，验证了显式建模反馈如何约束查询更新的有效性。

Conclusion: 将反馈显式建模为方向性约束而非目标点，能够更有效地利用互补证据进行查询重构，显著提升稠密检索的性能。

Abstract: Dense retrieval has become a fundamental paradigm for modern information retrieval, supporting efficient semantic search across a wide range of applications. Its effectiveness, however, relies critically on query embeddings that adequately capture users' information needs, which can be difficult for short, ambiguous, or underspecified queries. Feedback-based query reformulation approaches like pseudo-relevance feedback (PRF) alleviate this by incorporating additional evidence from retrieved or generated content. However, existing embedding-space approaches commonly aggregate or interpolate feedback vectors, implicitly treating them as complete target representations. Such pointwise fusion can unnecessarily suppress query corrections along directions not expressed by individual feedback items, even when these directions are supported by complementary evidence. To address this limitation, we propose Directional Posterior Feedback Fusion (DIFF), a query reformulation framework that models feedback as directional evidence rather than target points. DIFF represents feedback as confidence-weighted directional evidence in the tangent space of the original query and jointly infers a controlled query update through prior-regularized posterior fusion, without retraining the underlying dense retriever. Experiments across multiple dense retrievers and different feedback sources show that DIFF achieves the best overall retrieval performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of explicitly modeling how feedback should constrain query updates.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond the Vote: When Do LLMs Add Value to Retrieval-Based Tabular Classification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29520)
*Ziquan Wang,Yuxuan Nie,Jian Ma,Yingjie Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对比了四种通用大语言模型与距离加权最近邻投票在表格分类任务中的检索式上下文学习表现，发现Distance-KNN在所有检索池大小下均达到最高平均准确率，LLM仅在低标签共识且近邻域情况下偶尔优于Distance-KNN，整体上LLM推理未能持续超越简单的加权投票基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索式上下文学习使LLM能从相似示例预测表格标签，但尚不清楚LLM推理是否真正优于对相同示例的直接投票，需要系统性对比明确LLM在何种条件下提供额外预测价值。

Method: 在19个表格分类数据集上，固定演示数量，使用相同检索邻居，对比四种通用LLM与距离加权KNN；分析检索候选池大小、标签共识度、特征空间距离、演示数量、提示构式、检索表示及标签平衡选择对性能的影响。

Result: 扩大检索池提升所有预测器准确率，但Distance-KNN在每个池大小下均达最高平均准确率；LLM增益集中于低标签共识区域且因模型而异；在低共识下，部分LLM在近邻和中邻域优于Distance-KNN，但在最远邻域全线落后；多种补充实验基本保持该结论。

Conclusion: LLM推理在检索式表格ICL中仅在特定条件（低标签共识、近邻域）提供额外预测价值，总体上距离加权最近邻投票是更强且更稳健的基线，LLM并未持续超越简单加权投票。

Abstract: Retrieval-based in-context learning (ICL) enables large language models (LLMs) to predict tabular labels from similar examples, but it remains unclear whether LLM inference improves on direct voting over those same examples. We address this question by comparing four general-purpose LLMs with distance-weighted nearest-neighbor voting (Distance-KNN) using identical retrieved neighbors across 19 tabular classification datasets. With the number of demonstrations fixed, enlarging the retrieval candidate pool improves accuracy for all predictors, yet Distance-KNN achieves the highest mean accuracy at every pool size. More detailed analyses reveal that LLM gains are concentrated where retrieved neighbors exhibit low label consensus and vary across models. These gains further depend on the average feature-space distance to the test query: under low label consensus, some LLMs outperform Distance-KNN in close and intermediate neighborhoods, but all fall behind on average in the most distant group. Complementary experiments on the number of demonstrations, prompt formulation, retrieval representation, and label-balanced demonstration selection largely preserve the overall findings. These results characterize when LLM inference adds predictive value beyond distance-weighted voting in retrieval-based tabular ICL.

</details>


### [103] [X-Embedding: Two-Stage Training for Multilingual Patent Prior-Art Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29521)
*Wentao Wu,Xu Wang,Linqing Chen,Fenhong Zhu,Hanmeng Zhong,Yuanxiang Huangfu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出X-Embedding，一种两阶段框架，用于多语言专利先行技术检索中的X类引文检索。第一阶段利用专利结构和多语言表达学习专利领域表示；第二阶段利用审查报告中的X类引文作为正样本，优化X类检索相关性。采用Matryoshka表示学习支持64和1024维嵌入。在中英日三语言集合上实验，64维下整体宏观Hit@100达89.09%，显著优于基线；两阶段训练优于直接监督微调，验证了顺序领域和任务自适应的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言专利先行技术检索需要识别与发明新颖性或创造性步骤特别相关的X类引文，这超越了跨语言和文档视图的主题相似性。现有方法难以同时捕捉专利领域知识、多语言对齐和X类相关性判断，因此需要一种能够顺序进行专利领域适应和任务特定优化的框架。

Method: X-Embedding两阶段框架：第一阶段通过对齐专利内部互补章节及跨语言专利家族成员对应章节，学习专利领域表示，利用专利结构和多语言表达；第二阶段以审查报告中的X类引文为正样本，从检索候选和A类引文中采样负样本，学习X类相关性。采用Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）在一个模型中同时支持64维和1024维嵌入。

Result: 在中英日三语言集合、四种查询视图上评估：64维下整体宏观Hit@100达89.09%，比Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B高21.25个百分点，比RoBERTa高3.68个百分点。相同二阶段训练和评估条件下，两阶段训练比直接监督微调高3.19分，英语和日语分别提升5.34和4.72分，12个语言-视图组合中9个得分更高。宏观Hit@10和Hit@20分别提升3.71和3.79分。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持顺序进行专利领域适应和任务适应，能够有效提升多语言专利集合上的聚合X类检索性能。两阶段训练优于端到端微调，且低维嵌入仍保持高检索质量，验证了框架的有效性和效率。

Abstract: Multilingual patent prior-art retrieval requires identifying X-category references that are particularly relevant to the novelty or inventive step of a claimed invention, beyond topical similarity across languages and document views. We present X-Embedding, a two-stage framework that first learns patent-domain representations and then optimizes X-category retrieval relevance. The first stage aligns complementary sections within patents and corresponding sections across different-language patent-family members, learning from both patent structure and multilingual expressions. The second stage learns X-category relevance using examination-report X citations as positives and negatives drawn from retrieval candidates and A-category citations. Matryoshka representation learning (MRL) enables 64- and 1024-dimensional embeddings within one model. Evaluation on a common Chinese, English, and Japanese collection with four query views yields 89.09% overall macro Hit@100 at 64 dimensions, exceeding Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B and RoBERTa by 21.25 and 3.68 percentage points, respectively. Under the same second-stage training and evaluation conditions, two-stage training outperforms direct supervised fine-tuning by 3.19 points, with gains of 5.34 and 4.72 points for English and Japanese and higher scores in nine of twelve language-view combinations. Overall macro Hit@10 and Hit@20 also improve by 3.71 and 3.79 points, respectively. These results support sequential patent-domain and task adaptation for improving aggregate X-category retrieval on this multilingual collection.

</details>


### [104] [pplx-embed-v2-late Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29523)
*Markus Krimmel,Louis Milliken,Ekaterina Glazkova,Maximilian Schall,Antoine Chaffin,Sedigheh Eslami,Jonas Golde,Denis Bykov,Claire Beamer,Jeremy Yang,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两个多语言多模态的晚期交互嵌入模型 pplx-embed-v2-late（0.6B 和 9B 参数），它们在各自参数量级别上在文本、图像和视觉文档检索基准上达到最先进水平，并支持非对称部署（大模型索引、小模型查询）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发高效的多语言多模态检索模型，既能在文本、图像和视觉文档等多模态任务上表现优异，又能通过非对称部署平衡检索性能与查询延迟，满足实际应用中对低延迟和高精度的双重需求。

Method: 采用表示蒸馏方法，从一个 18B 参数的教师模型蒸馏训练得到两个学生模型（0.6B 和 9B）。两个模型均为晚期交互架构（类似 ColBERT），共享同一嵌入空间，从而实现文档用 9B 模型编码索引、查询用 0.6B 模型编码的非对称部署。

Result: 两个模型在各自参数规模下均在文本、图像和视觉文档检索基准上达到 SOTA。其中 0.6B 模型在视觉文档检索任务上表现可与数倍大小的模型媲美。模型已在 Hugging Face 开源。

Conclusion: pplx-embed-v2-late 系列通过知识蒸馏实现了高效的多语言多模态晚期交互检索，支持非对称部署策略，为实际检索系统提供了兼顾精度与延迟的实用解决方案。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-late, a pair of 0.6B and 9B multilingual and multimodal late-interaction models. Both models achieve state-of-the-art results within their respective size classes across a range of text, image, and visual document retrieval benchmarks. On visual document retrieval tasks, pplx-embed-v2-late-0.6b is competitive with models several times larger than itself. The models are trained via representation distillation from an 18B teacher. Consequently, they share an embedding space that supports asymmetric deployment: documents can be indexed with the 9B model and queried with the low-latency 0.6B model. Both models are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/perplexity-ai/pplx-embed-v2.

</details>


### [105] [ERCLIP: Small-Data Adaptation of CLIP for Free-Length Image-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29939)
*Hoang-Bao Le,Allie Tran,Binh T. Nguyen,Liting Zhou,Cathal Gurrin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ERCLIP 是一种小数据适应 CLIP 的方法，用于长文本图文检索。通过将长文本拆分为句子独立编码，融合句子嵌入并使用对比损失训练，在推理时保持 CLIP 双编码器不变，并引入冻结 CLIP 保持损失以缓解零样本分类性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 的文本编码器限制为 77 个 token，会截断详细的图像描述，限制了长文本图文检索性能。现有方法如 LongCLIP 需要大量长文本数据，而 ERCLIP 旨在仅用少量数据（20k 对）适应 CLIP 以处理长文本，且不修改推理架构。

Method: 训练时将长文本拆分为句子，每个句子由 CLIP 文本编码器独立编码；句子嵌入通过平均池化或门控残差 Transformer 评分器融合，并施加对比损失。推理时使用未修改的 CLIP 双编码器（仍限 77 token）。为防止微调损害零样本分类，在图像和短文本嵌入上添加冻结 CLIP 保持损失。

Result: 使用 20k 数据对训练一个 epoch，ERCLIP 在长文本 R@1 上比 CLIP 提升 6.5-11.2 点，以 LongCLIP 2% 的长文本数据缩小了 62-77% 的差距。保持损失恢复了 1.5-2.1 个 ImageNet 零样本分类点（弥补 30-42% 的损失），并使 COCO 图文检索 R@1 增加高达 2.4 点，且无推理开销。

Conclusion: ERCLIP 以极小的数据和训练成本有效将 CLIP 适应长文本检索，通过保持损失在不牺牲零样本能力的情况下提升长文本性能，实现了无推理额外开销的高效适应。

Abstract: CLIP's 77-token text encoder truncates detailed image descriptions. We present ERCLIP, a small-data adaptation of CLIP for long-caption image--text retrieval. During training, a long caption is split into sentences that the CLIP text encoder encodes independently; the sentence embeddings are fused by mean pooling or a gated residual Transformer scorer and receive their own contrastive loss. At inference ERCLIP is an unmodified CLIP dual encoder: test captions are still read through the 77-token window, and only training sees the full caption. We find that this fine-tuning erodes CLIP's zero-shot classification, most when the fusion output is not a combination of CLIP embeddings, and add a frozen-CLIP preservation loss on the image and short-caption embeddings. With 20k pairs and one epoch, ERCLIP improves long-caption R@1 over CLIP by 6.5--11.2 points, closing 62--77% of the gap to LongCLIP with 2% of its long captions. Preservation recovers 1.5--2.1 ImageNet zero-shot points (30--42% of the loss) and adds up to 2.4 points of COCO image-to-text R@1, at no inference cost.

</details>


### [106] [Think Harder Where It Matters: Item-Level Adaptive Recursion for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30128)
*Zhanshuo Cao,Jiakai Tang,Yuqi Zhou,Sunhao Dai,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LoopRec 提出一种自适应推理框架，通过轻量门控在每个递归边界独立决定每个项目是提前退出还是继续深度计算，将固定全局深度转化为可学习的逐项属性，在四类主流骨干网络上持续超越固定深度基线，且学习到的计算分配与低频、近期交互需更深推理的直觉一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型对用户历史中所有项目分配统一计算预算，忽视了不同项目对最终预测的贡献差异。诊断实验发现：(1) 同一序列内项目计算需求非均匀，多数浅层收敛，少数关键项目需完整深度；(2) 固定分配次优，给定预言机访问目标项目时，非均匀深度分配始终优于固定深度，说明统一计算既浪费已收敛项目的容量，又牺牲整体推荐质量。
