We validate the framework on the public credit-card fraud benchmark (284,807 transactions) with five constructed candidate assets spanning the redundancy spectrum. Only the genuinely differentiated asset passes the significance gate ($Δ$AUC = +0.0029, DeLong $p = 1.4\times 10^{-9}$, Holm-corrected $p = 7.2\times 10^{-9}$) and receives an acquire recommendation (DDS = +0.663); the fully redundant asset scores lowest (DDS = -0.135). The recommendation is stable across 19 of 22 admissible strategic-weight configurations and across all decision thresholds in [0.15, 0.66]. We state clearly which quantities in the case study are computed from data and which are simulated governance inputs, and we discuss the design limits of a single-dataset evaluation.

</details>


### [139] [Auditing Conformal Prediction for Language Models Under Inference-Time Model Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28937)
*Achref Doula,Max Mühlhäuser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出了语言模型中一致性预测(CP)评估协议的局限性：当校准和部署使用不同的推理配置(如量化模式、推理温度)时，会导致推理时模型偏移，破坏分割一致性预测的可交换性假设，导致匹配评估高估部署时的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CP评估通常采用匹配协议，即校准和部署使用相同的推理配置。但在实际部署中，为满足内存、吞吐量或服务策略约束，推理配置往往不同。作者认为这暴露了当前CP评估协议的根本局限，需要形式化并量化这种推理时模型偏移的影响。

Method: 1. 将现象形式化为推理时模型偏移：由推理配置而非输入-标签分布导致的一致性分数分布变化。2. 理论表征其如何在任务分布和训练模型不变的情况下破坏分割一致性预测的可交换性。3. 实证评估：跨越8个语言模型、4个有限标签基准、12种推理配置进行系统实验。

Result: 发现匹配评估会显著高估部署时的覆盖率，且这种效应在不同模型和数据集间表现出异质性。实验证实了推理配置变化导致的一致性分数分布偏移会实质性破坏覆盖率保证。

Conclusion: 推理配置应作为CP评估中的一等实验轴报告。当前的匹配评估协议不足以反映真实部署性能，需要建立考虑推理配置差异的评估标准。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) for language models is typically evaluated under a matched protocol, in which calibration and deployment use the same post-training inference configuration, such as quantization mode and inference temperature. In deployment, these choices often differ from those used at calibration to meet memory or throughput constraints or serving policies. We argue that this exposes a limitation in current CP evaluation protocols for language models. We formalize the underlying phenomenon as inference-time model shift: a change in the conformal score distribution induced by inference configuration rather than by the input--label distribution, and theoretically characterize how it breaks split-conformal exchangeability even when the task distribution and trained model are unchanged. We then evaluate its consequences across 8 language models, 4 finite-label benchmarks, and 12 inference configurations. We find that matched evaluation can substantially overstate deployment-time coverage, and that the effect is heterogeneous across models and datasets. We argue that inference configuration should be reported as a first-class experimental axis in CP evaluations for language-model systems.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Objective Protein Optimization via Modular Diffusion Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28943)
*Meir Roketlishvili,Felix Huber,Wenda Chu,Yisong Yue,Raul Astudillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MODS（模块化多目标蛋白优化框架），通过推理时扩散引导实现多目标蛋白优化，结合增广切比雪夫标量化权重和适应度代理集成，在六个蛋白优化任务上达到竞争性超体积指标，并在多个任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白工程常需同时改进多个性质，需寻找代表有用适应度权衡的高性能序列。设计空间组合爆炸大，实验轮次有限，现有方法难以高效探索多目标权衡。

Method: MODS采用模块化框架：为每个提议采样增广切比雪夫标量化权重和适应度代理集成成员，定义标量引导奖励；权重变化引导不同目标权衡，集成成员变化多样化预测；支持任意扩散模型和兼容引导过程；每批评估后仅更新代理模型，无需重新训练生成模型。

Result: 在六个蛋白优化任务（使用基于实验数据训练的预测神谕）上，配合两种扩散模型和两种引导过程，MODS在所有任务上均达到竞争性最终超体积指标，并在多个任务上显著超越最佳基线。

Conclusion: MODS展示了一种灵活有效的多目标蛋白优化方法，能在有限反馈下高效探索序列空间，兼容多种扩散模型和引导策略，具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Protein engineering often requires improving several properties simultaneously, motivating the search for high-performing sequences that represent useful fitness tradeoffs. Discovering these sequences is challenging because the design space is combinatorially large and experimental campaigns often permit only a few rounds of batched measurements. We introduce \textit{MODS}, a modular framework for multi-objective protein optimization via inference-time diffusion steering. For each proposal, MODS samples augmented Chebyshev scalarization weights and a fitness-surrogate ensemble member to define a scalar steering reward. Varying the weights directs generation toward different objective tradeoffs, while varying ensemble members diversifies the predictions used for guidance. Because MODS expresses multi-objective acquisition through scalar rewards, it can be paired with different diffusion models and compatible steering procedures. After each evaluation batch, MODS updates the surrogates without retraining the generative model. We evaluate MODS on six protein optimization tasks using predictive oracles trained on experimental assay data, with two diffusion models and two steering procedures. MODS achieves competitive final hypervolume across all six tasks with both diffusion models, and substantially outperforms the best evaluated baseline on several tasks. Our results demonstrate a flexible and effective approach to multi-objective protein optimization with limited feedback.

</details>


### [141] [Is 2:4 Sparsity Still Worth It? Semi-Structured Sparsity versus Quantization in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28947)
*Xiuyi Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究 NVIDIA Tensor Core 的 2:4 半结构化稀疏与量化结合时的权衡：在同等比特数或同等 Tensor Core 档位下，对比“先加 2:4 稀疏”还是“先降精度”。实验发现：只要硬件支持更低精度，直接降精度优于加 2:4 稀疏（更准、更快）；只有量化到硬件最低精度后，2:4 才能进一步加速线性层（1.3–1.7×）。在 H100 上用 vLLM 服务 FP8+2:4 模型，预填吞吐提升 27%，GSM8K 准确率持平，但需重训恢复质量——困惑度恢复快、推理能力恢复慢；Blackwell 的 4:8 稀疏 FP4 损失更大，重训预算未恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ampere 以来 Tensor Core 原生支持 2:4 稀疏且可与量化组合，实际部署面临决策：给定精度下，是引入 2:4 稀疏还是直接降精度？需在公平条件下（同比特数含稀疏元数据、同 Tensor Core 档位）全面评估两者在 Transformer 所有 GEMM 算子上的准确性与性能，指导工程选择。

Method: 在三代 GPU（Ampere、Hopper、Blackwell）上测量内核性能，覆盖 Transformer 层全部六个 GEMM 操作数；对比同比特数（含稀疏索引开销）与同名义 Tensor Core 档位两种公平设定；引入在线剪枝与量化流程，评估稠密/稀疏、不同精度（FP16/FP8/FP4）组合的准确率（困惑度、GSM8K）、吞吐与延迟；并在 H100 上用 vLLM 部署重训后的 FP8+2:4 模型做端到端测试。

Result: 1) 只要存在更低精度，直接降精度优于加 2:4：量化误差随精度下降而缩减，而 2:4 在硬件强制轴上丢弃的能量不随位宽缩小。2) 量化至硬件最低精度后，2:4 仍能加速线性层 1.3–1.7×。3) H100 上 FP8+2:4 经重训后，vLLM 预填吞吐 +27%，GSM8K 持平；困惑度恢复快，推理能力恢复慢。4) Blackwell 采用成对 4:8 稀疏 FP4，精度损失更大，给定重训预算未恢复。

Conclusion: 工程上应优先降精度而非加 2:4 稀疏；仅当精度已达硬件底限时，2:4 才能提供额外加速且需投入重训恢复质量。Blackwell 的 4:8 稀疏模式对精度冲击更大，现有重训预算难以弥补，提示未来需更强的稀疏感知训练方法。

Abstract: NVIDIA tensor cores have supported 2:4 semi-structured sparsity since Ampere, and the pattern composes with quantization. When the two are combined, the practical question is which step to take next: from a given precision, add 2:4 sparsity or drop to the next lower precision? We compare the two at equal bits, with sparsity metadata counted, and at equal nominal tensor-core tiers, over all six GEMM operands of a transformer layer, with kernels measured on three GPU generations and online pruning and quantization included. Our results show that as long as a lower precision is available, adding 2:4 is the worse step, both less accurate and slower, because the energy that 2:4 discards from each group of four along the hardware-forced axis does not shrink with bits, while quantization error does. Once quantization reaches the lowest precision the hardware supports, 2:4 still speeds up linear layers by 1.3--1.7\x{}, and on an H100 a retrained 2:4 model served in FP8 by vLLM reaches 27\% higher prefill throughput than the dense FP8 model, with GSM8K accuracy on par. Its quality has to be won back by retraining, however, which restores perplexity long before reasoning; on Blackwell, whose sparse FP4 uses a pairwise 4:8 pattern, the loss is larger and our retraining budget did not recover it.

</details>


### [142] [Online Open-World Wafer Defect Discovery using Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28949)
*Quanling Zhao,Ava Emami,Tsuyoshi Idé,Rajiv Joshi,Steven Holmes,Michael Passow,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WaferHDC是一个基于超维计算(HDC)的在线开放世界晶圆缺陷发现框架,解决传统离线闭集分类器无法检测新型缺陷的问题,通过内存中心架构实现快速异常检测和缺陷模式动态分组,在WM-811K数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶圆缺陷分析依赖离线闭集分类器,无法检测连续半导体制造过程中出现的新型未见缺陷特征,需要一种能够在线、开放世界地发现和分组新缺陷模式的方法。

Method: WaferHDC基于超维计算(HDC)的内存中心架构,首先建立正常空间分布模型,然后将反复出现的故障动态分组为持久缺陷内存,使用紧凑超向量表示,无需标签或预定义类别数量,内存空间可随新缺陷模式动态扩展。

Result: 在工业标准WM-811K数据集上,WaferHDC在调整兰德指数上比最强在线基线高13.3个百分点,在标准化互信息上高10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: WaferHDC证明了基于超维计算的在线开放世界框架对于连续晶圆缺陷分析的有效性,能够无需重新训练静态分类器或归档原始晶圆历史即可动态适应新缺陷模式。

Abstract: Conventional wafer defect analysis relies on offline, closed-world classifiers that fail to detect novel, unseen defect signatures emerging during continuous semiconductor manufacturing. To address this, we propose WaferHDC, an online open-world wafer defect discovery framework based on hyperdimensional computing (HDC). The memory-centric architecture of WaferHDC enables rapid anomaly detection and grouping of recurring failures into compact hypervector representations. This allows the memory space to dynamically scale with new defect modes without retraining a static classifier or archiving entire raw wafer history. WaferHDC first models normal spatial distributions, then dynamically groups recurring failures into persistent defect memory without labels or predefined class counts. It outperforms the strongest online baseline by 13.3 p.p. in adjusted Rand index and 10.5 p.p. in normalized mutual information on the industry-standard WM-811K dataset. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of WaferHDC for continuous and open-world wafer defect analysis.

</details>


### [143] [Transformers as Generic Deep Neural Networks: Exact Compilation, the Cost of Removing Activation-Activation Products, and Multi-Agent Attention Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28958)
*Sergey Salishev,Zeev Volkovich,Oleg Granichin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文展示了Transformer模块可以精确编译为通用深度神经网络，仅使用固定仿射映射、路由、平方、Sigmoid、逆平方根和GELU（或其Sigmoid近似），避免了激活-激活乘积，从而消除了每头至少N^2标量非线性评估的开销。对于Top-k注意力，证明了精确或条件均值尾部质量给出无偏、最小均方误差的注意力分布估计，而尾部丢弃有条件偏差。当查询或智能体聚类时，质心优先选择键，将计算得分从N^2降至Nk。实验表明编译模型在DeiT-Tiny、MambaIRv2-Light和Pythia-70M上与参考模型匹配，标准训练后量化适用，且精确尾部质量估计在DeiT-Tiny上仅用一半键即优于尾部丢弃，在Pythia-70M上当k≤8时亦优于尾部丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer部署依赖专用NPU算子（注意力、归一化）和Transformer特定量化算法，作者旨在证明计算本身不强制这种专用化，而是可以将Transformer编译为通用DNN算子，从而简化硬件需求并降低计算复杂度。

Method: 1. 将每个Transformer块（注意力、LayerNorm、GELU MLP）精确编译为由固定仿射映射、路由、平方、Sigmoid、逆平方根和GELU（或其Sigmoid近似）组成的通用DNN。2. 避免激活-激活乘积，利用元素级运算（普遍存在于NPU）消除每头N^2标量非线性评估。3. 对于Top-k注意力，证明交换尾部得分下精确或条件均值尾部质量给出无偏、最小均方误差估计，尾部丢弃有条件偏差。4. 利用查询/智能体聚类，质心优先选择键，将得分计算从N^2降至Nk。

Result: 在DeiT-Tiny、MambaIRv2-Light和Pythia-70M上，编译模型使用所有键时与参考模型匹配，标准训练后量化直接适用。精确尾部质量估计在DeiT-Tiny上仅用一半键即优于尾部丢弃，在Pythia-70M上当k≤8时亦优于尾部丢弃。

Conclusion: Transformer无需专用硬件算子即可高效部署，通用DNN算子集足以精确实现。Top-k注意力的尾部质量估计提供理论最优无偏估计，聚类进一步降低计算复杂度。此工作为Transformer在通用硬件上的高效部署提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Transformers are deployed as a special model class. Neural processing units (NPU) add dedicated operators for attention and normalization, and quantization relies on Transformer-specific algorithms. We show the computation does not force this. Every Transformer block, with attention, LayerNorm and a GELU MLP, compiles exactly into a generic deep neural network built from fixed affine maps, routing and the square, sigmoid and inverse square root, plus GELU or its sigmoid approximation. Without activation-activation products, every transform-wise compilation adds at least $N^2$ scalar nonlinear evaluations per head for dense scores. Keeping that one operation type removes this cost; its elementwise form, present on every surveyed NPU, suffices with the sigmoid and inverse square root. For top-$k$ attention with exchangeable tail scores, we prove that exact or conditional-mean tail mass gives an unbiased, minimum-mean-square-error estimate of the attention distribution, while tail drop is conditionally biased. When queries or agents cluster, centroids choose the keys first, reducing the number of computed scores from $N^2$ to $Nk$ per head. On DeiT-Tiny, MambaIRv2-Light and Pythia-70M the compiled models match their references with all keys, standard post-training quantization applies, and the exact tail-mass estimate beats tail dropping with half as many keys on DeiT-Tiny and, for $k\le8$, on Pythia-70M.

</details>


### [144] [Unit-Aware Differentially Private Quantum Machine Learning for Clinical and Wearable Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28968)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨在差分隐私（DP）约束下，量子机器学习（QML）模型是否比经典模型更能保持效用。实验在三个临床队列和一个可穿戴设备队列上进行，结果显示量子模型在三个临床队列上平均 ROC AUC 领先达 0.104，但在可穿戴队列落后。经 Holm 校正后无显著差异，表明仅为趋势而非量子优势。解析梯度界显示降低裁剪阈值可减少噪声提升效用。在 IBM 156 量子位处理器上实测推理误差分别为 0.046（私有）和 0.063（非私有），验证了 NISQ 硬件可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床注册表和可穿戴传感器数据用于早期健康风险评估，但模型发布可能泄露敏感信息。差分隐私通过注入噪声限制泄露，但噪声总量随可训练参数数量增长，对小规模健康队列尤为严重。这促使采用参数高效的量子机器学习模型，其变分电路能用极少参数表达复杂决策边界。核心问题是：在相同输入维度和隐私预算下，量子模型能否比经典模型保留更多效用？

Method: 将每条临床记录或参与者视为隐私单元，在三个临床队列和一个可穿戴队列上实验。对比容量匹配的量子模型、多层感知机和逻辑回归，跨五个隐私预算和五个随机种子评估。使用差分隐私训练（DP-SGD），分析梯度裁剪阈值对效用的影响。最后在 IBM ibm_fez（156 量子位超导处理器）上用八个固定测试用例进行推理实验，对比模拟器结果。

Result: 量子模型在三个临床队列上平均 ROC AUC 领先达 0.104，但在可穿戴队列落后（因参与者少需更大噪声）。24 次测试经 Holm 校正后无显著差异，仅显示趋势。解析梯度界表明降低裁剪阈值可减少噪声、提升效用，尽管引入裁剪偏差。硬件实测：私有推理平均绝对概率误差 0.046，非私有 0.063，支持 NISQ 硬件可行性。

Conclusion: 在差分隐私约束下，量子模型对小规模临床队列显示出效用优势趋势，但尚未达到统计学显著的量子优势。降低梯度裁剪阈值可在隐私预算固定时改善效用。IBM 量子处理器上的推理实验验证了噪声中等规模量子硬件执行差分隐私量子模型推理的可行性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Clinical registries and wearable sensors support early health-risk assessment, yet models trained on these records may leak sensitive information when released. Differential privacy (DP) bounds this leakage by injecting noise whose total magnitude grows with the number of trainable parameters, a cost especially severe for small health cohorts. This motivates parameter efficient models including quantum machine learning (QML), whose variational circuits can represent complex decision boundaries with few trainable parameters. It raises a question: at matched input dimensionality and privacy budget, does a quantum model retain more utility than a classical one? We investigate this question on three clinical cohorts and one wearable cohort, treating each clinical record or participant as a privacy unit. We compare capacity-matched quantum, multilayer perceptron, and logistic regression models across five privacy budgets and five seeds. The quantum model leads on three clinical cohorts by up to 0.104 ROC AUC averaged across budgets; however, trails on the wearable cohort, where the small number of participants requires substantial noise. No difference survives Holm correction across 24 tests, so these results indicate a trend rather than a quantum advantage. An analytic gradient bound shows that lower clipping thresholds can improve utility by reducing noise despite clipping bias. Finally, an experiment on IBM quantum computer (ibm_fez, a 156-qubit superconducting processor) using eight fixed test cases yields mean absolute probability errors of 0.046 (private) and 0.063 (non-private) relative to simulation, supporting the feasibility of inference on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/TITANS-Lab-Artifacts/DP-QML-Health.

</details>


### [145] [From Weather to Enhanced Weathering: Weather Foundation Models for Carbon Dioxide Removal at Operational Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28973)
*Sasankh Munukutla,Felipe Oviedo,Danrong Zhang,Kenji Takeda,Juan M Lavista Ferres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了五个天气基础模型(FMs)作为冻结编码器配合轻量级解码器，用于增强岩石风化(ERW)碳移除量化预测。在巴西和美国264个田块的5,192个年代序列上，采用留一田块交叉验证，发现最佳模型TerraNova-ERW(基于Aurora 1.5)能对未见田块内的测量进行正向排序。零样本预测新田块平均移除量尚不可行，归因于数据规模不足；但少样本预测(每田块2个样本)已可行：能识别出60%以上最高移除量田块，将估算田块平均移除量所需样本减半，在最密集采样田块上可减少28-98%的采样量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强岩石风化(ERW)有潜力实现吉吨级年CO₂移除，但目前量化移除量需要密集土壤采样和实验室分析，成本高昂。现有天气基础模型在自然天气任务上表现优异，但其在ERW操作数据上的迁移能力未被系统评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索基础模型表示是否能迁移到ERW任务，以及需要何种数据规模和采样策略才能实现实用化预测。

Method: 选取五个天气基础模型作为冻结编码器，接轻量级解码器，在5,192个年代序列(来自巴西和美国264个田块)上采用留一田块交叉验证(leave-one-field-out)进行整体评估。对比零样本、少样本(每田块2个样本)及全监督设置，构建TerraNova-ERW模型(基于Aurora 1.5)，并在约10⁵自然风化序列上验证架构扩展性。

Result: 1) 所有天气FMs的表示均能迁移到ERW：TerraNova-ERW能对从未见过的田块内测量进行正向排序(Spearman ρ>0)。2) 零样本预测新田块平均移除量不可行，归因于ERW数据规模远小于自然风化数据(~10⁵序列)。3) 少样本预测(每田块2样本)实用：识别出>60%最高移除量田块，将估算田块均值所需样本减半，最密集田块采样量可减少28-98%。

Conclusion: 天气基础模型的表示能有效迁移到ERW碳移除量化任务，但零样本泛化受限于数据规模。少样本微调(每田块仅需2样本)即可大幅降低采样成本，使ERW监测具备操作可行性。未来ERW数据积累到~10⁵序列规模时，有望实现零样本预测。本工作为大规模ERW部署的监测、报告和核查(MRV)提供了数据驱动的采样优化路径。}

Abstract: Enhanced Rock Weathering (ERW) is a carbon removal solution with the potential to remove gigatons of CO$_2$ per year, but quantifying that removal today requires dense soil sampling and laboratory analysis. We evaluate five weather Foundation Models (FMs) as frozen encoders feeding lightweight decoders, under a leave-one-field-out protocol on 5,192 chronosequences from 264 fields across Brazil and the US. To our knowledge, this is the first holistic evaluation of weather FMs for ERW and the largest evaluation on operational ERW data. We find that across weather FMs, representations transfer to ERW: our best model, TerraNova-ERW, built on Aurora 1.5, positively orders measurements within fields it has never seen. Zero-shot prediction of a new field's mean removal is not yet feasible, and we attribute this to data scale: the same architecture excels on $\sim$$10^5$ natural-weathering sequences, anchoring the scale we expect ERW data must reach. Few-shot prediction, blending the model with a few samples per field, is supported today: TerraNova-ERW recovers over 60% of the highest-removal fields with two samples per field, roughly halves the samples needed to estimate a field's mean removal, and can cut sampling on our densest fields by 28-98%.

</details>


### [146] [How Does Orthogonalization Adapt to the Neural Network Hessian Structure? A Gradient Self Outer-Product Analysis at Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28975)
*Shenyang Deng,Garvit Goyal,Tianyu Pang,Shuhua Yu,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为 Muon 优化器中动量矩阵的对角占优现象提供了定量渐近理论，解释了过参数化如何导致神经网络初始化时梯度外积矩阵的对角占优，从而揭示了正交化预条件子与 Hessian 结构匹配的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽指出 Muon 的隐式预条件子与层级 Hessian 的行分块对角占优结构一致，且在语言模型中观测到动量外积矩阵的对角占优，但缺乏对神经网络为何产生这种对角占优的理论解释。

Method: 在四种典型模型（对称矩阵分解、深度线性网络、双层 ReLU 网络、单层线性注意力）中，于初始化时分析梯度外积矩阵的期望 E[G G^T]（等于动量外积期望 E[V V^T]），推导隐藏维度增长时对角项与非对角项的显式增长率。

Result: 所有模型中对角项均以显式速率超越非对角项；注意力机制中查询、键、值权重的非对角项甚至完全消失。模拟实验与语言模型初始化实验验证了理论预测的缩放规律。

Conclusion: 过参数化是导致梯度外积矩阵对角占优的根本原因，这为 Muon 正交化预条件子适配神经网络 Hessian 结构提供了理论依据，并定量揭示了不同架构中对角占优的具体缩放行为。

Abstract: Muon orthogonalizes a weight matrix's momentum before each step, and on neural networks this simple preconditioner beats AdamW in a wide range of tasks. Existing analyses explain Muon within general optimization frameworks or on a few specific models, and many of them view orthogonalization as a preconditioner. Since the Hessian is the optimal preconditioner under a quadratic approximation of the loss, these works leave a basic question open: what makes orthogonalization suited to the Hessian of a neural network? Prior work shows that the layer-wise Hessian of a neural network is row-block diagonally dominant, and Muon's implicit preconditioner, the Kronecker product $(\mathbf{V}\mathbf{V}^\top)^{1/2}\otimes\mathbf{I}$ with $\mathbf{V}$ the momentum, has the same row-block structure exactly when $\mathbf{V}\mathbf{V}^\top$ is diagonally dominant. Diagonal dominance of $\mathbf{V}\mathbf{V}^\top$ has been observed in language models, but why a neural network produces it has not been explained. This work provides a quantitative asymptotic theory for neural networks at initialization, and points out the intuition that the overparameterization of the weight matrices drives the diagonal dominance. We analyze $\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{G}\mathbf{G}^\top]$, which equals $\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{V}\mathbf{V}^\top]$ at initialization, in four canonical models: symmetric matrix factorization, deep linear networks, two-layer ReLU networks, and single-layer linear attention. In each case the diagonal entries outgrow the off-diagonal ones at explicit rates as the hidden dimensions grow; for the query, key and value weights of attention the off-diagonal vanishes altogether. Simulations of the four models and experiments on language models at initialization confirm the predicted scaling.

</details>


### [147] [SSMCGM-Stream: A Streaming State-Space Model for Personalized Diabetes Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28976)
*Shakson Isaac,Chirag Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SSMCGM-Stream，一种用于连续血糖监测(CGM)的流式状态空间模型，能够以常数时间进行个性化预测、生理结构化的反事实推理和可解释的预测归因；基于此构建的SSMCGM-Agent通过受保护的语言模型接口提供风险感知的自然语言决策支持。在T1DEXI队列评估中，该方法在保持与强神经网络基线相当的预测性能的同时，大幅降低了计算成本，实现了持续的设备端糖尿病决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续血糖监测(CGM)支持糖尿病管理，但需要既准确、可解释又足够高效以供实时部署的预测模型。现有方法难以同时满足这三个要求：准确性、可解释性和实时计算效率。

Method: 1. SSMCGM-Stream：流式状态空间模型，维护随每次血糖读数更新的个性化隐藏状态，提供常数时间预测、生理结构化的反事实推理和基于隐藏状态归因的可解释预测。2. SSMCGM-Agent：基于SSMCGM-Stream构建，通过受保护的语言模型接口将预测、解释和反事实转化为落地的建议，提供风险感知的自然语言决策支持。

Result: 在T1DEXI队列评估中，SSMCGM-Stream达到与强神经网络基线相当的预测性能，同时大幅降低计算成本，使持续的设备端糖尿病决策支持成为可能。

Conclusion: SSMCGM-Stream和SSMCGM-Agent框架成功解决了CGM预测中准确性、可解释性和实时效率的三重挑战，为个性化、可解释且可部署的糖尿病管理决策支持系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring supports diabetes management but requires forecasting models that are accurate, interpretable, and efficient enough for real-time deployment. We present SSMCGM-Stream, a streaming state-space model that maintains a personalized hidden state updated with each incoming glucose reading to provide constant-time forecasting, physiologically structured counterfactual reasoning, and interpretable forecasts through hidden-state attribution. Built on this foundation, SSMCGM-Agent delivers risk-aware, natural-language decision support by translating forecasts, explanations, and counterfactuals into grounded recommendations through a guarded language-model interface. Evaluated on the T1DEXI cohort, SSMCGM-Stream achieves forecasting performance comparable to strong neural baselines while substantially reducing computational cost, enabling continuous on-device diabetes decision support.

</details>


### [148] [Attention-based Classification for Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28988)
*Amelia Kawasaki,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文评估了Attention Tracker在AgentDojo基准上的间接提示注入检测性能，发现其声称的数据集无关性不成立；提出AttnDA（基于Fisher线性判别的每头关注度分数向量分类器），在跨数据集设置下提升检测性能，但迁移到AgentDojo仍具挑战；协方差消融实验显示去除头间相关结构有助于性能，复杂协方差假设在跨域数据上导致显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白盒检测方法Attention Tracker声称具备数据集无关的检测能力，但在真实的智能体环境中，长结构化工具输出破坏了其对注意力分布的基本假设，导致检测性能接近随机猜测，亟需改进。

Method: 提出AttnDA：将Attention Tracker的标量阈值替换为基于每头关注度分数向量的Fisher线性判别分析（LDA）分类器；在包含四个基准的跨数据集设置上对比两种方法；并进行协方差消融实验，分析头间相关结构对性能的影响。

Result: AttnDA在智能体和工具集成基准上均提升了检测性能，但无论分类器选择如何，迁移到AgentDojo仍具挑战；消融实验表明，移除头间协方差结构显著提升跨域性能，而复杂的协方差假设会导致跨域数据上性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 基于关注度分数的检测信号无法可靠迁移到智能体环境；在头级注意力特征上训练线性分类器可改善检测，前提是拥有目标部署域的训练数据。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection (IPI) attacks, in which adversarial instructions are embedded in external data processed by a language model, pose a significant threat to the integrity of deployed LLM agents. Attention Tracker (Hung et al., 2025) is a recently proposed white-box detection method that identifies prompt injection attacks by monitoring how a model's attention is distributed between user task tokens and data tokens, claiming dataset-agnostic detection via a scalar focus score threshold. In this paper, we provide the first evaluation of Attention Tracker in a realistic agentic IPI setting using the AgentDojo benchmark, finding that the dataset agnosticism claim does not hold. We find that in agentic environments, long structured tool outputs subvert the fundamental assumptions Attention Tracker makes about attention that the method depends on and produces near-chance detection on AgentDojo across most model families. We propose AttnDA, an extension replacing the scalar threshold with a Fisher Linear Discriminant Analysis classifier over the per-head focus score vector, and evaluate both methods in a cross-dataset setting spanning four benchmarks. AttnDA improves detection performance across agentic and tool-integrated benchmarks, though transfer to AgentDojo remains challenging regardless of classifier choice. A covariance ablation further shows that removing inter-head correlation structure substantially contributes to performance, with complex covariance assumptions causing significant degradation on cross-domain data. These results indicate that the attentional distraction signal underlying focus-score-based detection does not transfer reliably to agentic environments, and that learned linear classifiers over head-level attention features offer improved detection performance, provided training data from the target deployment domain is available.

</details>


### [149] [Doob-Based Generative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28990)
*Maojiang Su,Qijie Zhu,Yu Xiao,Shuyang Yu,Minshuo Chen,Zhaoran Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Doob Policy Optimization (DoobPO)，一种用于扩散和流策略的无似然强化学习微调框架，利用Doob的h变换实现策略改进与生成策略学习的分离，通过轻量级比率模型和条件漂移匹配蒸馏目标策略，适用于PPO和GRPO等策略改进规则，在MuJoCo基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流模型为机器人和序列决策系统提供了表达力强的策略类，但其动作似然通常难以计算，限制了广泛采用的PPO、GRPO等策略优化方法的直接应用。需要一种无需显式似然的RL微调方法。

Method: DoobPO分两步：1) 学习轻量级比率模型表示期望的策略更新（支持PPO、GRPO等改进规则）；2) 通过条件漂移匹配将比率倾斜的目标策略蒸馏回生成策略。理论上证明实用条件漂移匹配目标与理想Doob漂移匹配目标梯度等价，且诱导的扩散过程解决了端点约束随机最优控制问题，实现最小能量修正。

Result: 在MuJoCo连续控制基准上，DoobPO对比竞争性的扩散/流策略基线取得强性能，验证了其为生成策略提供了原则性且实用的RL微调路径。

Conclusion: DoobPO通过Doob h变换实现了生成策略的无似然RL微调，统一适用于扩散和流策略，理论保证了最小能量修正，实验证明了其有效性，为生成式策略的强化学习微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models provide expressive policy classes for robotic and sequential decision-making systems. However, their action likelihoods are generally intractable, limiting the direct use of widely adopted policy optimization methods such as PPO and GRPO. We propose Doob Policy Optimization (DoobPO), a framework that leverages Doob's $h$-transform for likelihood-free reinforcement learning fine-tuning of generative policies. DoobPO separates policy improvement from generative policy learning. It first learns a lightweight ratio model that represents the desired policy update, and then distills the resulting ratio-tilted target policy into the generative policy via conditional drift matching. This formulation applies uniformly to both diffusion and flow policies and supports a broad family of policy improvement rules, including PPO and GRPO. We prove that the practical conditional drift matching objective is gradient-equivalent to the ideal Doob drift matching objective. We further show that the induced diffusion process solves an endpoint-constrained stochastic optimal control problem. This establishes that it achieves the minimum-energy modification required to realize the desired policy tilt. Across MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks, DoobPO achieves strong performance against competitive diffusion- and flow-policy baselines, showing a principled and practical route to RL fine-tuning for generative policies.

</details>


### [150] [Rethinking Time Series Tokenization from a Frequency Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28997)
*Zelong Tian,Ming Jin,Bo Du,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TimeFT，一种基于频域的时间序列分词器，通过频域划分、频移和奈奎斯特采样实现近乎无失真的分词，解决了现有分词策略固有的频谱失真问题，显著提升预测、分类和异常检测等任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列分词策略将序列划分为子序列，理论分析表明这会引入频谱失真，导致学习到的表示偏离真实的趋势、周期性和季节性等底层模式，从而损害模型性能。

Method: TimeFT 采用频域划分、频移和奈奎斯特采样三步获取 token，无需额外参数，计算复杂度极低，可直接替换现有分词器。

Result: 在预测、分类、异常检测等多样化任务上的大量实验表明，TimeFT 能够持续且显著地提升模型性能。

Conclusion: TimeFT 通过频域视角消除了分词过程中的频谱失真，提供了一种近乎无损、免参数、即插即用的时间序列分词范式，为时间序列建模奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: Tokenization that partitions time series into subsequences has become a foundational paradigm in modern time series modeling. However, we find that current tokenization strategies inherently introduce spectral distortions, forcing the learned representations to diverge from the true underlying patterns such as trend, periodicity, and seasonality, thereby impairing model performance. Through theoretical analysis from a frequency perspective, we derive the boundary conditions under which such distortions occur. To overcome this distortion, we propose TimeFT, a nearly distortion-free frequency-based tokenizer for time series data. TimeFT obtains tokens through frequency-domain partitioning, frequency shifting, and Nyquist sampling. The method is parameter-free, incurs negligible computational complexity, and can serve as a drop-in replacement for existing tokenizers. Our extensive experiments on diverse tasks such as forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection demonstrate that TimeFT consistently and significantly improves performance.

</details>


### [151] [FlashNext-RL: A System Optimization Study for Long-Horizon Agentic RL of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28998)
*Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashNext-RL，一个针对Qwen3.8-Flash-Next模型的专用强化学习训练优化框架，基于prime-rl并结合vLLM进行软件工程（SWE）rollout生成。通过优化主机卸载、梯度累积、混合专家、稀疏注意力和门控残差流的内核，实现了在单个8×B200节点上以BF16精度训练完整48层模型。非填充训练吞吐量从约395 tokens/s/GPU（32K配置）提升至4,438 tokens/s/GPU（131K配置），硬件矩阵乘法吞吐量从约20提升至213 TFLOPs/GPU。系统支持1,024可训练rollouts的批次和131,072 token的上下文限制，迭代平均耗时22.4分钟。结果强调了针对模型架构、内存布局和内核需求定制RL训练的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体强化学习需要高效的rollout生成和长序列训练。然而，Qwen3.8-Flash-Next的内存需求和模型特定算子使得现有开源实现难以应用。现有通用RL基础设施无法充分利用该模型的架构特性，导致训练效率低下、显存不足等问题。

Method: 1. 基于prime-rl构建FlashNext-RL框架，集成vLLM用于SWE rollout生成。 2. 在单个8×B200节点上进行BF16全模型训练，冻结前两层、n-gram表、稀疏注意力索引器和多token预测头。 3. 优化主机卸载策略、梯度累积机制，以及混合专家（MoE）、稀疏注意力和门控残差流的专用内核。 4. 进行全模型开发研究和受控累积研究（8层模型）量化各优化贡献。 5. 部署三个推理节点支持实时SWE RL运行。

Result: 1. 非填充训练吞吐量：从~395 tokens/s/GPU（32K配置）提升至4,438 tokens/s/GPU（131K配置），提升约11.2倍。 2. 硬件矩阵乘法吞吐量：从~20提升至213 TFLOPs/GPU，提升约10.6倍。 3. 受控累积研究量化了各项优化的个体贡献。 4. 实时SWE RL运行：目标批次1,024可训练rollouts，上下文限制131,072 token，步骤5-15平均迭代时间22.4分钟，熵有界且训练器-采样器KL散度不匹配受控。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对特定模型架构、内存布局和内核需求定制RL训练框架，比仅依赖通用RL基础设施更为关键。FlashNext-RL通过模型专用优化，显著提升了长时程智能体RL的训练效率和可扩展性，为大规模模型的RL训练提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning (RL) requires both efficient rollout generation and training on long sequences. However, the memory requirements and model-specific operators of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next make this difficult with existing open-source implementations. To resolve these limitations, we introduce FlashNext-RL, a model-specific RL training optimization framework based on prime-rl, with vLLM for software engineering (SWE) rollout generation. Our main focus is BF16 training of the full 48-layer model on a single 8xB200 node, with the first two layers, the n-gram table, the sparse-attention indexer and the multi-token prediction head frozen. We optimize host offload, gradient accumulation and the kernels for mixture-of-experts, sparse attention and the gated residual stream. In the full-model development study, non-padding training throughput increases from approximately 395 tokens/s/GPU in the initial 32K-token configuration to 4,438 tokens/s/GPU in the optimized 131K-token configuration, with hardware matmul throughput (aka without considering the cost of attention components) increasing from approximately 20 to 213 TFLOPs/GPU. A controlled cumulative study on an 8-layer model further quantifies the contributions of individual optimizations. Together with three inference nodes, the system supports a live SWE RL run with a target batch of 1,024 trainable rollouts and a 131,072-token context limit. Over steps 5-15, an iteration takes 22.4 minutes on average, with bounded entropy and trainer-sampler mismatch KL. These results highlight the importance of tailoring RL training to the model's architecture, memory layout and kernel requirements, rather than relying only on general-purpose RL infrastructure.

</details>


### [152] [Local and Sparse Kernel Operator Learning for Incompressible Flows: An Operator Lagrange Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29009)
*Ramansh Sharma,Matthew Lowery,Houman Owhadi,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部稀疏核方法，用于学习不可压流体的解算子，解决了早期全局稠密核方法的扩展性问题，在保持精度的同时显著提高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期的保性质核方法存在全局输出重构和稠密算子回归，导致计算和存储随问题规模增长而急剧恶化，难以应用于大规模流体模拟。

Method: 1. 用局部无散度插值（任意次多项式）替代全局输出重构，在GPU上并行求解，保持无散度性、周期性，并在多尺度核下逼近Kolmogorov统计。2. 采用任意输入维度下半正定的紧支撑核实现稀疏算子回归，支撑半径（稀疏度）由数据拟合确定，可选仿射多项式捕捉全局趋势。3. 算子Lagrange权重连接两部分，每个新输入仅需一次稀疏求解，作用于训练场而非局部系数，避免了空间系数抵消导致的误差放大。

Result: 在十个流体基准测试中：验证几乎处处选择稀疏矩阵，稀疏度随训练集增长多降低；仿射增强使强制各向同性湍流误差降低超一个数量级；稀疏模型精度不低于早期稠密方法，优于神经算子；Lagrange评估在局部重构下与直接评估相当，在全局重构下精度高近三个数量级；提供了GPU计时和存储数据。

Conclusion: 该局部稀疏核方法有效解决了扩展性瓶颈，在保持物理性质（无散度、周期性、统计特性）的同时，实现了比稠密核方法和神经算子更优的精度-效率权衡，Lagrange评估机制显著抑制了数值误差放大，适合大规模不可压流模拟。

Abstract: We present a local and sparse kernel method for learning the solution operators of incompressible flows. It builds on our earlier property-preserving kernel method, whose global output reconstruction and dense operator regression scale poorly. Our method replaces the global output reconstruction by local divergence-free interpolants with divergence-free polynomials of any degree, solved in parallel on the GPU. These interpolants are divergence free, periodic on periodic domains, and, with a multiscale kernel, approach Kolmogorov statistics under refinement. A compactly supported kernel, positive semidefinite in any input dimension, makes the operator regression sparse. Its support radius, and hence the sparsity, is fitted to data, and optional affine polynomials in the input capture global trends. Operator Lagrange weights join the two. They cost one sparse solve per new input and act on the training fields before the output reconstruction, so their local coefficients are never formed. We show that, unlike direct evaluation, Lagrange evaluation does not amplify solve errors by the cancellation among spatial coefficients. On ten fluid benchmarks, validation selects sparse matrices nearly everywhere, with densities that mostly decrease as the training set grows, and affine augmentation lowers the error by more than an order of magnitude for forced isotropic turbulence. These sparse models are at least as accurate as our earlier dense method and more accurate than neural operators. Lagrange evaluation is as accurate as direct evaluation with local output reconstructions, and up to nearly three orders of magnitude more accurate with global ones. We also present GPU timings and storage.

</details>


### [153] [Dissimilarity-Based Markov Decision Processes for Stabilizing State Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29024)
*Xun Shen,Yuepeng Wang,Daniyal Sajedi-Hosseini,Akifumi Wachi,Ken Kawano,Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Mehrshad Sadria,Sébastien Gros,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于差异度的马尔可夫决策过程（DMDP）框架，通过优化状态分布与目标分布的差异度来学习稳定状态分布的策略，建立了渐近稳定性的充分条件，并开发了有限维分布表示和策略证书，量化了学习与估计误差对稳定性的影响，结合PPO和SAC实现，在线性系统和HalfCheetah任务上验证了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习通常优化期望累积奖励，但可靠且可重复的行为还依赖于状态分布随时间的演化。仅优化期望回报无法保证状态分布收敛到期望目标或保持稳定。因此需要一套理论框架来学习能够稳定状态分布的策略。

Method: 1. 制定差异度马尔可夫决策过程（DMDP），策略作用于当前状态分布，目标测量其与目标分布的差异度。2. 建立最优策略使目标分布渐近稳定的充分条件。3. 引入分布的有限维表示，证明其参数动力学的稳定性保证可扩展到底层状态分布。4. 开发学习策略的证书，包括使用估计分布参数部署。5. 量化学习和估计误差对保证的影响，识别保持渐近稳定的条件。6. 将分布估计和目标依赖奖励与PPO、SAC结合用于实际实现。

Result: 在线性系统的可计算示例中展示了理论证书的有效性；在HalfCheetah任务上实验表明，分布反馈和显式目标匹配对于调节速度分布具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 本文为学习稳定状态分布的策略提供了完整的理论框架，包括DMDP建模、稳定性条件、有限维表示、策略证书及误差分析，并通过实验验证了方法在连续控制任务中的有效性，为分布感知的强化学习奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for stochastic systems typically optimizes expected cumulative rewards, while reliable and repeatable behavior also depends on how the state distribution evolves over time. Optimizing expected return alone does not guarantee that this distribution converges to a desired target or remains stable. This paper develops a theoretical framework for learning policies that stabilize state distributions. We formulate the problem as a Dissimilarity-based Markov Decision Process (DMDP), in which the policy acts on the current state distribution and the objective measures its dissimilarity to a target distribution. We establish sufficient conditions under which the optimal policy makes the target distribution asymptotically stable. To support policy learning, we introduce a finite-dimensional representation of distributions and show how stability guarantees for its parameter dynamics extend to the underlying state distributions. We further develop certificates for the learned policies, including their deployment using estimated distribution parameters. These results quantify how learning and estimation errors affect the guarantees and identify conditions under which learned policies retain asymptotic stability. For practical implementation, we combine distribution estimates and target-dependent rewards with PPO and SAC. A computable example on a linear system illustrates the proposed theoretical certificates. Furthermore, experiments on a HalfCheetah task demonstrate the benefits of distribution feedback and explicit target matching for regulating the velocity distribution.

</details>


### [154] [Better Policies, Worse Verifier Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29029)
*Daniel Cieślak,Jacek Łubiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究验证器栈在后训练和测试时计算循环中的行为，发现当策略掌握了部分任务 F 后，联合目标 y = F ∧ M 的在策略 AUC 会发生「在策略审计崩塌」：权重从 F-失败转移到 M-信息，导致审计偏好更差的组件验证器，最优解变成全结果预测器而非组件神谕。实验证实该崩塌会降低离策略 F-AUC、损害未见生成器并增加下游约束违反。修复方案是：在组件自身的失败上审计，并在刷新分布中保留这些失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证器栈越来越多地嵌入后训练和测试时计算循环中。现有控制循环将组件验证器在新鲜策略输出上按完整成功标签评估并刷新，但忽略了策略分布变化对组件审计指标的扭曲效应。论文旨在揭示并量化这种分布偏移如何导致对组件验证器 F-能力的错误评估，进而引发有害的模型选择和部署决策。

Method: 1. 理论建模：针对联合目标 y = F ∧ M，推导在策略 AUC 分解式 (1-q)A_own + qA_cross，其中 q = Pr[F=1|y=0] 随策略掌握 F 而上升。2. 识别集与阈值：给出锐利识别集、完全擦除阈值，及审计偏好更差验证器的精确临界点。3. 实验验证：三类实验——精确神谕模拟、多标签文本控制任务、心电图预测注册研究——分别展示崩塌、选择反转、离策略性能下降与损害迁移。4. 修复方案：提出保留自身失败支持、受控混合审计，并给出匹配的标签复杂度界。

Result: 1. 理论上证明：当 q → 1 时，审计目标退化为全结果预测器，组件 F-AUC 权重归零，出现「在策略审计崩塌」。2. 实验显示：精确神谕即表现出崩塌；文本任务中审计选中规范 F-AUC 更低的头；ECG 研究中按审计行动会降低离策略 F-AUC、损害未见生成器、增加约束违反。3. 保留组件自身失败分布的受控混合审计能消除崩塌，且标签复杂度达到最优。

Conclusion: 在策略审计崩塌是验证器栈在分布偏移下的根本缺陷：策略改进会悖论性地破坏对组件能力的正确评估。核心修复是「在组件自身的失败上审计，并保留这些失败在刷新分布中」，这同时实现了统计最优性和下游安全性。该成果为基于验证器的后训练/测试时计算循环提供了理论基石与实用准则。}

Abstract: Verifier stacks increasingly sit inside post-training and test-time compute loops. We study a specific control loop: a component verifier is evaluated on fresh policy outputs against the full success label and refreshed when that aggregate score falls. For a conjunctive target $y = F \wedge M$, its on-policy AUC is $(1-q)A_{\mathrm{own}} + qA_{\mathrm{cross}}$, where $q = \Pr[F=1 \mid y=0]$. As the policy masters $F$, $q$ can rise toward one, removing weight from $F$-failures and rewarding information about $M$. We call this on-policy audit collapse. We derive a sharp identified set, a complete-erasure threshold, and the exact point at which the audit can prefer a worse component verifier; its population optimum is the full-outcome predictor, not the component oracle. An exact oracle exhibits the collapse, a natural multi-label text control shows inversion can select a head with lower canonical $F$-AUC at the observed failure mixture, and a prediction-registered ECG study shows that acting on the audit can reduce off-policy $F$-AUC, transfer the damage to an unseen generator, and increase downstream constraint violations. Preserving own-failure support prevents the damage. Matching label-complexity bounds and a controlled-mixture audit give the corresponding repair: audit the component on its own failures, and keep those failures in the refresh distribution.

</details>


### [155] [Gate Initialisation versus Recurrent Dropout and Zoneout in Learning Long Dependencies: A Controlled Study of LSTM and GRU Networks on Synthetic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29034)
*Oleksandr Kholodniak,Oleksandr Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因子设计实验（720次运行），系统研究了LSTM/GRU中门控初始化（常规 vs Chrono）与循环正则化器（Dropout、Zoneout 及其放置位置）的交互效应，并考察早期梯度传播（输入敏感度衰减）是否能预测长依赖学习成功。结果显示：Chrono 初始化极大地加速了长序列任务上的收敛，即使时间跨度估计粗略也有效；常规初始化下 Dropout 多延缓学习，Zoneout 仅在部分条件有益；早期输入敏感度在控制早期验证损失后预测力极弱，且种子内相关性不稳健。结论支持在长依赖任务上采用 Chrono 初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常将 Dropout/Zoneout 与门控初始化分别研究，缺乏因子设计下的交互分析；同时不清楚早期梯度传播指标（如隐藏状态对输入的敏感度随滞后衰减）能否在训练早期识别出能成功学习长依赖的运行。

Method: 在单层 LSTM 和 GRU 上，针对 Adding Problem 和 Delayed Recall 两个长依赖基准任务，采用 2（初始化：常规、Chrono）× 6（循环正则化器放置方案）× 2（架构）× 多随机种子的因子网格，共 720 个独立运行。每个运行记录 5000 步内的验证损失及最终隐藏状态对每个时滞输入的敏感度，任务数据无限生成，排除泛化干扰，纯测优化动态。

Result: 1. Chrono 初始化下所有 360 运行均在 5000 步内达标；常规初始化仅在时间跨度约为真实值 1/4~4 倍的无正则运行中有 119/120 达标，更长序列则收敛极慢或失败。2. 常规初始化下 Dropout 普遍拖慢收敛，Zoneout 仅在特定放置位置略有帮助。3. 纳入早期验证损失后，早期输入敏感度衰减曲线对‘是否最终学会’几无增量预测力。4. 种子内分析显示：学会的种子早期敏感度衰减更平缓，但一致性指数仅 0.61，且强依赖条件、对种子重采样不鲁棒。

Conclusion: Chrono 门控初始化在长依赖任务上表现稳健高效，即使时间跨度估计粗略也远优于常规初始化；常规初始化下常用循环正则化器收益有限甚至有害；早期梯度传播指标无法可靠地在训练早期筛选出成功运行。建议在长序列建模中优先采用 Chrono 初始化。

Abstract: Dropout and zoneout are routinely added to long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) networks, but, to our knowledge, their placements have not been crossed with gate initialisation in a factorial design, and it is not known whether early gradient propagation tells which runs will learn long dependencies. We trained one-layer LSTMs and GRUs on the adding problem and on delayed recall in a factorial grid of 720 runs, crossing conventional and chrono gate initialisation with up to six recurrent regulariser placements at one rate each, with pre-specified follow-ups, and recorded how sensitive the final hidden state was to the input at every lag. Because both tasks supply fresh or exhaustive training data, the study measures the regularisers' effects on optimisation and does not assess generalisation from a finite training set. At lengths chosen to make conventional initialisation difficult, all 360 chrono-initialised runs reached the validation criterion within 5000 updates, as did 119 of 120 unregularised runs with a horizon about four times too short or too long, whereas conventionally initialised networks learned slowly or did not reach the criterion within the budget at the longer lengths. Under conventional initialisation, dropout generally delayed learning and zoneout helped only in some conditions. The early input sensitivity added little to predicting which conditions learn once the early validation loss was modelled per task, architecture and initialisation. Within conditions, a flatter early decay went with the seeds that learned, but in a pre-specified test the association was modest (concordance 0.61), condition-dependent and not robust to resampling seeds. The results support chrono initialisation, even with a rough horizon, on such tasks.

</details>


### [156] [ART-MPG: Autoregressive R/T Manifold Path Guiding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29049)
*Zehao Zhao,Yanyang Yang,Xiaole Zhang,Xiyu Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ART-MPG，一种用于采样镜面链路的紧凑神经模型，通过自回归采样链路长度、反射/透射字符串及方向，并扩展流形路径引导的重试加权策略，在 14 个场景中以相同 SPP 实现比现有在线/离线基线更低的均方误差和运行方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜面链路将重要光传输集中在路径空间的极小区域，且其结构强依赖于链路拓扑，现有方法难以高效采样此类高频贡献，导致渲染噪声大、收敛慢。

Method: ART-MPG 包含三大创新：(1) 紧凑神经网络联合预测链路长度、自回归 R/T 字符串、以及基于拓扑条件的四分量 von Mises-Fisher 混合模型采样方向；(2) 将 MPG 的重试采样扩展为同时条件于链路长度与 R/T 字符串，理论上保持无偏期望的同时降低期望重试次数与倒数估计器方差；(3) 区分形式无偏性证明与实验中使用的截断/钳位渲染器。

Result: 在 14 个测试场景、多种 SPP 设置下，该方法始终以相同 SPP 获得比在线/离线基线更低的 MSE，且跨运行标准差更小，验证了更优的收敛稳定性。

Conclusion: ART-MPG 通过拓扑感知的神经采样与改进的重试策略，在理论无偏性与实际渲染质量之间取得良好平衡，显著提升了镜面链路主导场景的渲染效率与稳健性。

Abstract: Specular chains concentrate important transport in small regions of path space and the structure depends on chain topology. We introduce ART-MPG, a compact neural model that samples chain length, an autoregressive reflection/transmission (R/T) string, and a direction from a topology-conditioned four-component von Mises-Fisher (vMF) mixture. We extend the repeated trial weighting in Manifold Path Guiding (MPG) by conditioning retry sampling on both chain length and the sampled R/T string, accounting for its known mass outside the stochastic re-hit trials. For a fixed proposal and target chain, this preserves the ideal expectation while reducing the expected retry count and reciprocal-estimator variance compared with retries conditioned on chain length alone. We distinguish the formal unbiasedness result from the capped and clamped renderer used in experiments. We evaluate fourteen scenes across a range of samples per pixel (SPP) against existing online and offline rendering algorithms. Our method consistently produces lower mean squared error at equal SPP than the online and offline baselines, with lower standard deviation across runs.

</details>


### [157] [Dynamics of Hidden Learning Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29054)
*Qinyou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了纤维调节理论(FRT)和纤维获取理论(FAT)来刻画隐藏学习结构的动态，前者描述受保护的功能修复与修复后的可用学习响应，后者描述可训练结构的形成与更新；两者相互约束，联合产生单独操作无法达到的有利响应。在Qwen2.5-32B上实现了受保护头修复与固定容量LoRA更新，跨两条训练轨迹验证协调策略在所有对比中均优于单独策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习理论主要关注当前预测如何决定下一步学习，但忽略了隐藏学习结构（如参数子空间、特征表示）的动态演化如何约束和赋能学习过程。需要一套形式化框架来刻画结构修复与结构获取的相互作用，并指导实际大模型中的结构化自我调节。

Method: 1. 提出FRT：定义受保护修复义务与修复后的响应可达集。2. 提出FAT：区分中性安装、学习信号接入、额外功能方向三阶段，刻画可训练结构的生成与更新。3. 证明联合准入性与响应可达性，给出有限监督学习构造。4. 实现：在Qwen2.5-32B上用AdamW、重放与输出蒸馏，实施受保护头修复（冻结非目标头）与固定容量LoRA更新（秩固定、周期性重置）。

Result: 在两条不同随机种子与样本顺序的训练轨迹上，协调策略（FRT+FAT）在教师强制Exact Match指标上全部四次对比均显著优于仅FRT或仅FAT策略；在第二条轨迹上，宏平均NLL在两种学习规则下均最低。实验验证了结构条件自我调节在信息与动作约束下的有效性。

Conclusion: FRT与FAT为隐藏学习结构的动态提供了统一理论框架，揭示了结构修复与结构获取的相互约束机制。在大语言模型上的实证表明，协调两种操作能在受限信息与动作下实现结构化自我调节，持续提升学习效率与泛化。

Abstract: A learner's present predictions do not fully determine how it will learn next. We study the dynamics of hidden learning structure through two related operations. Fiber Regulation Theory (FRT) describes protected repair of existing function and the learning responses left available after repair. Fiber Acquisition Theory (FAT) describes the formation and renewal of trainable structure, distinguishing neutral installation, access to a learning signal, and additional functional directions. The two operations also constrain one another. A repair obligation can force a change in a learning channel, while acquired features can make new protected repairs feasible or reduce the minimum coefficient cost of existing ones. We characterize joint admissibility and response reachability, and give a finite supervised-learning construction in which the two operations together produce a favorable response absent from either operation alone. We implement protected head repair and fixed-capacity LoRA renewal in Qwen2.5-32B with AdamW, under replay and output distillation. Across two training trajectories differing in seed and within-task example order, the coordinated policy achieves higher teacher-forced Exact than either separate policy in all four comparisons. It also has the lowest macro NLL under both learning rules on the second trajectory. The implementations realize structure-conditioned self-regulation within specified information and action constraints: accessible state readouts and arrived evidence guide repairs and structural renewals, and ordinary learning continues from the modified state.

</details>


### [158] [Distilling Multi-Teacher Scoring Principles for Unsupervised Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29063)
*Dongchan Cho,Jiho Han,Juwon Hwang,Changhee Min,Minsang Kim,Namsoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnoMix 通过从冻结的异构教师检测器的异常分数中提取弱成对监督，利用 CoRank 多教师排序蒸馏目标，将多种互补的异常评分原理蒸馏到一个轻量级学生模型中，实现无监督时间序列异常检测的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常形式多样，单一检测器难以覆盖所有异常类型；虽然无监督异构检测器集成能提供互补信号，但部署集成会保留每个检测器的推理和维护成本。因此需要研究如何在无标签的无监督设定下，将异构教师检测器蒸馏为单一轻量级检测器。

Method: AnoMix 首先独立归一化每个教师的异常分数序列，然后提出 CoRank 多教师排序蒸馏目标：将教师分数差距转化为成对监督，通过教师共识构建弱监督信号，训练紧凑的学生模型（基准设置下参数量 < 80k）。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上，AnoMix 成功整合了互补的教师评分原理，性能优于单个检测器和静态分数集成，且学生模型极其紧凑。零样本变体表明蒸馏的排序信号可迁移至未见数据集，甚至超越大型预训练时间序列基座模型。

Conclusion: AnoMix 证明了在无监督设定下，通过弱成对监督和排序蒸馏，可以将异构教师检测器的互补知识高效压缩到单一轻量级模型中，实现了高性能、低成本的时间序列异常检测部署，且具备零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Time series anomalies take diverse forms, and individual detectors encode different anomaly scoring principles that rarely cover all anomaly types. Recent unsupervised time-series anomaly detectors provide complementary scoring signals, yet deploying them as an ensemble preserves the inference and maintenance cost of every individual detector. We study whether heterogeneous teacher detectors can be distilled into a single lightweight detector in the unsupervised setting, where no anomaly labels are available to define a shared target. AnoMix addresses this by extracting weak pairwise supervision from the training-set anomaly scores of frozen teachers. It normalizes each teacher score sequence independently and trains the student with CoRank, a multi-teacher ranking-distillation objective that converts teacher score gaps into pairwise supervision through teacher consensus. Across seven benchmark datasets, AnoMix successfully integrates complementary teacher scoring principles into a single deployable model, improving over individual detectors and static score ensembles while using a compact student with under 80k parameters on the benchmark settings. A zero-shot variant further shows that the distilled ranking signal transfers to unseen datasets and can outperform large pretrained time-series foundation models for anomaly detection.

</details>


### [159] [The Price of Choice in Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29067)
*Daniel Cieślak,Andrzej Czyżewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究选择性分类器的风险覆盖契约审计问题，分析了交并检验（IUT）在菜单丰富度下的样本复杂度，证明了K区间菜单的极小极大样本量为K(1-κ)/(κγ²)（对数因子内），并通过模拟和真实VLM实验验证了理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性分类器常声称满足风险覆盖契约：存在预声明菜单中的某规则（阈值或K个分数区间的并集）使覆盖率≥κ且误差≤α。反驳此存在性声明需排除所有允许规则。交并检验（IUT）无需多重性校正即可保证有效性，但其样本成本随菜单丰富度增加。本文旨在表征该成本并与最优审计比较。

Method: 理论分析：在固定内部风险目标α和间隙γ下，推导K区间菜单在覆盖率下界κ处的极小极大样本量下界，并证明精确IUT达到该速率；扩展到VC维度为d的菜单。实验部分：模拟K=4下不同κ的菜单成本变化；在三个VLM裁判上进行保留实验，测试60个分割特定的置信度门控族的精确隐藏窗口；在多标注者目标上评估隐藏迁移性；在冻结图像和多模态分类器上验证边界行为。

Result: 极小极大样本量为K(1-κ)/(κγ²)（对数因子内），精确IUT达到此速率。下界即使在已知分数分布下成立，上界推广到VC维度d的菜单。拒绝拒绝的程序（当任何规则在审计样本上看似可行时）可能付出线性K成本，而平方根速率是可能的。无参考测试在恒定风险备择下规模更小，但未知异质风险可能导致线性成本不可避免。模拟显示原始菜单成本随κ从0.5到0.95变化7.7倍，定理激励归一化后端点差异约5%。VLM实验：所有60个置信度门控族均显示正精确隐藏窗口，30个中28个在审计A选中的契约在未触碰的审计B上保持隐藏。多标注者目标上隐藏较弱，10次重分割中3次迁移。冻结图像和多模态分类器表现出与IUT保证一致的边界行为，且相对实际证书有强功效。

Conclusion: 本文精确表征了选择性分类器风险覆盖契约审计的样本复杂度，证明了IUT在菜单丰富度下的最优性，并揭示了不同测试程序的成本权衡。实验证实了理论预测，并展示了在真实VLM判断器上的实用有效性，为可靠的选择性分类器部署提供了统计保证。

Abstract: A selective classifier may be claimed to satisfy a risk-coverage contract if some rule from a predeclared menu (a threshold or a union of $K$ score intervals) attains coverage at least $κ$ and error at most $α$. Refuting this existential claim requires ruling out every permitted rule. The intersection-union test (IUT) achieves validity without a multiplicity correction, but its sample cost can increase with menu richness. We characterize this cost and compare it with the optimal audit. For a fixed interior risk target $α$ and gap $γ$, the minimax sample size for K-interval menus at coverage floor $κ$ is $K(1-κ)/(κγ^2)$, up to logarithmic factors, in the stated small-gap, large-menu regime; exact IUT attains this rate. The lower bound holds even with a known score distribution, while the upper bound extends to menus of VC dimension $d$. Procedures that refuse to reject whenever any rule appears feasible on the audit sample can pay a linear $K$ cost even when a square-root rate is possible. Reference-free testing reaches a smaller scale under constant-risk alternatives, whereas unknown heterogeneous risks can make the linear cost unavoidable. In simulations with $K=4$, the raw menu cost varies 7.7-fold between $κ=.5$ and $κ=.95$; after theorem-motivated normalization the endpoint values differ by approximately 5%. Held-out experiments with three VLM judges show positive exact-hiding windows in all 60 split-specific confidence-gate families, with 28/30 contracts chosen on audit-A remaining hidden on untouched audit-B. On a cleaner multi-annotator target, hiding is weaker and transfers in 3/10 resplits. Frozen image and multimodal classifiers show boundary behavior consistent with the IUT guarantee and strong power relative to practical certificates.

</details>


### [160] [Value-Rectified Distillation for Flow-based Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29075)
*Ke Jiang,Wen Jiang,Yoshinobu Kawahara,Xiaoyang Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Value-Rectified Distillation (VRD) 框架，解决基于 Flow Matching 的离线 RL 行为策略蒸馏中的 OOD 模式平均问题。通过价值引导的生成轨迹对齐与动态耦合机制，显式解耦多模态行为并隔离高价值动作，包含 VRD-Q（价值分位数行为蒸馏）和 VRD-R（潜在空间重组）两种算法，在 D4RL、OGBench、Minari 基准上显著提升性能，尤其在多模态非专家数据集上效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线 RL 中，基于 Flow Matching 的行为策略蒸馏面临 OOD 模式平均问题：标准蒸馏会强制策略在多模态数据集中插值不同行为模式，导致生成动作塌缩到无效的分布外区域，严重损害策略性能。

Method: 提出 VRD 框架，将离线策略蒸馏重构为价值引导的生成轨迹对齐问题。核心是动态耦合机制，主动修正流动力学以解耦不同行为模式，隔离高价值动作。实例化为两种互补算法：VRD-Q 利用价值分位数行为蒸馏统计过滤低价值行为；VRD-R 重组潜在空间，几何隔离 FM 行为策略中的高价值生成轨迹。

Result: 在 D4RL、OGBench、Minari 等连续控制与规划任务的广泛实验中，VRD 显著优于现有生成式基线，尤其在多模态、非专家数据集上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: VRD 通过价值引导的动态耦合机制有效解决了 FM 蒸馏中的 OOD 模式平均问题，实现了多模态行为的显式解耦与高价值动作的隔离，为离线 RL 中的生成式策略学习提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We aim to address the problem of Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Mode Averaging, a critical problem that emerges when distilling Flow Matching (FM) based behavior policies in offline Reinforcement Learning (RL): When learning over multi-modal datasets, standard distillation inadvertently forces the policy to interpolate across different behavioral modes, causing the generated actions to collapse into invalid, OOD regions. To resolve this, we propose Value-Rectified Distillation (VRD), a novel framework that reformulates offline policy distillation as a value-guided generative trajectory alignment problem. By employing a dynamic coupling mechanism, VRD actively rectifies flow dynamics to explicitly decouple distinct behavioral modes, cleanly isolating high-value actions. Specifically, we instantiate VRD through two complementary algorithms: VRD-Q, which utilizes value-quantile behavior distillation to statistically filter out low-value behaviors, and VRD-R, which reorganizes the latent space to geometrically isolate high-value generative trajectories from the FM-based behavior policy. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that VRD improves existing generative baselines across diverse continuous control and planning tasks on the D4RL, OGBench, and Minari benchmarks, especially over those multi-modal, non-expert datasets.

</details>


### [161] [Causal world models under physical parameter shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29088)
*Chu Lin,Yi Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究世界模型在分布外物理条件下的预测性能，通过因果建模分析输入选择和机制计算两个组件。在合成基准中，冻结依赖掩码将OOD RMSE从1.769降至0.697；在CausalWorld机器人链路质量外推任务中，提出手指局部世界模型，显式建模质量参数进入惯性和动力学偏置项，结合已知PD控制器或学习扭矩网络，显著提升长时预测R2（0.609→0.727），且优于现有最强循环世界模型。核心发现：物理参数进入动力学机制的方式决定其分布外值是帮助还是损害预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在物理条件超出训练分布时预测精度会下降。现有方法未充分利用因果建模中输入选择和机制计算的分离，导致分布外泛化能力有限。需要研究如何通过显式因果结构设计，使物理参数（如链路质量）在动力学机制中以正确方式参与计算，从而实现分布外鲁棒预测。

Method: 1. 合成基准：使用配对干预推导冻结依赖掩码，限制预测器仅使用因果相关输入。2. CausalWorld机器人任务：构建手指局部世界模型，将链路质量通过显式仿射系数引入惯性矩阵和动力学偏置项；动作通过已知PD控制器或学习扭矩网络作用；学习残差捕捉接触效应。对比基线：固定质量输入为训练均值的单独训练控制器、MLP、循环预测器及最强RWM配置。

Result: 合成基准：冻结依赖掩码将平均OOD RMSE从1.769降至0.697，接近生成器神谕掩码0.694。机器人任务：已知PD模型使中位时均关节R2从0.609升至0.727（150步rollout），10个任务种子全改进（p=0.002）。100步预测中，学习扭矩模型中位关节R2 0.755-0.768，与最强RWM（0.752）相当；而分布外质量输入导致MLP和循环预测器性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 物理参数进入动力学计算机制的方式（如显式仿射系数 vs 隐式神经网络输入）决定了其分布外值是增强还是损害预测性能。显式因果结构设计能将分布外物理参数转化为一致的预测增益，而非隐式黑盒建模。

Abstract: World models predict how embodied systems evolve under actions, but their accuracy can degrade when physical conditions move outside the training distribution. We study two components of causal modeling: which inputs a predictor may use, and how its mechanism computes their effects. In a synthetic benchmark with shortcut features, a frozen dependency mask derived from paired interventions reduces mean out-of-distribution (OOD) RMSE from 1.769 for a fully connected predictor to 0.697, close to the generator-oracle mask (0.694). For robot link-mass extrapolation in CausalWorld, with disjoint training and test mass ranges, we develop finger-local world models in which link masses enter inertia and dynamics-bias terms through explicit affine coefficients, actions act through either a learned torque network or the known PD controller, and learned residuals capture contact-related effects. Compared with a separately trained control whose mass inputs are fixed at training means, the known-PD model turns out-of-range masses into a consistent predictive gain: median time-averaged joint R2 over 150-step rollouts rises from 0.609 to 0.727, with improvement in all ten task-seed runs (exact two-sided sign test, p = 0.002). In a shared 100-step forecast, the learned-torque model is competitive with the strongest recurrent robotic world model (RWM) configuration (median joint R2 of 0.755 - 0.768 versus 0.752), whereas supplying out-of-range masses sharply degrades the MLP and recurrent predictors. These results indicate that how a physical parameter enters the dynamics mechanism determines whether its out-of-range value helps or harms prediction.

</details>


### [162] [MHeatmap: Two-Walk Laplacian Reordering and Mosaic Layout for Rectangular Matrix Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29114)
*Juntang Wang,Sheng'en Li,Liming Zhu,Yihan Wang,Siyuan Cao,Hao Wu,Man Zhang,Shixin Xu,Dongmian Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MHeatmap（马赛克热图）解决加权矩阵热图可读性差的问题：Two-Walk 谱序列化利用二部图的二阶游走捕捉共享邻居结构，通过 Fiedler 向量得到协调的行列顺序；马赛克布局按行列总和比例调整单元格面积，颜色编码数值。实验表明在 15 种合成场景中全优于基线，7 个真实数据集中 14 项指标最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权矩形矩阵（如职业-行业、文档-词项、起点-终点、标注类-聚类）的热图在任意行列顺序下难以阅读，且等大小单元格掩盖了行列总和的巨大差异，需要同时解决序列化与面积编码问题。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) Two-Walk 谱序列化：将矩阵建模为加权二部图，引入长度为二的游走（行共享列、列共享行）构建显式相似度链接，计算图拉普拉斯的 Fiedler 向量并排序得到协调的行列顺序；2) 马赛克布局：将单位正方形划分，行高与行总和成比例、列宽与列总和成比例，颜色表示原始条目值。理论贡献包括 Two-Walk 能量函数结合直接对齐与共享邻居平滑性的证明、稀疏矩阵向量乘法的精确推导、马赛克铺砖与面积语义的形式化定义。

Result: 在 300 个合成矩阵（15 种生成机制）和 7 个真实数据集上评估：Two-Walk 在所有 15 种合成机制中获得最低归一化 2-SUM，在 14 个 Band@10% 指标中取得最高值；当共享邻域能表达潜在关系结构时增益最大。

Conclusion: MHeatmap 通过 Two-Walk 谱序列化有效捕捉二部图的高阶结构，配合马赛克面积编码直观展示边际分布，显著提升加权矩阵热图的可读性与分析效用，尤其适合潜在结构由共享邻域表达的场景。

Abstract: Weighted rectangular matrices record relations between two types of objects, such as occupations and industries, documents and terms, origins and destinations, or annotated classes and computed clusters. Their heatmaps become difficult to read when arbitrary row and column orders scatter related objects, while equal size cells hide large differences in row and column totals. We present MHeatmap (Mosaic Heatmap), which addresses these problems in two stages. Two-Walk spectral seriation first represents the matrix as a weighted bipartite graph. Ordinary bipartite ordering uses only observed row to column links. Two-Walk also uses length-two walks, so rows that share columns and columns that share rows receive explicit similarity links. Sorting a Fiedler vector of the resulting graph Laplacian gives coordinated row and column orders. The Mosaic layout then partitions a unit square so that row height is proportional to the row total and column width is proportional to the column total, while color represents the observed entry. We prove how the Two-Walk energy combines direct alignment with shared-neighbor smoothness, derive an exact matrix vector product that permits sparse computation, and formalize the tiling and area semantics of Mosaic. Across 300 synthetic matrices and seven real data sets, Two-Walk obtains the lowest normalized 2-SUM in all 15 synthetic regimes and the highest Band@10% in 14. The largest gains occur when shared neighborhoods express the latent relational structure.

</details>


### [163] [Mitigating Reward Hacking in Realistic Scenarios Through Representation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29129)
*Austin Meek,Kyle Cox,Matthew Nguyen,Austin J. Brockmeier,Steven Basart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在真实生产环境（含工具调用和长上下文）中监控和缓解奖励黑客攻击，通过三种方法提取的转向向量进行动态干预，在不显著损害模型连贯性和能力的前提下大幅降低奖励黑客行为，且编码环境提取的向量能泛化到其他领域，为生产系统的潜在空间监控和控制铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励黑客攻击是当前生产机器学习系统的主要问题，会导致代价高昂且危险的故障。需要在包含工具调用和长上下文的真实设置中研究如何监控和缓解此问题。

Method: 应用动态干预到三种方法获得的转向方向：1) 标注轨迹的均值差向量，2) 基于梯度优化的向量，3) 模型引用反奖励黑客规范时提取的向量。动态干预比统一向量加到残差流扰动更小，实现更精确的编辑。

Result: 能以低成本显著减少奖励黑客攻击，而不带来相应显著的模型连贯性或能力损失；从编码环境提取的转向向量也能泛化到减少其他领域的奖励黑客行为。

Conclusion: 研究结果为真实生产系统上的潜在空间监控和控制铺平了道路。

Abstract: Reward hacking is a major problem in current production machine learning systems, resulting in costly and dangerous failures. Here we study how to monitor for and mitigate reward hacking in realistic settings, including tool calls and long contexts. We apply a dynamic intervention to steering directions obtained by three different methods: difference-in-means vectors from labeled rollouts, gradient optimization-based vectors, and vectors extracted when models reference an anti-reward-hacking model spec. This dynamic intervention perturbs model representations less than uniform steering vector addition to the residual stream, allowing for more precise edits. We find that we are able to cheaply and significantly reduce reward hacking without correspondingly significant costs to model coherence or capability, and that steering vectors originally extracted from coding environments also generalize to reducing reward hacking in other domains. Our results pave the way towards latent space monitoring and control over realistic production systems.

</details>


### [164] [Conditional Flow Models Convert Epistemic Uncertainty into Apparent Aleatoric Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29172)
*Navodita Sharma,Abhishek Shetty,Max Simchowitz,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究条件流模型（CFM）在回归任务中产生的条件分布携带了哪些超越点预测器的额外信息。发现CFM样本的方差系统性地大于真实条件分布，即使在近乎确定性的回归问题中也是如此。这种额外方差将模型拟合真实条件分布时的认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）转化为生成样本中的表观偶然方差（aleatoric variance）。实验表明，该诱导方差追踪模型对复杂或难拟合目标的欠拟合程度，并随训练增加或数据增多而减小。论文给出了简化设定下的理论解释，并展示了实际应用：利用条件流匹配设计覆盖真实回归目标的高覆盖率预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CFM在机器学习中越来越多地取代确定性预测器（包括传统上作为回归问题建模的任务），需要理解CFM产生的条件分布除了点预测外还提供了什么有用信息。

Method: 通过受控的合成实验和真实数据实验，与显式平滑估计器对比；在简化设定下提供理论分析；演示条件流匹配在构建高覆盖率预测集上的实际应用。

Result: CFM样本常表现出比真实条件分布更大的方差，这种效应在模型未解析出对条件变量的依赖时最显著。额外方差将认知不确定性转化为表观偶然方差，该方差追踪模型对复杂目标的欠拟合，随训练轮数增加或数据量增多而减小。

Conclusion: CFM产生的过大方差不是缺陷，而是模型认知不确定性的体现，可被利用来构建具有高覆盖率保证的回归预测集，为不确定性量化提供实用工具。

Abstract: Conditional Flow Models (CFMs) are increasingly being used in place of deterministic predictors across machine learning, including for tasks that were classically formulated as regression. Given their success in such tasks, we ask the question: what useful information do the conditional distributions produced by CFMs carry beyond what a point predictor would provide?
  We identify a systematic phenomenon: in many natural regimes, CFM samples routinely have larger variance than the true conditional distribution, even in nearly deterministic regression problems. This effect is most pronounced where the model has not resolved the dependence on the conditioning variable, and thus the additional variance can be viewed as converting epistemic uncertainty in fitting the ground truth conditional distribution into apparent aleatoric variance in the generated samples. Through controlled synthetic and real-data experiments, and comparisons with explicit smoothing estimators, we show that the induced variance tracks model underfitting of complex or hard-to-fit targets, and decreases with additional training or data. We provide a theoretical account of the phenomenon in a simplified setting and demonstrate a consequent practical use-case of conditional flow-matching: designing high-coverage predictive sets that cover the ground-truth regression target.

</details>


### [165] [Truncated Out-of-Bag Probability Gain: A Noise-Robust Criterion for Random-Forest Wrapper Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29195)
*Sejong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TOP-Gain（截断袋外概率增益）方法，用于随机森林包装器特征选择，通过截断袋外概率下降来减少噪声，在多个基准数据集上显著优于基线方法，且计算效率更高、特征选择更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林包装器特征选择利用袋外（OOB）预测作为内置验证信号，但前向选择步骤中的准则会累积OOB估计噪声，导致特征排名不稳定。

Method: 提出TOP-Gain，通过计算候选特征对真实类别OOB概率的样本级平均增加量来评分，将概率下降截断为零。在简单噪声模型下，截断可移除约三分之二未受影响样本贡献的噪声方差，同时保留信号，使候选特征排名更具可重现性。

Result: 在26个OpenML-CC18数据集的预注册基准测试中，TOP-Gain在ROC-AUC、准确率和Macro-F1上平均排名最优，显著优于13/14个基线，与VSURF准确率相当但单核计算量减少21倍。在相同包装器内，其选择的特征集比净增益、Brier分数、对数损失和准确率准则更稳定，且不损失准确率。在24个额外数据集的确证性研究中复现了优势。准确率提升广泛存在，稳定性增益随相关特征稀疏度增加而增长。

Conclusion: TOP-Gain通过截断OOB概率下降有效抑制噪声，实现了更准确、更稳定且计算高效的特征选择，适用于高维稀疏相关特征场景。

Abstract: Random-Forest wrapper feature selection can use out-of-bag (OOB) predictions as a built-in validation signal, but the criteria that decide each forward step accumulate OOB estimation noise. We propose TOP-Gain (Truncated Out-of-bag Probability Gain), which scores a candidate feature by the mean sample-level increase in the OOB probability of the true class, with decreases truncated at zero. Under a simple noise model, truncation removes about two-thirds of the noise variance contributed by unaffected samples while preserving the signal, and it makes the ranking of candidate features markedly more reproducible. In a pre-registered benchmark on 26 OpenML-CC18 datasets, TOP-Gain achieved the best mean rank in area under the ROC curve (ROC-AUC), accuracy and Macro-F1 among 15 methods; it was significantly better than 13 of 14 baselines and matched the accuracy of VSURF with 21 times less single-core computation. Within an identical wrapper, it selected significantly more stable feature sets than net-gain, Brier, log-loss and accuracy criteria without loss of accuracy. A confirmatory study on 24 further datasets replicated the advantage over all baselines. The accuracy gains were broad rather than tied to specific dataset characteristics, and, as pre-registered, the stability gain grew as relevant features became sparser

</details>


### [166] [PerSPL: Personalized Semi-supervised Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29219)
*Xiaoan Xu,Tingzhou Wei,Juncheng Dong,Korel Gundem,Ethan X. Fang,Zhengling Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerSPL 是一种骨干无关的个性化半监督偏好学习框架，通过配对均值分数中心化稳定跨提示词的分数坐标，迭代将无标签响应转化为用户特定的训练信号，在四个基准上对已见和未见用户均取得最高平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化奖励模型需要用户特定的偏好比较，但收集此类反馈成本高且每用户监督有限；同时存在大量无用户身份或偏好标签的响应池，现有方法未利用这些数据。直接学习困难，因为成对目标仅约束提示词内的相对分数，而伪标签单个响应需要跨提示词可比的用户分数。

Method: 提出 PerSPL 框架：采用配对均值分数中心化稳定跨提示词的分数坐标系，迭代地将无标签响应转化为用户特定的训练信号，实现半监督个性化偏好学习。该方法与骨干网络无关，可灵活集成。

Result: 在 PersonalLLM、HelpSteer2、Reddit TLDR 和 Chatbot Arena 四个基准上，对比七种代表性基线，PerSPL 在已见和未见用户上均达到评估方法中最高的平均准确率。后两个基准提供真实用户偏好比较，分别针对摘要和开放域聊天机器人交互。

Conclusion: 当每用户偏好标签稀缺时，无标签响应可为个性化奖励建模提供额外监督；PerSPL 通过配对均值分数中心化和迭代伪标签有效利用了这些数据，显著提升了个性化奖励模型的性能。

Abstract: Personalized reward models require user-specific preference comparisons, but collecting such feedback is costly and provides only limited supervision for each user. At the same time, large pools of responses can be generated without user identities or preference labels, yet existing personalized reward models leave this data unused. Directly learning from these responses is difficult because pairwise objectives constrain relative scores within a prompt, whereas pseudo-labeling individual responses requires scores that are comparable across prompts for the same user. We propose \textbf{PerSPL} (Personalized Semi-supervised Preference Learning), a backbone-agnostic framework that uses pair-mean score centering to stabilize this cross-prompt score coordinate and iteratively turns unlabeled responses into user-specific training signals. Against seven representative baselines across four benchmarks---PersonalLLM, HelpSteer2, Reddit TLDR, and Chatbot Arena---PerSPL achieves the highest reported mean accuracy among the evaluated methods for both seen and unseen users. The two latter benchmarks provide real-user preference comparisons in summarization and open-domain chatbot interaction, respectively. These results indicate that unlabeled responses can provide additional supervision for personalized reward modeling when per-user preference labels are scarce.

</details>


### [167] [Delay-Guided Attention for Quality Prediction in Multistage Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29220)
*Jiarui Zhang,Zhaoyang Guan,Zhixuan Xia,Naichen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DAMF（Delay-Guided Attention for Multi-stage Forecasting），针对多阶段制造中阶段间时滞随时间变化导致的质量预测难题，显式建模阶段间时滞分布并将其作为时间先验引入跨阶段注意力机制，实现了对最终质量的精准预测与时滞的可解释恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段制造中，阶段间时滞随时间变化，导致同一时间戳的测量值反映不同的工艺状态。现有方法虽保留生产顺序，但多采用时间戳对齐特征融合或隐式建模延迟依赖，未能识别与每个下游观测相关的上游历史信息，限制了预测精度与可解释性。

Method: 1. 阶段对级时滞预测器：对比当前下游表示与候选滞后上游表示。2. 发生-幅度分解：分别估计正时滞概率及其条件幅度，得到样本级、阶段对级的离散时滞分布，无需推理时指定工厂延迟。3. DelayAlignment：将软时滞分布作为时间先验融入可微分跨阶段注意力。4. 门控残差融合：调控跨生产阶段的信息传播。5. 段级 Viterbi 解码：将逐点时滞分布转化为时序连贯的时滞路径，用于解释；预测阶段保留软分布用于对齐。

Result: 在真实 LiquidSugar 数据集上，DAMF 超越基线模型提升最终质量预测性能；在两个受控时滞基准上，时滞恢复更准确稳定；消融实验验证了时滞预测、延迟引导注意力、结构化时序解码三大模块的贡献。

Conclusion: DAMF 通过显式建模动态时滞分布并引导跨阶段注意力，有效解决了多阶段制造中时变时滞导致的质量预测难题，同时提供了可解释的时滞路径恢复，兼具预测精度与工艺洞察。

Abstract: Quality prediction in multistage manufacturing is challenging when inter-stage lags vary over time, as measurements taken at the same timestamp may reflect different process states. Existing approaches often preserve production order but fuse timestamp-aligned features or model delayed dependencies implicitly, without identifying the upstream history relevant to each downstream observation. We propose Delay-Guided Attention for Multi-stage Forecasting (DAMF), which explicitly estimates inter-stage lag distributions and uses them to guide cross-stage information transfer for final quality prediction. For each adjacent stage pair, a lag predictor compares the current downstream representation with candidate lagged upstream representations. An occurrence-magnitude factorization separately estimates the probability of a positive lag and its conditional magnitude, yielding a sample- and stage-pair-specific distribution over discrete lag states without requiring plant-specified delays at inference. DelayAlignment incorporates this soft distribution as a temporal prior into differentiable cross-stage attention, while gated residual fusion regulates information propagation across production stages. For interpretation, segment-wise Viterbi decoding converts pointwise lag distributions into temporally coherent lag paths, whereas forecasting retains the soft distributions for alignment. On the real-world LiquidSugar dataset, DAMF improves final quality prediction over the compared baselines. Evaluations on two controlled-delay benchmarks show more accurate and stable lag recovery, and ablation studies support the contributions of lag prediction, delay-guided attention, and structured temporal decoding.

</details>


### [168] [MA-UITD: Offline Multi-Agent Reward Inference for Characterizing Continuous Social Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29237)
*Zeyuan Zhang,Yiyun Yang,Yuan Cheng,Herbert Zheng Wu,Yusi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-UITD（多智能体单边逆时序差分学习），一种离线多智能体逆强化学习方法，能从联合演示中推断各智能体的显式奖励函数，无需环境交互、内层策略优化或已知动力学，适用于连续状态、离散或有界连续动作空间。核心创新是基于信念条件后继特征表示单边长期价值，结合共享奖励特征与智能体特定偏好向量，通过逆时序差分一致性和噪声对比行为学习实现推断。在模拟合作/竞争/一般和任务及真实社交觅食数据上均表现优异，能定量刻画领导者与跟随者不同的空间及交互依赖奖励结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体逆强化学习方法多需要额外环境交互、内层策略优化循环，或对环境动力学和动作空间有强假设，难以应用于固定行为数据集。多智能体交互中观察到的选择同时反映个人偏好和对他人的预期，推断奖励具有挑战性。需要一种离线方法，能从联合演示中解耦并推断各智能体特定的奖励函数，且不依赖已知动力学或策略优化内循环。

Method: MA-UITD的核心方法包括：1）信念条件后继特征：表示各智能体自身动作的长期后果，通过对其他智能体行为取平均来边缘化交互影响；2）共享奖励特征与智能体特定偏好向量：将单边价值函数连接到目标奖励函数，实现奖励的显式分解；3）逆时序差分一致性约束：利用贝尔曼方程残差进行奖励推断，无需已知环境动力学；4）噪声对比行为学习：将观察到的联合策略与噪声策略对比，避免内层策略优化循环。整体形成完全离线的推断框架。

Result: 在模拟的合作、竞争和一般和任务中，MA-UITD在离散动作设置下优于基线方法，在连续动作设置下能推断出行为兼容的奖励剖面。应用于实验收集的社交觅食轨迹数据时，成功推断出领导者和跟随者截然不同的空间奖励结构和交互依赖奖励结构，为理解社会行为的神经计算提供了潜在行为目标的定量刻画。

Conclusion: MA-UITD提供了一个有效的离线多智能体逆强化学习框架，克服了现有方法对环境交互、内层优化和强假设的依赖。通过单边后继特征和噪声对比学习，实现了从固定数据集中推断智能体特定奖励函数。该方法在仿真和真实社交行为数据上均表现良好，为量化社会互动中的潜在奖励结构、理解社会决策的神经计算机制提供了新工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent inverse reinforcement learning offers a powerful framework not only for imitation learning but also for understanding the neural computations supporting social interactions and decision-making. Inferring rewards from interacting agents is challenging because observed choices reflect both personal preferences and expectations about others. Existing methods often require additional environment interaction, repeated policy optimization as an inner loop, or restrictive assumptions about environment dynamics and action spaces, limiting their applicability to fixed behavioral datasets. We introduce Multi-Agent Unilateral Inverse Temporal Difference Learning (MA-UITD), an offline method for inferring explicit agent-specific rewards from joint demonstrations in continuous-state environments with either discrete or bounded continuous actions. Its core idea is to represent the long-term consequences of each agent's own actions through belief-conditioned successor features that average over other agents' behavior. Shared reward features and agent-specific preference vectors connect these unilateral values to the target reward function, while inverse temporal-difference consistency and noise-contrastive behavioral learning enable inference without known dynamics or an inner policy-optimization loop. Across simulated cooperative, competitive, and general-sum tasks, MA-UITD outperforms baselines in discrete-action settings and infers behaviorally compatible reward profiles in continuous-action settings. When applied to experimentally collected social-foraging trajectories, MA-UITD inferred distinct spatial and interaction-dependent reward structures for leaders and followers, providing a quantitative characterization of latent behavioral objectives that may be relevant for understanding the neural computations underlying social behavior.

</details>


### [169] [Estimating Performance for Safe Deployment of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29262)
*Zeju Li,Konstantinos Kamnitsas,Mobarakol Islam,Melanie Roschewitz,Cheng Ouyang,Chen Chen,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOVAL 是一种无标签测试数据上的模型评估工具，通过类别级置信度校准和集成方法，解决分布偏移和类别不平衡导致的置信度过度乐观问题，实现准确率、F1、AUC 等指标的最优估计，支持分类、分割等任务的类、实例、像素级性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在真实场景中面临训练与测试数据分布差异（如采集条件变化）的挑战；无标签下评估预训练模型性能对安全至关重要，但深度模型输出的概率过于乐观，且类别不平衡加剧校准失效，导致预测不可靠。

Method: 提出 MOVAL 工具：1) 类别级置信度校准，调整模型输出以估计除准确率外的多种指标，缓解类别不平衡对校准的负面影响；2) 集成方法，融合多个校准后的置信度得分以获得更鲁棒的估计。

Result: 在自然图像和医学图像数据集上，针对各类采集偏移，覆盖分类到分割任务，MOVAL 在准确率、F1-Score、AUC 等评估指标上达到最优；同时能在类别、实例、像素级提供准确的性能估计。

Conclusion: MOVAL 作为部署工具，可在多种域偏移下监控和估计模型性能，保障机器学习模型的透明和安全部署。

Abstract: The main challenge in applying deep neural networks to real-world scenarios lies in distributional discrepancies between training and test datasets, often caused by variations in data acquisition conditions. Assessing the performance of pre-trained models without labels is crucial for safety, but this is complicated by deep models' overly optimistic probabilistic outputs, often exacerbated by class imbalances, leading to unreliable predictions. To address these issues, we introduce MOVAL, a novel MOdel eVALuation tool that estimates performance using unlabeled test data. MOVAL incorporates class-wise confidence calibration to adjust model outputs, allowing for the estimation of metrics beyond accuracy and mitigating the detrimental effects of class imbalance on confidence calibration. Additionally, we propose an ensemble approach that combines multiple calibrated confidence scores for a more robust solution. We validate MOVAL on natural and medical image datasets with various acquisition shifts, covering tasks from classification to segmentation. It achieves state-of-the-art results across evaluation metrics such as accuracy, F1-Score, AUC, and more. Furthermore, we demonstrate that MOVAL provides accurate performance estimations at the class, instance, and pixel levels, ensuring the transparent and safe deployment of machine learning models. We provide MOVAL as a deployment tool for monitoring and estimating performance under a range of domain shifts.

</details>


### [170] [Design and Tuning of Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29276)
*Dominik Jehle,Tom Zehle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯进化优化框架自动发现任务特定的多智能体系统拓扑，结合进化算子和贝叶斯优化，在代码生成和表格预处理任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计有效的多智能体系统（MAS）具有挑战性且资源密集，需要自动发现任务特定的拓扑结构以减少人工设计成本。

Method: 贝叶斯进化优化框架：利用进化算子生成多样化工作流图，结合图感知高斯过程和期望改进（Expected Improvement）进行贝叶斯优化，在昂贵评估预算下为进化搜索提供显式选择准则。

Result: 在BigCodeBench代码生成基准上，进化架构在所有评估的智能体系统中达到最高整体性能；在时序表格数据集上，优化的MAS预处理在多个任务上优于默认TabPFN模型，在IID数据集上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了贝叶斯选择在昂贵评估预算约束下指导进化多智能体系统搜索的有效性和潜力。

Abstract: Designing effective multi-agent systems (MAS) remains a challenging and resource-intensive task. We propose a Bayesian Evolutionary Optimization framework for automatically discovering task-specific MAS topologies. The approach combines evolutionary operators to generate diverse workflow graphs with Bayesian optimization to focus cost intensive evaluations on promising candidates. In contrast to LLM-based candidate selection, our approach uses a graph-aware Gaussian Process and Expected Improvement to provide an explicit selection criterion during evolutionary search. We evaluate the method on BigCodeBench for code generation and on a tabular preprocessing benchmark using TabPFN. On BigCodeBench, evolved architectures achieve the highest overall performance among the evaluated agent systems. On temporal tabular datasets, optimized MAS preprocessing improves over the default TabPFN model on several tasks, while remaining competitive on IID datasets. Overall, the results demonstrate the potential of Bayesian selection for guiding evolutionary MAS search under expensive evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [171] [Flat-RLVR: Sharpness-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Reasoning Capacity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29281)
*Yeonbum Kim,Hyun Kyu Lee,Sung Whan Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Flat-RLVR 方法，通过在 LLM 后训练中引入锐度感知最小化寻找平坦奖励区域，缓解 RLVR 中的分布尖锐化问题，提升采样多样性同时保持单次尝试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（特别是 GRPO）虽能显著提升 LLM 推理能力，但会导致分布尖锐化，使策略分布过度集中，抑制罕见但正确的轨迹，降低样本多样性。

Method: 提出 Flat-RLVR，结合锐度感知最小化（SAM）在 RLVR 后训练中搜索平坦奖励区域，与 GRPO 变体结合，平衡采样效率与推理能力。

Result: 理论证明平坦奖励 RLVR 可减少分布尖锐化，实验显示样本多样性提升且单次尝试准确率保持不变。

Conclusion: Flat-RLVR 通过平坦奖励优化有效缓解分布尖锐化，为 LLM 后训练提供更优的采样效率与推理能力权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly through group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), has emerged as a central paradigm for the post-training of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is highly effective at enhancing the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, it commonly induces \textit{distribution sharpening}, whereby the policy distribution becomes excessively peaked and rare-yet-correct trajectories are progressively suppressed, thus diminishing sample diversity. To this end, we promote flat-reward optima in RLVR to smooth the policy distribution. We theoretically link flat reward RLVR to reduced distribution sharpening, and empirically show improved sample diversity while maintaining single-attempt accuracy. Our method, called \texttt{Flat}-RLVR, searches for flat reward regions via sharpness-aware minimization during LLM post-training, combining this with GRPO variants to achieve better trade-offs between sampling efficiency and reasoning capacity.

</details>
