### [172] [Approximate Solomonoff Induction: On the Robustness of Learned Universal Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29289)
*Qian Li,Shang-Hua Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在log-loss最小化训练下，大语言模型近似贝叶斯混合预测器时的鲁棒性。证明了近似预测器ρ在KL散度约束下，其最坏情况下的额外预测误差为Θ(η/w_μ)，在先验概率小的环境中性能显著下降；但在先验分布平均下，额外误差仅为O(η)。并建立了分布偁移定理，将平均情况与最坏情况保证插值，最后扩展到有限采样设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过海量多样数据上的log-loss最小化训练，展现出广泛能力和泛化技能，但其理论解释尚不完全。Grau-Moya等人从元学习角度给出概念解释：最小化总体log-loss可恢复贝叶斯混合预测器ξ，理想情况下对应Solomonoff普遍归纳。然而实际算法只能产生近似最优预测器，因此核心问题是：预测性能在近似下是否鲁棒？

Method: 理论分析方法：1) 建立近似预测器ρ与贝叶斯混合预测器ξ之间的KL散度约束D_KL(ξ||ρ) ≤ η；2) 推导累积期望平方预测误差的最坏情况界；3) 对比ξ在环境μ中的误差O(log(1/w_μ))与ρ的超额误差；4) 证明先验平均下的超额误差界；5) 建立从训练先验w到测试先验w'的受控分布偁移定理；6) 扩展到有限采样设置，处理D_KL可能无界的情况。

Result: 主要结果：1) 任意满足D_KL(ξ||ρ) ≤ η的预测器ρ具有有界累积期望平方预测误差；2) ξ在环境μ中的误差为O(log(1/w_μ))，ρ相对于ξ的超额误差紧界为Θ(η/w_μ)，表明小先验概率环境下近似会显著降低性能；3) 先验平均下超额误差仅为O(η)；4) 分布偁移定理插值了平均情况和最坏情况保证；5) 有限采样扩展适用于神经网络和Transformer学习通用预测器的场景。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了近似贝叶斯混合预测器时的性能权衡：虽然先验平均下近似鲁棒（O(η)），但在低先验概率环境中最坏情况退化严重（Θ(η/w_μ)）。分布偁移定理提供了从训练分布到测试分布的平滑过渡保证。有限采样分析为实际神经网络训练提供了理论支撑。这些结果加深了对大语言模型泛化能力及其局限性的理论理解。

Abstract: Why large language models trained via log-loss minimization over massive and diverse datasets exhibit broad capabilities and generalizable skills is not yet fully understood. Grau-Moya et al. (ICML 2024) offer a conceptual explanation through the lens of meta-learning: if sequence-generating environments $ν$ are drawn from a prior $w_ν$, minimizing population log-loss recovers the corresponding Bayesian mixture predictor $ξ=\sum_νw_νν$. In the idealized limit in which $w$ is a universal prior, the optimal predictor corresponds to Solomonoff universal induction. Practical machine learning algorithms, however, generally produce only \emph{approximately} optimal predictors. This raises a natural question: \emph{Does the prediction performance remain robust under approximation}? In this paper, we first provide a tight bound on the worst-case effect of approximation on prediction error. We show any predictor $ρ$ satisfying $D_{KL}(ξ\|ρ)\leq η$ retains bounded cumulative-expected-squared prediction error. However, while the Bayesian mixture predictor $ξ$ has error $O(\log(1/w_μ))$ in an environment $μ$, we establish a tight $Θ(η/w_μ)$ bound on the excess error of $ρ$ relative to $ξ$. Thus, approximation can substantially degrade performance in environments assigned small prior probability. In sharp contrast, when environments are averaged according to prior $w$, we prove that the excess prediction error is only $O(η)$. Furthermore, we establish a distribution-shift theorem for dominated shifts from a training prior $w$ to a test prior $w'$, interpolating our average-case and worst-case guarantees. Finally, we extend our robustness analysis to a finite-sampling setting relevant to learning universal predictors with neural networks and Transformers, where $D_{KL}(ξ\|ρ)$ could be unbounded. [For the full abstract, see the paper.]

</details>


### [173] [ExpEvolve: Structuring and Reusing Experience for Open-Ended Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29303)
*Yongqiang Chen,Chenxi Liu,Deyu Zou,Zhenhao Chen,Tongliang Liu,Bo Han,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExpEvolve 是一种进化编码代理，通过引入想法层面的提取与控制，显著提升了大语言模型在开放式科学发现任务中的样本效率与最终性能，最高达 5.0 倍提升，并具备跨模型与跨任务的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的进化编码代理（如 AlphaEvolve）主要基于表面启发式进行低层级的程序变异，缺乏持久的控制单元；科学发现依赖于思想的积累，因此需要引入高层级的想法因子来解释程序间的得分差异，从而指导更高效的探索与利用。

Method: 提出 ExpEvolve 框架，包含想法层面的提取与控制机制：从程序中发现并利用解释得分差异的高层级因子；理论表征了想法因子发现改善样本效率的条件；构建实用框架，在经验利用与发现停滞时的探索中引导进化过程。

Result: 在 11 个开放式数学任务上，ExpEvolve 显著提升最终性能与样本效率（最高 5.0 倍）；改进可迁移至 4 种其他 LLMs 及 MLS-Bench 上的 6 个机器学习任务；分析表明其能发现想法层面因子，提供更好的探索与利用。

Conclusion: ExpEvolve 通过引入想法层面的提取与控制，解决了进化编码代理中低层级变异的局限，实现了更高效的科学发现过程，具有广泛的泛化能力与实用价值。

Abstract: Evolutionary coding agents such as AlphaEvolve are one of the notable successes in using Large Language Models (LLMs) to build AI Scientists. These agents tackle open-ended scientific problems by iteratively improving and evolving programs, leveraging the prior knowledge and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Despite their success, mutations over programs are mostly low-level upon surface heuristics, without a persistent unit of control. In contrast, science compounds through ideas. In this work, we propose ExpEvolve, which injects idea-level extraction and control into evolution: it discovers and leverages the high-level factors from programs that explain scoring differences among different programs. We characterize theoretical conditions on when discovery with idea factors improves sample efficiency. We also build a practical framework to show that the extracted high-level idea factors are helpful for guiding exploitation from experience and exploration when discovery comes to a plateau. Across 11 open-ended mathematical tasks, ExpEvolve significantly improves the final performance and sample efficiency up to 5.0x. The improvements also transfer to 4 other LLMs and to 6 machine learning tasks from MLS-Bench. Analysis shows that ExpEvolve can discover idea-level factors to provide better exploration and exploitation for evolutionary discovery.

</details>


### [174] [Interpretable Cutting Plane Selection for Mixed-Integer Linear Programming Using Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29311)
*Gwen maudet,Mathis Da Cunha,Grégoire Danoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GPSCS（基于遗传规划与统计的切割平面选择框架），首个可解释的学习型切割选择策略，结合符号评分函数与自适应切割数量决定机制，在 SCIP 求解器中显著提升求解速度并展现强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MILP 求解器依赖静态手工设计的切割策略，虽有效但非最优；基于神经网络的学习方法缺乏可解释性且无收敛保证。需开发可解释、自适应且具泛化能力的切割选择框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 遗传规划进化符号评分函数，利用切割与求解上下文特征对候选切割排序；2) 轻量级统计机制检测评分显著下降点，自适应决定每轮分离添加的顶部切割数量。

Result: 在七类合成与真实基准实例上（评估实例平均比训练实例难 41 倍），GPSCS 相比 SCIP 默认策略平均求解时间提升 6%，比神经网络方法和无切割基线实现两倍加速，表现出强鲁棒性与泛化性。

Conclusion: GPSCS 是首个将手工启发式显式泛化为可解释策略的学习型框架，兼具可解释性、自适应性与强泛化能力，为 MILP 求解器切割选择提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cutting planes (cuts) remove fractional solutions from mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) without excluding any feasible integer solutions, making them a key ingredient in modern MILP solvers. Current state-of-the-art solvers rely on fine-tuned handcrafted cut strategies, which, despite their effectiveness, remain inherently static and can be suboptimal. More recently, learning-based approaches have been proposed to design adaptive cut strategies; however, most of these methods are based on neural networks, offering limited interpretability and providing no guarantees of convergence. In this paper, we propose \emph{Genetic Programming and Statistics-based Cut Selection} (GPSCS), the first learning-based framework that explicitly generalizes handcrafted cut selection heuristics into a fully interpretable policy. First, a genetic programming model evolves a symbolic scoring function that ranks candidate cuts using features derived from the cuts and the solving context. Second, a lightweight statistics-based mechanism adaptively determines the number of top-ranked cuts to add at each separation round by detecting significant score dropouts. We evaluate GPSCS within the SCIP solver on seven classes of synthetic and real-world benchmark instances, with a strong emphasis on generalization: evaluation instances are on average 41 times harder than those used for training. Experimental results show that GPSCS improves average solving time by $6\%$ compared to SCIP's default cut selection strategy and achieves a two-fold speedup over both a recent neural-network-based approach and a no-cut baseline, demonstrating strong robustness and generalization across heterogeneous MILP families.

</details>


### [175] [YingLan: A Multimodal Ocean Foundation Model for Unified Representation Learning from Heterogeneous Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29313)
*Sen Jia,Siqi Wei,Junyu Wang,Zijun Lv,Pan Liu,Meng Xu,Xiyou Fu,Yuxi Sun,Qiang Liu,Shuyu Zhang,Lin Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了YingLan多模态海洋基础模型，通过物理属性条件动态编码、时空增量耦合（STIC）和海洋状态演化感知预训练，整合卫星图像、网格海洋场和浮标观测等异构数据，在深圳近海7天海表盐度预测任务中达到0.687508 PSU的RMSE，优于LangYa、XiHe和GloNet2基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋建模方法主要针对特定任务，难以从卫星图像、网格海洋场和浮标观测等异构数据中学习可迁移的表示。这些数据在结构、物理语义和时空特征上存在显著差异，导致多模态海洋建模极具挑战性。

Method: YingLan包含三大核心组件：1) 物理属性条件动态编码机制，根据物理语义和观测特征生成自适应输入投影，灵活表示异构海洋变量；2) 时空增量耦合（STIC），通过潜在时空增量的跨尺度交互建模多尺度时空依赖；3) 海洋状态演化感知预训练，结合掩码观测重构与时序演化目标，学习跨预测时域的连贯海洋状态表示。模型基于MindSpore在华为昇腾910B NPU上训练。

Result: 在深圳近海7天海表盐度（SSS）预测任务中，YingLan实现平均RMSE 0.687508 PSU，相较于LangYa、XiHe和GloNet2分别降低相对误差0.58%、5.31%和12.42%。

Conclusion: 结果初步证明了模型在区域海洋预报中的有效性，但仍需更广泛的跨任务和跨区域评估以验证其泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal ocean modeling remains challenging due to substantial heterogeneity in data structures, physical semantics, and spatiotemporal characteristics across satellite imagery, gridded oceanographic fields, and buoy observations. Existing approaches largely focus on task-specific ocean modeling, while learning transferable representations from heterogeneous observations remains an open problem. To address this challenge, we propose YingLan, a multimodal ocean foundation model that integrates physics-attribute-conditioned dynamic encoding, Spatiotemporal Increment Coupling (STIC), and ocean-state evolution-aware pretraining. Specifically, the dynamic encoding mechanism generates adaptive input projections conditioned on physical semantics and observation characteristics, enabling flexible representation of heterogeneous ocean variables. STIC models multiscale spatiotemporal dependencies through cross-scale interactions of latent temporal increments. The pretraining strategy further combines masked observation reconstruction with temporal evolution objectives to facilitate the learning of coherent ocean-state representations across different prediction horizons. YingLan is implemented using MindSpore and trained on Huawei Ascend 910B NPUs, demonstrating its compatibility with domestic AI computing infrastructure. In a seven-day sea surface salinity (SSS) forecasting task conducted in the Shenzhen coastal waters, YingLan achieves an average RMSE of 0.687508 PSU, corresponding to relative error reductions of 0.58%, 5.31%, and 12.42% compared with LangYa, XiHe, and GloNet2, respectively. These results provide initial evidence of the model's effectiveness in regional ocean forecasting, while broader cross-task and cross-regional evaluations remain necessary to assess its generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [176] [DISRL: Dual-Scale Incremental Semi-Supervised Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Community Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29315)
*Tianming Zhang,Jinxu Wang,Yixuan Zhang,Zhengyi Yang,Chenyu Hou,Bin Cao,Jing Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DISRL框架，通过双尺度增量半监督强化学习解决动态局部社区检测中的时序监督不确定性和误差级联问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态局部社区检测面临两大挑战：时序监督不确定性（稀疏过时标签在拓扑变化下无法提供种子特定指导）和无界误差级联（顺序策略迁移在长时程累积探索误差）。

Method: DISRL包含两个核心模块：1）监督构建模块，从稀疏已知社区动态生成种子特定指导；2）双尺度社区探索模块，将短期时序窗口内的适应与跨窗口的长期参数固解耦。同时构建了包含多样社区演化场景的动态图基准。

Result: 大量实验表明，DISRL在各种动态设置下持续超越现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: DISRL有效解决了动态局部社区检测中的核心挑战，为时序图上的种子社区跟踪提供了鲁棒的增量学习方案。

Abstract: Dynamic local community detection tracks the evolving community of designated seed nodes across graph snapshots. However, it faces two critical challenges: temporal supervision uncertainty, where sparse and outdated labels fail to provide seed-specific guidance under topology changes, and unbounded error cascades, where sequential policy transfer accumulates exploration errors over long horizons. To address these challenges, we propose DISRL, a Dual-scale Incremental Semi-supervised Reinforcement Learning framework. DISRL introduces a supervision construction module that dynamically generates seed-specific guidance from sparse known communities, and a dual-scale community exploration module that stabilizes policy evolution by decoupling short-term adaptation within temporal windows from long-term parameter consolidation across windows. Furthermore, we construct a dynamic graph benchmark with diverse community evolution scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DISRL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under various dynamic settings.

</details>


### [177] [BrainCodes: A Lightweight EEG Foundation Model over Discrete Neural Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29320)
*Kleanthis Avramidis,Ivan Tashev,Dimitra Emmanouilidou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出BrainCodes，一种轻量级EEG基础模型，通过自监督学习在800+小时、1000+参与者的数据上预训练，结合量化神经分词器和时空编码器，仅用3%参数量即达SOTA性能，并有效迁移至可穿戴设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型过度强调模型规模，导致难以在资源受限设备和日常环境部署；作者主张可通过紧凑架构学习可迁移的EEG特征。

Method: 架构包含量化神经分词器和支持任意电极配置的时空编码器；预训练目标显式鼓励通过通道位置编码捕捉空间连接性，并通过辅助训练目标增强对窄带神经振荡的敏感性。

Result: 在睡眠分期、注意力评估、认知负荷估计等多样神经生理监测任务上达到或超越SOTA；迁移至低资源可穿戴设备时，可训练参数比同类架构减少97%；评估框架还刻画了学习表示在空间、时间、频谱信号属性上的几何不变性。

Conclusion: BrainCodes证明了紧凑架构结合精心设计的自监督目标能学习高质量、可迁移的EEG表示，为资源受限场景下的脑机接口和神经监测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Recent electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models increasingly emphasize model scale, potentially limiting their deployment on resource-constrained devices and in everyday environments. We argue that transferable EEG features can instead be learned with a compact architecture and introduce BrainCodes, a lightweight foundation model pretrained through self-supervised learning on more than 800 hours of recordings from over 1,000 participants. Our architecture combines a quantization-based neural tokenizer with a spatiotemporal encoder that supports arbitrary electrode configurations. The pretraining objective explicitly encourages representations that capture spatial connectivity through channel positional encoding, and sensitivity to narrowband neural oscillations through an auxiliary training objective. Our model matches or surpasses the state of the art across diverse neurophysiological monitoring tasks, including sleep staging, attention assessment, and cognitive load estimation. BrainCodes transfers effectively to low-resource wearable devices while using up to 97% fewer trainable parameters than comparable architectures. Beyond predictive accuracy, our evaluation framework characterizes the geometry and invariances of learned EEG representations across measurable spatial, temporal, and spectral signal properties.

</details>


### [178] [Reader--Writer Coupling in Transformer MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29340)
*Giyeong Oh,Sunghee Ahn,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer MLP层中隐藏通道的“读者-写者”双重角色，通过分析雅可比矩阵与其转置的一致性，发现训练过程中形成了一个小规模的强耦合核心通道，这些通道的写者方向与读者响应方向相反，且这种对立模式在训练早期确立并持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer MLP层内部隐藏通道如何通过读者（决定何时响应）和写者（决定写回什么）角色协同工作，以及训练如何耦合这两个因子集，揭示模型内部的信息流动机制。

Method: 将MLP层视为键值记忆，利用其写回残差流的特性，计算更新的精确雅可比矩阵（方阵），分析雅可比矩阵与其转置的一致性，分解为读者-写者项，通过内积得到每个通道的符号配对坐标，据此排序并识别强耦合核心通道。

Result: 各层均存在少量强耦合的核心通道，初始化时符号随机，但核心通道几乎全为对立：写者指向与读者响应方向相反。这种对立核心从小模型到大模型检查点普遍存在，在训练前几百分比步骤内确立并随耦合增长保持稳定。大多数通道将贡献返回给触发它的读者，后续层的读者接收；极少直接影响logits。翻转对立写者会使输出向常见token偏移，将写者置换到另一读者上会重新生长以适应该读者，而读者保持不变。

Conclusion: Transformer MLP层通过读者-写者对的对立耦合实现高效的信息处理与传递，训练早期固定这种结构并维持，展现了模型内部自我组织的稳健机制。

Abstract: A transformer MLP gives each hidden channel two roles: a reader that decides when the channel responds, and a writer that decides what it adds back to the residual stream. Read as a key--value memory, the layer can be measured one factor set at a time: the pairing of readers to writers is carried only by their shared index, invisible to any quantity computed from one factor set alone. Yet training couples the two factors through their joint update, so we ask what reading them together shows. The block supplies the joint instrument: the MLP writes back into the very stream it reads, so the exact Jacobian of its update is square, and its agreement with its own transpose resolves into reader--writer terms whose inner products give each channel, read as a couple, a signed pairing coordinate. Ranked by this coordinate, each block we measure has a small core of strongly coupled channels; where initialization gives either sign equally, the core is nearly all opposed: each writer points against the direction its reader responds to. This opposed core recurs from the smallest models trained from scratch to released checkpoints; its sign is in place within a few percent of training and holds while the coupling grows. Read as couples, most channels return their contribution against the reader that fired it, and the readers of later blocks take it up; few show in the logits. Training holds the couples in place: flipping the opposed writers moves the output toward common tokens, and a writer permuted onto another reader regrows to fit that reader while the reader stays.

</details>


### [179] [Benchmarking and Error Analysis of Deep Search and Deep Research LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29346)
*Zixuan Yu,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型（LLM）驱动的深度搜索与深度研究代理的中间执行过程中的错误进行系统诊断，建立了系统级错误与任务级错误的分类体系，并通过实证分析提出了增强代理鲁棒性、推理能力和可靠性的设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估范式主要关注最终任务结果，忽视了代理在长周期、多步骤执行过程中极易出错的中间轨迹，导致对代理可靠性的理解存在显著缺口。

Method: 提出一套综合方法论与分析框架，用于诊断中间工作流错误；建立结构化分类体系，将漏洞划分为系统级错误与任务级错误两大维度；通过系统检查逐步执行轨迹与代理-环境交互，揭示这些错误对整体性能的影响。

Result: 实证实验与系统分析识别出的错误类型表明，中间过程错误显著降低代理整体性能；基于此给出了可落地的设计指南，旨在提升深度搜索与深度研究代理的鲁棒性、推理能力与可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦中间执行轨迹的错误诊断与分类，本工作为构建更可靠的自主深度搜索和深度研究代理提供了理论基础与实践指导。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous Deep Search and Deep Research Agents capable of executing long-horizon, multi-step tasks, evaluating their reliability becomes increasingly critical. However, existing evaluation paradigms predominantly focus on final task outcomes, leaving a significant gap in understanding the intermediate execution traces, where agents are highly susceptible to various errors. To address this critical gap, we introduce a comprehensive methodology and analytical framework designed specifically to diagnose errors in intermediate workflows. Building upon this framework, we establish a structured taxonomy that categorizes these vulnerabilities into two primary dimensions: System-Level Errors and Task-Level Errors. Through a systematic examination of step-by-step execution traces and agent-environment interactions, we reveal how these underlying errors affect overall performance. Finally, through empirical experiments and systematic analysis of the identified error types, we provide actionable design guidelines, aiming to enhance the robustness, reasoning capabilities, and reliability of Deep Search and Deep Research agents.

</details>


### [180] [Data Schedules and Learning Rate Schedules Should Be Designed Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29348)
*Haodong Wen,Kairong Luo,Tingkai Yan,Wenguang Chen,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文探讨了中间训练（MT）数据引入时机与学习率衰减策略的联合优化，发现最优数据混合调度取决于学习率调度：若学习率衰减，提前混入MT数据更优；若学习率恒定，则将MT数据置于训练末尾更优。实验表明应在MT数据引入后再衰减学习率，并给出了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型训练流水线中，中间训练（MT）通常在预训练（PT）之后、使用更高价值的小数据集进行。常见做法是先完全使用PT数据，再完全切换到MT数据。本文将这种完全切换视为数据混合调度的特例，研究在固定总计算量和MT token预算下，是否完全切换到MT数据最优，以及数据引入时机与学习率调度的相互作用。

Method: 通过消融实验研究MT数据引入时机与学习率衰减时机的联合选择，对比不同数据混合调度（如提前混入、完全切换）在不同学习率调度（衰减 vs 恒定）下的最终MT损失表现。并构建理论模型解释最优数据调度对学习率调度的依赖性。

Result: 当学习率在持续训练中衰减时，提前引入MT数据并与PT数据混合可降低最终MT损失；若使用恒定学习率，则将所有MT数据置于训练末尾更优。最佳策略是仅在MT数据进入训练后才衰减学习率。

Conclusion: 数据混合调度的最优性取决于学习率调度，两者需联合优化。理论模型揭示了这一现象的机制：学习率衰减改变了梯度更新的有效权重，使得早期混入高价值MT数据能更好地利用有限MT token预算。实践中应在MT数据引入后再衰减学习率。

Abstract: Mid-training (MT) is a training phase in LLM training pipelines that continues pretraining (PT) on smaller datasets of higher value. Taking the PT and MT phases as a whole, training usually starts with only PT data and then transitions completely to MT data. In this work, we view this complete transition from PT to MT data as a special case of a broader class of data mixing schedules that specify how the proportions of PT and MT tokens in each batch change over training. Under fixed total training compute and MT token budgets, we ask: Is this complete transition to MT data optimal for minimizing final MT loss? We show that the answer depends on the learning rate (LR) schedule: one data schedule may outperform another under one LR schedule, but changing only the LR schedule can reverse the outcome. Specifically, when the LR decays throughout continued training, introducing MT data earlier and mixing it with PT data lowers the final MT loss compared with placing all MT data at the end of training, but this result is reversed if we use constant LR. We therefore study how to jointly choose when to introduce MT data and when to decay the LR through ablation studies, and find that it is better to decay the LR only after MT data enters training. Finally, we develop a theoretical model that explains why the optimal data schedule for a fixed MT token budget depends on the LR schedule.

</details>


### [181] [GaP-MoE : Explicit Expert Specialization in Mixture-of-Experts via Orthogonal Gradient Accumulation and Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29355)
*Donghyun Jeong,Yoon-Yeong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GaP-MoE，通过梯度投影直接诱导专家专业化，解决稀疏混合专家模型中的表示崩塌问题，无需修改损失函数，在微调和预训练任务上达到或优于标准SMoE性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家模型虽提升可扩展性，但常遭遇表示崩塌：多个专家收敛至相似功能，丧失多样性。现有方法通过改进路由器或引入辅助损失隐式惩罚专家相似性，但路由器方法仍易发生路由崩塌，损失方法可能与任务目标冲突。

Method: GaP-MoE在每个训练步骤中，基于归一化路由分数从TopK专家中随机选取一个领导者，将其余追随者专家的梯度投影到领导者近期梯度张成子空间的正交补上，消除专家梯度的方向重叠，不改变任务损失，使专业化作为优化动力学的自然结果涌现。

Result: 在BERT-based微调(GLUE)和GPT-2-based预训练(WikiText-103, enwik8, text8)上评估，GaP-MoE性能与标准SMoE相当或更优，且可作为即插即用模块集成到现有SMoE架构中。

Conclusion: GaP-MoE通过梯度操作而非损失修改实现专家专业化，有效缓解表示崩塌，避免了路由崩塌和优化冲突，为SMoE提供了兼容性强、理论清晰的改进方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) models improve scalability by routing input tokens to only a subset of expert networks, but they often suffer from representation collapse, in which multiple experts converge toward similar functions and lose their intended diversity. Prior work has addressed this limitation either by improving router architectures or by introducing auxiliary loss terms that implicitly penalize similarity among experts. However, router-based approaches remain vulnerable to routing collapses, and loss-based approaches can conflict with the task's original objective during optimization. In this work, we propose GaP-MoE (Gradient Accumulation and Projection in MoE), which induces expert specialization by directly manipulating expert output gradients rather than modifying the loss function. At each training step, GaP-MoE stochastically selects one of the $TopK$ routed experts as a leader based on normalized routing scores, and projects the gradients of the remaining follower experts onto the orthogonal complement of the subspace spanned by the leader's recent gradients. This removes directional overlap between expert gradients without altering the task loss, allowing specialization to emerge as a direct consequence of the optimization dynamics rather than an externally imposed constraint. We evaluate GaP-MoE in both fine-tuning settings (BERT-based, on GLUE) and from-scratch pretraining settings (GPT-2-based, on WikiText-103, enwik8, and text8), and further confirm its compatibility as a drop-in module for existing SMoE architectures. Experimental results show that GaP-MoE achieves performance comparable to or better than standard SMoE.

</details>


### [182] [Compositional neural networks for multimodal X-ray and neutron scattering inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29362)
*Valentin Munteanu,Alexander Hinderhofer,Dmitry Lapkin,Vladimir Starostin,Frank Schreiber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于先验摊销流匹配的模块化框架，用于概率性多测量分析，通过单独训练单观测后验估计器并在推理时通过组合采样器进行晚融合，实现了不确定性感知的反演和协同精化，并以反射率为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线和中子散射实验通常只记录强度而缺乏相位信息，导致逆问题非唯一；结合互补测量可减少歧义，但将其集成到机器学习框架中仍具挑战。

Method: 基于先验摊销流匹配的模块化框架，单独训练针对不同观测类型（X射线、飞行时间中子、极化中子反射率）的单观测后验估计器，推理时通过组合采样器进行晚融合，无需联合重新训练即可复用预训练模型。

Result: 在反射率案例中，单观测演示覆盖了X射线、飞行时间中子和极化中子反射率；协同精化研究中，任务匹配估计器实现了多中子对比度的同构组合以及X射线和中子测量的异构多模态组合。

Conclusion: 组合式流匹配提供了一种模块化的不确定性感知反演和协同精化方法，适用于多观测约束共同物理结构同时保留测量特定参数的其他散射问题。

Abstract: X-ray and neutron scattering are indispensable for structural characterization across condensed-matter physics and materials science. Yet, because scattering experiments generally record intensities without phase information, distinct structures can yield similar scattering patterns, rendering the corresponding inverse problems non-unique. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining complementary measurements, but their integration into machine-learning frameworks remains challenging. Here we introduce a modular framework for probabilistic multi-measurement analysis based on prior-amortized flow matching. Single-observation posterior estimators are trained separately for individual observation types or physical probes and combined only at inference time through a compositional sampler. This late fusion approach allows pretrained single-observation models to be reused without dedicated joint retraining. As a case in point, we consider reflectometry, which provides depth-resolved, nanometer-scale information about thin films and interfaces. Our single-observation demonstrations span X-ray, time-of-flight neutron and polarized neutron reflectometry. In separate co-refinement studies, task-matched estimators enable homogeneous composition across multiple neutron contrasts and heterogeneous multimodal composition of X-ray and neutron measurements. Together, these results establish compositional flow matching as a modular approach to uncertainty-aware inversion and co-refinement, with potential applicability to other scattering problems in which multiple observations constrain a common physical structure while retaining measurement-specific parameters.

</details>


### [183] [Cascading Bandits with Noisy Observations: Algorithms and Regret Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29381)
*Yashaswini Kaluguri,Nikhil Karamchandani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的随机级联老虎机设置，其中探测有序短名单涉及位置相关成本且仅能观测到带噪反馈。研究识别了噪声模型的一个子类，在此子类中最优短名单可通过单臂得分排序获得；针对得分存在和不存在两种情况，分别设计了基于UCB的算法和DP-UCB算法，并给出了相应的遗憾界，最后通过实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有级联老虎机模型通常假设观测无噪或成本固定，而实际应用中探测臂往往伴随位置相关成本且反馈存在噪声。需要研究在同时存在噪声和成本的情况下，如何高效学习最优短名单的选择与排序策略。

Method: 1. 分析噪声模型结构，确定最优短名单可分解为单臂得分排序的条件（无假阳性时选择与排序均可分解，无成本时仅排序可分解）。2. 针对得分存在的情况，提出基于UCB的算法，利用上置信界估计单臂得分并按得分排序。3. 针对得分不存在的一般情况，提出DP-UCB算法模板，将乐观原则嵌入精确动态规划中求解最优短名单。

Result: 1. 得分存在时，UCB算法实现 O((L-K) log T) 实例相关遗憾，匹配算法无关下界（常数因子内）。2. 得分不存在时，DP-UCB算法实现 O(LK log T) 遗憾。3. 在合成及真实数据集上的大量数值实验验证了理论遗憾界和算法有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了带位置成本和噪声反馈的级联老虎机框架，刻画了最优短名单可分解性的条件，并针对不同设计了最优或近最优算法，填补了同时存在噪声与成本时的理论空白，为推荐系统、搜索排序等实际场景提供了有原则的学习方法。

Abstract: We propose a novel stochastic cascading bandit setup where probing an ordered shortlist of $K$ out of $L$ arms incurs position-dependent costs, and the learner observes only noisy feedback regarding each arm's true output. We identify a sub-class of noise models for which the optimal shortlist can be found by sorting arms according to a per-arm score: such a score exists for both selection and ordering when the channel has no false positives, and for ordering alone (but not selection) when measurements are costless; however in general neither selection nor ordering is separable once noise and cost are both present. For the case where both selection and ordering scores exist, we provide a UCB-based algorithm with $O((L-K)\log T)$ instance-dependent regret over a time horizon $T$, which matches an algorithm-agnostic lower bound (up to constant factors). For the more challenging scenario where scores don't exist, we develop a new algorithmic template, DP-UCB, that embeds optimism directly inside an exact dynamic program, yielding $O\big(LK\log T\big)$ regret. Finally, we validate our theoretical results through extensive numerical evaluations and simulations over both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [184] [Parallelizable Probabilistic Latent Reasoning for Diverse Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29385)
*Seokhyun Lee,Seongjun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概率潜在推理模型 (PLRM)，一种可并行化的变分框架，用于在紧凑的连续潜在空间中进行概率推理。PLRM 采用 JEPA 风格目标，后验并行推断潜在分布，先验通过教师强制预测后续潜在分布，并引入离散策略变量与混合先验，保留多轨迹推理的高斯混合结构，避免坍缩为单一高斯。实验显示 PLRM 在单轨迹数据集上与确定性潜在推理模型性能相当且训练高效，在多轨迹数据集上持续超越随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言空间逐 Token 生成的 Chain-of-Thought (CoT) 模型，推理轨迹与表面语言变化耦合，难以高效表示多样化推理路径。当前潜在推理模型多为确定性，仅捕捉单一轨迹；随机潜在模型虽建模分布，但优化不稳定、性能有限，且依赖自回归潜在预测无法并行训练，导致训练时间过长。需要一种可并行、稳定且能建模多模态推理分布的潜在推理框架。

Method: PLRM 采用 JEPA（Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture）风格的变分目标：后验编码器从观测到的推理轨迹并行推断每一步的潜在分布；先验网络通过教师强制学习预测下一步的潜在分布。为建模异构推理过程，引入离散策略变量和混合先验（Gaussian Mixture Prior），显式保留多条不同推理轨迹诱导的高斯混合结构，防止模式坍缩。训练阶段完全并行化，推理时可采样多条潜在轨迹。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，PLRM 在单轨迹数据集上达到与确定性潜在推理模型相当的性能，同时训练效率显著提升；在多轨迹数据集上，PLRM 持续优于现有的随机潜在推理基线，展示了对多模态推理分布的有效建模能力。

Conclusion: PLRM 通过并行化变分框架、JEPA 风格目标及混合先验机制，成功解决了潜在推理模型在确定性建模、优化稳定性、训练并行化和多模态分布捕捉方面的瓶颈，为高效、灵活的概率潜在推理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Language models relying on verbalized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) are constrained to token-by-token generation in the language space, where many tokens serve textual coherence rather than core reasoning. This entangles genuine differences in logical trajectories with superficial variations in verbalization. Moving reasoning into a compact, continuous latent space offers a promising alternative for efficiently representing and exploring diverse reasoning trajectories. However, current latent models remain largely deterministic, capturing only a single reasoning trajectory rather than modeling a distribution over multiple valid alternatives. While stochastic approaches parameterize distributions over latent reasoning steps, they frequently suffer from unstable optimization and limited empirical performance. Furthermore, their reliance on autoregressive latent prediction during training precludes effective parallelization, leading to prohibitively long training times. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose Probabilistic Latent Reasoning Models (PLRM), a parallelizable variational framework for probabilistic latent reasoning. PLRM adopts a JEPA-style objective in which the posterior infers latent distributions in parallel from observed reasoning trajectories, while the prior learns to predict the subsequent latent distribution via teacher forcing. To model heterogeneous reasoning processes, we introduce a discrete strategy variable together with a mixture-based prior, allowing the model to preserve the Gaussian-mixture structure induced by multiple distinct reasoning trajectories rather than collapsing them into a single Gaussian. Across diverse benchmarks, PLRM achieves performance competitive with deterministic latent reasoning models on single-trajectory datasets while enabling efficient training and consistently outperforming competing stochastic baselines on multi-trajectory datasets.

</details>


### [185] [Nominate-then-Adjudicate: LLM-Assisted One-Pass and Per-Instance MIP Solver Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29391)
*Yifan Zhao,Qianyang Zhao,Xinyu Guan,Kai Wei,Yuming Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对混合整数规划（MIP）求解器参数配置问题，提出一种“提名-裁决”流水线：一次性采样固定参数组合库，配合基础模型单次调用、无需求解器调用即可完成实例级配置选择，在多个基准上显著优于默认配置和现有实例级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIP求解器暴露数百至数千个参数，最佳设置随实例而异，但逐实例搜索成本极高；现有方法要么依赖昂贵的逐实例配置，要么因特征表示局限无法区分结构不同但特征相似的实例。

Method: 1. 离线一次性采样固定参数组合库（portfolio），推理时复用，消除逐实例搜索。2. 使用固定20维特征表示实例。3. 引入基础模型作为裁决器，无需任务特定权重更新，单次模型调用即完成配置选择，零求解器调用。

Result: 在超过20%实际差异阈值的对比中，完整裁决器对最优提名者记录538胜185负；对默认配置胜率从84.9%提升至90.2%；在匹配的单次通过协议下，领先于聚类级方法及最强逐实例基线ISAC+。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结构多样性（不同公式族）和决策价值（求解器性能）评估，证明了固定组合库+单次模型调用的范式能以极低推理开销实现高质量参数配置，为异构大规模优化工作负载提供了实用的配置接口。

Abstract: Mixed-integer programming solvers expose hundreds or even thousands of parameters whose best setting varies per instance, yet per-instance search is prohibitively expensive. We frame this as configuration for heterogeneous Big Data optimization workloads and propose a nominate-then-adjudicate pipeline built around two design choices. First, a fixed portfolio is sampled once and reused across instances, removing per-instance configuration search at inference; on the evaluated families, the resulting shortlist retains substantial oracle headroom, making selection within it the empirical focus. Second, the fixed 20-feature representation can map structurally different instances to the same vector, preventing a feature-only selector from distinguishing them even when they prefer different configurations. The foundation model receives no task-specific weight updates, and selection is one-pass: one model call and no solver calls at inference. The study represents structural Variety through distinct formulation families and decision Value through solver-performance outcomes; the one-call, zero-solve protocol specifies the decision interface and leaves end-to-end latency unmeasured. Among comparisons exceeding a 20% practical-difference threshold, the complete adjudicator records 538 wins and 185 losses against the top nominee, while its win rate against the default rises from 84.9% to 90.2%. Against per-instance configuration baselines under a matched one-pass protocol it leads the cluster-level methods and the best-performing evaluated per-instance baseline, ISAC+.

</details>


### [186] [Progressive Permutation Alignment with Temperature-Controlled Spectral Sharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29396)
*Yuta Horioka,Naoya Chiba,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式对齐方法解决隐式神经表示中因排列对称性导致的权重空间插值损失屏障问题，通过逐层相关矩阵估计、匈牙利算法锚定、温度控制谱退火及逐层权重匹配细化，实现确定性、轻量级且跨架构稳定的神经元对应恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经表示将 3D 信号直接编码在网络权重中，但排列对称性使功能等价的独立训练模型在权重空间表现为远距离，线性插值产生巨大损失屏障。现有神经元对齐方法要么过早离散匹配陷入局部最优，要么依赖昂贵的连续松弛梯度优化，在 NeRF 等困难场景下均不可靠。

Method: 采用渐进式对齐策略，按输入到输出的层间依赖逐层处理：每层估计相关矩阵并用匈牙利算法得到离散锚点，再通过温度控制的谱退火逐步将相关结构锐化为置换矩阵，过程确定性且无需内层梯度优化；最后以对齐解为初始化，逐层权重匹配进一步精细化。

Result: 在 MLP、CNN 和 NeRF 上实验表明，该方法可靠恢复神经元对应关系，实现跨架构稳定对齐；在独立训练 NeRF 模型对齐实验中，权重插值损失屏障显著低于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的渐进式对齐框架有效解决了 INRs 中的排列对称性导致的权重空间不连续问题，提供了一种确定性、轻量且泛化性强的神经元对齐方案，为模型融合、插值等下游任务奠定基础。

Abstract: In implicit neural representations (INRs), 3D signals are encoded directly in network weights, making stable weight-space operations essential. However, permutation symmetry causes independently trained yet functionally equivalent models to appear distant in weight space, often inducing large loss barriers under linear interpolation. Existing neuron alignment methods either commit early to discrete matches and become trapped in inconsistent local optima or rely on computationally expensive gradient optimization over continuous relaxations, both of which are unreliable in challenging cases such as NeRF. We adopt progressive alignment, a layer-wise strategy that follows inter-layer dependencies from input to output. At each layer, a correlation matrix is estimated and aligned with a discrete anchor obtained by the Hungarian algorithm, after which temperature-controlled spectral annealing gradually sharpens the correlation structure toward a permutation matrix. This update is deterministic and lightweight, avoiding the inner-loop gradient optimization required by continuous relaxation methods. A final refinement stage further optimizes the network layer-by-layer via weight matching, starting from the aligned solution. Experiments on MLPs, CNNs, and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) show that the proposed method reliably recovers neuron correspondences and achieves stable alignment across architectures. In practical experiments aligning independently trained NeRF models, our method reduces the loss barriers under weight interpolation compared with existing approaches.

</details>


### [187] [The Predictive--Optimization Gap in GuanDan Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29411)
*Qicheng Zou,Biao Yuan,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Xiaokun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了强化学习中预测有效性与优化效用的差距：在掼蛋游戏中，虽然状态-收益交互能提供额外的预测价值，但基于冻结比赛胜率预测器变化（ΔΦ）的轮级监督干预，在单一谱系中虽有效，却在五个预注册复现种子中均无正向结果，汇总置信区间包含零，且跨迭代效应反复变号，无稳定的后期优势。这表明某信号虽能描述结果相关进展，却未能提供可靠的优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中常将结果预测信号视为优化目标，但预测有效性与优化效用是不同主张。作者旨在通过掼蛋这一多轮不完全信息游戏，实证考察预测信号是否真能作为可靠的优化方向。

Method: 1. 先进行保留分析，验证状态-收益交互是否在常数符号收益效应之外提供增量预测价值。2. 采用冻结比赛胜率预测器的变化量（ΔΦ）作为受控的轮级监督干预。3. 在匹配的 DanZero 风格蒙特卡洛自我对弈协议中实施干预，并进行五个预注册复现种子实验，汇总十个种子结果。

Result: 保留分析确认匹配进展具有预测意义；单一谱系中进度增强检查点显著优于基线；但方法层面结果反转：五个预注册复现种子均无正向结果，汇总十种子置信区间包含零，跨迭代效应反复变号且无共同的后期优势。

Conclusion: 存在预测-优化鸿沟：在所研究的学习器下，某信号虽能描述结果相关的进展，却不能提供稳定有益的优化方向。这提示在强化学习中直接使用预测信号作为优化目标需谨慎。

Abstract: Outcome-predictive signals are often treated as promising optimization targets in reinforcement learning, but predictive validity and optimization utility are different claims. We expose this distinction in GuanDan, a multi-round imperfect-information game in which the implication of a round outcome depends on the public match state. Held-out analysis shows that state--gain interactions provide incremental predictive value beyond a constant signed-gain effect, establishing that match progress is predictively meaningful. We then use changes in a frozen match-win predictor, $ΔΦ$, as a controlled round-level supervision intervention in a matched DanZero-style Monte Carlo self-play protocol. The intervention can succeed in an individual lineage: a selected progress-augmented checkpoint substantially outperforms its matched Baseline on a locked evaluation. Yet the method-level result reverses this impression: none of five preregistered replication seeds is positive, the pooled ten-seed interval includes zero, and across-iteration effects repeatedly change sign without a common late-stage advantage. These results identify a predictive--optimization gap: under the studied learner, a signal can describe outcome-relevant progress while failing to provide a reliably beneficial optimization direction.

</details>


### [188] [Alternative Strategies for Active Learning with Bayesian Bradley Terry Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29416)
*Saman Jayasinghe,Sean Walton,Alma Rahat,Andy Gray,Chris Wheadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Polya-Gamma数据增强的贝叶斯Bradley-Terry模型（BBTM），结合知识梯度（KG）主动学习策略，用于从有限的成对比较中恢复排序。通过Kendall's tau距离、后验排序熵和后验分离度定义采集函数，并引入后验分离可靠性（PSR）作为内部排序可靠性指标。实验表明，BBTM在合成实验中优于基于偏好的贝叶斯比较判断（PBCJ）；低噪声下后验熵策略最佳，高噪声下基于后验分离度的KG策略更优；PSR随比较积累增加且与排序准确性相关；鲁棒性实验显示30%随机结果替换对中等噪声下性能影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较排序在有限判断数量下，排序恢复精度取决于推断方法和配对选择策略。现有方法在量化潜在能力和排序的不确定性、以及主动选择最信息量配对方面存在不足。需要一种高效的贝叶斯推断框架，能同时提供不确定性量化和主动学习策略，且不依赖参考排序即可评估排序可靠性。

Method: 1. 贝叶斯Bradley-Terry模型（BBTM）：使用Polya-Gamma数据增强实现高效的Gibbs采样，量化潜在能力和排序的后验不确定性。2. 三种基于知识梯度（KG）的主动学习策略：采集函数分别基于Kendall's tau距离、后验排序熵、后验分离度。3. 近似一步后验矩更新降低候选配对评估计算成本。4. 后验分离可靠性（PSR）：测量后验均值能力分布相对于后验不确定性的离散程度，作为无需参考排序的内部可靠性指标。

Result: 合成实验（不同项目数、比较预算、噪声水平）：BBTM整体优于PBCJ。低至中等噪声下，基于后验熵的配对选择策略排序恢复最佳；最高噪声下，基于后验分离度的KG策略产生更好的点估计排序。PSR随比较积累单调增加，与排序准确性提升一致。鲁棒性实验：中等噪声下，30%结果替换为随机选择对排序性能影响微乎其微；确定了达到指定准确度目标所需的信息量结果比例。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯推断结合主动学习配对选择能有效从有限、噪声成对比较中恢复排序。BBTM提供了高效的后验采样和不确定性量化；KG主动学习策略在不同噪声水平下表现互补；PSR提供了实用的内部排序可靠性监测指标。研究为实际应用中的比较判断系统设计提供了理论和实证支持。

Abstract: Comparative judgement uses pairwise comparisons to rank items, but recovering accurate rankings from a limited number of judgements depends on both the inference method and the pairs selected. We developed a Bayesian Bradley-Terry model (BBTM) that uses Polya-Gamma data augmentation for efficient Gibbs sampling and quantifies uncertainty in latent abilities and ranks. We propose three active learning strategies based on the knowledge gradient (KG) principle. We define our acquisition functions using Kendall's tau distance, posterior rank entropy, and posterior separation. Approximate one-step updates to posterior moments reduce the computational cost of evaluating candidate pairs. We also introduce Posterior Separation Reliability (PSR), which measures the spread of posterior mean abilities relative to posterior uncertainty. It provides an internal indicator of ranking reliability without requiring a reference ranking. In synthetic experiments covering different item counts, comparison budgets, and noise levels, BBTM generally recovers ranking more accurately than preference-based Bayesian comparative judgement (PBCJ). At low to moderate noise levels, the pairwise entropy pair selection strategy performs best in ranking recovery. However, at the highest noise levels, the KG strategy based on posterior separation produces better point rankings. PSR increases alongside improvements in ranking accuracy as comparisons accumulate. Robustness experiments identify the proportion of informative outcomes needed to meet specified accuracy targets within a fixed comparison budget. In a moderate noise setting, replacing 30\% of outcomes with random choices has little effect on ranking performance. These findings demonstrate the value of Bayesian inference and active learning pair selection for recovering ranks from limited, noisy paired comparisons.

</details>


### [189] [Generalization Bounds via Vector-Valued $L_p$ Correlation $V_α$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29425)
*Mohammad Hossein Momeni Hamaneh,Mohammad Mahdi Mojahedian,Mohammad Hossein Yassaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族依赖度量 V_α（向量值 L_p 范数相关性），它在 α=1 时退化为全变差（TV），在 α=2 时与方差/卡方相关，且在 α>2 时能处理重尾损失。作者推导了基于 V_α 的泛化界，证明了其在光滑双射重参数化下的不变性，提出了自适应密度稳定（ADS）条件并验证了 Gibbs 后验满足该条件，还将框架扩展到基于超样本的条件形式（类似 CMI），并在多个实验中展示了调节 α 对界紧致度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的泛化界主要依赖互信息或全变差，但在高维情形下两者行为差异巨大，且难以处理重尾损失。需要一个统一的、可调参数的依赖度量族，能在 TV 与方差/卡方之间插值，同时兼顾重尾鲁棒性、重参数化不变性和可计算性。

Method: 1. 定义向量值 L_p 范数相关性 V_α 作为依赖度量族；2. 基于 V_α 推导泛化界，分析 α=1,2,>2 时的特化形式；3. 证明 V_α 在光滑双射重参数化下的不变性；4. 提出自适应密度稳定（ADS）条件并显式控制 V_2，验证 Gibbs 后验在有界损失下满足 ADS；5. 引入基于超样本的条件 V_α 公式（类比 CMI）以解决尺度问题；6. 在输出空间评估依赖度作为实用代理。

Result: 在贝叶斯线性回归、带噪 MNIST 和预训练 ResNet-50/CIFAR-10 上的实验表明：调节 α 可在不同 regime 下改变界的紧致度；α=2 时的界可写为 ∫√Var_S[p(w|S)] dw，具有直观解释；ADS 条件为 Gibbs 后验给出了依赖逆温度和样本量的显式速率；条件超样本公式在扩展设置下表现出与原始干净 MNIST 相似的行为。

Conclusion: V_α 族提供了一个统一、灵活且理论性质良好（不变性、重尾鲁棒性、可解释性）的依赖度量框架，通过调节单一参数 α 可在 TV 到重尾泛化界之间平滑过渡，配合 ADS 条件和条件超样本扩展，为随机学习算法的泛化分析提供了实用且理论扎实的工具。

Abstract: Understanding generalization of randomized learning algorithms remains a central challenge. Mutual-information and total-variation bounds provide complementary ways to quantify data--hypothesis dependence, but they can behave quite differently in high-dimensional regimes. We study a one-parameter family of dependence measures, the vector-valued $L_p$-norm correlation $V_α$, and derive generalization bounds whose dependence terms interpolate between total variation (TV) at $α=1$ and variance and $χ^2$ related behavior at $α=2$. For $α>2$, the Hölder-conjugate loss moment is below two, yielding heavy-tailed specializations that can remain finite even when the loss variance is infinite. A particularly interpretable specialization occurs at $α=2$, where the bound can be expressed in terms of the variability of the algorithm's output density across datasets, $\int \sqrt{\mathrm{Var}_S[p(w\mid S)]}~ dw$. We show that the entire $V_α$ family is invariant under smooth bijective reparameterizations of the hypothesis space. We also introduce an adaptive density stability (ADS) condition that implies explicit control of $V_2$, and we verify it for Gibbs-type posteriors under bounded losses (yielding rates that depend on the inverse temperature and sample size). To address scaling, we extend the framework to a conditional formulation using supersamples, analogous in spirit to conditional mutual information (CMI), and discuss evaluations in the output space as a practical surrogate for estimating dependence. Simulations on Bayesian linear regression illustrate how tuning $α$ can change the tightness of the resulting bounds across regimes. Additional supersample experiments on noisy MNIST and on a pretrained ResNet-50 for CIFAR-10 show similar behavior of the projected loss-space bounds beyond the original clean-MNIST setting.

</details>


### [190] [BIRD-RL: Scaling Agentic Reinforcement Learning over Stateful Data-Centric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29426)
*Ge Qu,Jinyang Li,Xiaohan Xu,Xiaolong Li,Bowen Qin,Nan Huo,Ziwei Tang,Chenhao Ma,Reynold Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BIRD-RL是一种四阶段智能体强化学习课程，通过轨迹级持久执行基础设施实现数据库副本安全复用，解决SQL调试等数据中心任务中的空间效率问题，在BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite基准上7B和14B模型分别达到44.40%和48.00%成功率，可与专有模型媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具中心的智能体RL框架在状态化数据中心任务（如SQL调试）中存在严重空间效率低下问题：每个rollout需要独立数据库容器，导致资源浪费；若naive复用数据库而不跟踪多轮交互的演化状态，会造成轨迹污染和跨轨迹干扰，破坏策略更新稳定性。

Method: 提出BIRD-RL四阶段课程，交替强化基础推理能力和轨迹自适应的on-policy探索；核心是轨迹级持久执行基础设施，支持数据库副本安全复用同时保持轨迹隔离，提升空间RL效率12.8倍，无需Kubernetes即可实现可扩展的智能体RL。

Result: 在BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite基准上，7B和14B模型成功率分别为44.40%和48.00%，接近Claude-Opus-4.6 Agent的48.20%；同时训练出统一SLMs，兼具高级SQL调试和生成能力，展示多目标优化潜力。

Conclusion: BIRD-RL通过创新的课程学习和持久执行基础设施，有效解决了数据中心任务中智能体RL的空间效率和稳定性瓶颈，使小模型在复杂SQL任务上达到专有模型水平，为真实世界异构数据中心任务的多目标优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training small language models (SLMs), existing frameworks are predominantly tool-centric and struggle with data-centric environments. Specifically, applying tool-centric frameworks to stateful tasks like SQL debugging introduces severe spatial inefficiencies since proper environment isolation typically requires a separate database container per rollout. Naively reusing databases without tracking evolving states across multi-turn interactions causes trajectory contamination and cross-trajectory interference, which severely destabilizes policy updates. To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce BIRD-RL, a novel four-stage agentic RL curriculum that alternately enhances foundational agent reasoning and trajectory-adaptive on-policy exploration to circumvent the instability inherently associated with direct end-to-end learning. Crucially, to support this curriculum, BIRD-RL is driven by a Trajectory-scoped Persistent Execution Infrastructure, which enables safe database replica reuse while preserving trajectory isolation. This design improves spatial RL efficiency by 12.8x, successfully enabling scalable agentic RL for complex data-centric tasks without needs of Kubernettes. Empirical evaluations on the BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite benchmark demonstrate that our 7B and 14B models achieve Success Rates of 44.40% and 48.00%, respectively, showing competitive performance against strong proprietary model-based agents such as Claude-Opus-4.6 Agent (48.20%). Furthermore, BIRD-RL successfully trains unified SLMs capable of both advanced SQL debugging and generation, highlighting its broader potential for multi-objective optimization across heterogeneous, real-world data-centric tasks.

</details>


### [191] [One Act, Many Masks: Effect-Marginal Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29427)
*Shaojin Ma,Ruitao Wang,Pengyi Li,Jie Yang,Liangyu Chen,Gangyi Zhao,Hongyao Tang,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出多轮强化学习中存在“信用-更新错位”问题：不同文本响应可能导致相同的可执行效果，但信用分配仍绑定到具体文本实现，导致策略更新方向偏离。提出 Effect-Marginal Policy Optimization (EMPO)，通过效应级代理函数耦合等效文本响应的更新，保留原始信用信号，无需额外评论家或回合，在 ALFWorld、WebShop、Sokoban 上显著提升任务性能与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮 RL 虽然改进了细粒度信用分配，但策略优化仍使用响应特定的概率比。不同文本响应可产生相同的可执行效果，但后续结果产生的信用差异仍绑定在各自的文本实现上，导致信用-更新错位。分析发现信用-文本配对直接决定策略更新方向，即使策略上下文和可执行效果固定。因此需要将策略优化与执行层面的等价性对齐。

Method: 提出 Effect-Marginal Policy Optimization (EMPO)。引入效应级代理目标，将具有相同可执行效果的不同文本响应视为等价类，通过边际化概率比跨文本实现耦合更新。EMPO 保留原始信用信号，兼容轨迹级和步级信用估计器，且无需额外评论家网络或额外采样回合。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、Sokoban 三个交互式基准上，EMPO 优于强基线（如 PPO、GRPO 等），在任务成功率和学习效率（样本复杂度）上均取得显著提升。消融实验验证了效应级边际化对信用-更新对齐的关键作用。

Conclusion: EMPO 通过效应级边际化解决了多轮 RL 中的信用-更新错位问题，实现了策略优化与执行等价性的对齐。该方法即插即用、无额外开销，为 LLM 代理在复杂长时程交互任务中的高效学习提供了通用改进。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the performance of LLM agents on complex, long-horizon interactive tasks. However, despite progress in fine-grained credit assignment, policy optimization still applies these learning signals through response-specific probability ratios. Different textual responses can induce the same executable effect, yet credit differences arising from subsequent outcomes remain tied to their individual textual realizations. We identify this mismatch as credit-update misalignment. Our analysis reveals that credit-text pairing directly shapes policy-update directions, even when the policy context and executable effect are held fixed. Motivated by this finding, we propose Effect-Marginal Policy Optimization (EMPO), which aligns policy optimization with execution-level equivalence through an effect-level surrogate that couples updates across alternative textual realizations. EMPO preserves the original credit signals, integrates with both trajectory-level and step-level credit estimators, and requires no additional critics or rollouts. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Sokoban show that EMPO outperforms strong baselines in both task performance and learning efficiency.

</details>


### [192] [How Far Can Foundation Models Be Distilled for Efficient Jet Tagging?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29439)
*Jay Agarwal,Siddharth Khare,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在大型强子对撞机(LHC)喷注标记任务中，通过知识蒸馏将大型教师模型(包括基础模型)的性能迁移到极小型学生模型的效果。实验表明，教师模型强度是提升学生性能的关键，且仅含2万参数的蒸馏L-GATr-slim学生模型已超越所有从头训练的最先进喷注标记器，推理成本极低；甚至2千参数版本也能媲美参数量大千倍的ParT模型。同时发现同规模学生模型推理速度相差近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注标记是LHC机器学习的核心应用，近期模型虽融入更多物理知识或采用大规模预训练，但实验对推理成本有严格限制。需要探索知识蒸馏能否将大模型性能高效迁移到极小模型，并系统研究教师模型的哪些属性(架构、对称性、预训练)对学生提升最关键，以及学生模型能压缩到多小仍能接近最先进水平。

Method: 在喷注标记基准上，选取六个在架构、对称性和预训练上各异的教师模型(含两个基础模型)，蒸馏到五个小型学生模型中。随后对两个最强蒸馏学生(Transformer和L-GATr-slim)进行宽度和深度的进一步压缩，系统评估不同参数量下的性能与推理成本，并分析学生从教师学到的知识迁移机制。

Result: 所有教师均能提升所有学生性能，增益主要取决于教师强度而非具体架构。压缩后的L-GATr-slim仅2万参数即超越所有从头训练的已发布喷注标记器，推理成本仅为教师模型的一小分数；2千参数版本性能匹配参数量大1000倍的ParT。同规模学生模型推理速度相差近10倍。已开源所有代码与模型权重。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏能极高效地将大型喷注标记模型压缩为极小参数量学生模型，且教师模型的整体强度是决定蒸馏效果的主导因素。蒸馏的L-GATr-slim在极低参数量(2万甚至2千)下实现了最先进性能与极低推理成本的双重突破，为LHC实时触发系统等资源受限场景提供了理想解决方案。学生模型推理效率差异提示架构选择对部署同样关键。

Abstract: Jet tagging is one of the most important applications of machine learning at the Large Hadron Collider. Recent models build in more physics knowledge or rely on large-scale pretraining, but the experiments place strict limits on their inference cost. Knowledge distillation can transfer their performance to small students that are cheap to run. We ask which properties of a teacher help the student, how small a student can get and how close it can come to the state of the art. On a jet tagging benchmark, we distil six teachers that differ in architecture, symmetry and pretraining, including two foundation models, into five small students. Every teacher improves every student, and the gain depends mainly on the strength of the teacher. We then shrink our two strongest distilled students, Transformer and L-GATr-slim, in width and in depth. With 20 thousand parameters, a distilled L-GATr-slim outperforms every published jet tagger trained from scratch at a fraction of the inference cost of every teacher. Even with two thousand parameters, it matches ParT, a model with a thousand times as many parameters. We also study what the students learn from their teachers. Our benchmark of inference cost shows that students of the same size can differ by almost an order of magnitude in speed. We release the code and the weights of all trained models.

</details>


### [193] [Harness Scaling for Generalizable Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29458)
*Fei Bai,Huatong Song,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Jian Yang,Bryan Dai,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Harness Scaling 作为智能体强化学习的新扩展维度，通过在多样化执行配置（harness）上训练，而非单一固定 harness，显著提升模型对未见白盒/黑盒 harness 的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习通常在单一固定 harness 上训练，导致模型过度专业化、难以迁移到不同 harness 配置，限制了跨配置泛化。

Method: 构建因子化 harness 空间，选择具有平衡因子覆盖的紧凑训练子集，并组织跨 harness 训练，结合全局覆盖与同任务受控变化。

Result: 在多个基准、模型规模和 RL 算法上，Harness Scaling 持续提升对未见白盒配置的泛化；且通过结构化白盒变化训练的模型能有效迁移至未见外部黑盒 harness，超越单一复杂黑盒 harness 训练的模型。

Conclusion: Harness 多样性是智能体 RL 的有意义扩展维度，且这种多样性的组织方式对跨 harness 泛化至关重要。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have become a central component of modern agentic systems, shaping how models use tools, manage context, and coordinate long-horizon interaction. Realizing the benefits of these harnesses depends on how effectively the underlying model can make use of them, motivating reinforcement learning directly through the harness. However, existing approaches typically train through a single fixed harness, which can lead to specialization and limited transfer across harness configurations. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Harness Scaling}, a new scaling dimension for agentic RL that extends training from a single harness to diverse execution configurations. Concretely, we construct a factorized harness space, select a compact training subset with balanced factorial coverage, and organize cross-harness training to combine global coverage with controlled variation for the same task. Across multiple benchmarks, model scales, and RL algorithms, Harness Scaling consistently improves generalization to unseen white-box configurations. Moreover, models trained through structured white-box variation transfer effectively to unseen external black-box harnesses, outperforming models trained through a single complex black-box harness. These results establish harness diversity as a meaningful scaling dimension for agentic RL and highlight the importance of how this diversity is organized for cross-harness generalization.

</details>


### [194] [Asymmetry Tunneling Beyond Adaptive Equivariance Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29473)
*Eunho Jeong,Joon Jang,Hyeonjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究等变神经网络在面对现实数据中存在的对称性破坏结构时，是否需要自适应控制等变性放松。通过引入残差路径与严格等变模型并行，并限制其接口，观察到“残差坍塌”与“不对称性隧穿”现象，实验表明自适应控制并非捕捉对称性破坏结构的先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络将预设对称性作为强归纳偏置，但现实数据常包含对称性破坏结构，现有工作主要通过模型侧的等变性放松来应对。本文提出互补问题：等变性放松是否必须自适应控制？旨在探究严格等变模型配合受限残差路径能否捕捉任务相关的非对称结构。

Method: 在严格等变模型旁引入一条残差路径，并刻意限制二者的接口（如仅允许标量通道交互），以此考察任务相关结构的传递。在五个基准上实验，并通过四个互补诊断（如残差范数、特征可视化、消融实验、对称性破坏度量）表征现象。

Result: 实验观察到两种现象：残差坍塌（残差路径贡献趋零）与不对称性隧穿（残差路径在接口受限下仍能传递非对称信息并提升性能）。后者表明严格等变主干配合受限残差即可捕捉对称性破坏结构，无需自适应调节等变程度。

Conclusion: 自适应控制等变性放松并非捕捉潜在对称性破坏结构的必要条件。通过严格等变模型与受限残差路径的组合，即可实现有效的不对称性信息传递，为等变网络设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks encode prescribed symmetries as powerful inductive biases, yet real-world data may exhibit symmetry-breaking structure that these priors cannot fully capture. Prior work has largely addressed this challenge through model-side equivariance relaxation, seeking an appropriate level of equivariance for the data. This raises a complementary question: must equivariance relaxation be adaptively controlled? To investigate this question, we introduce a residual path alongside a strictly equivariant model and deliberately restrict their interface to examine the transmission of task-relevant structure. Across five benchmarks, we observe both residual collapse and survival despite these restrictions; we call the latter phenomenon asymmetry tunneling. We further characterize this phenomenon through four complementary diagnostics. Our results suggest that adaptive control of equivariance relaxation is not a prerequisite for capturing potential symmetry-breaking structure.

</details>


### [195] [Density-Level Backward Error for Low-NFE Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29475)
*Ewsey Obzherin,Valentin Khrulkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于密度水平的后向误差框架，用于低NFE生成ODE采样中的计算分配，通过识别改变演化边缘分布的修正方程缺陷分量，实现了求解器阶数感知的计算分配规则，并在CIFAR-10 EDM概率流ODE上验证了其在低NFE下对固定更新Euler采样的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低NFE生成ODE采样不仅受限于求解器阶数，还受限于固定网络评估预算在流中的分布方式；现有的基于轨迹或端点代理的调度搜索以及高阶求解器设计未能充分解决密度演化层面的离散化误差。

Method: 开发密度水平后向误差框架，识别求解器修正方程缺陷中改变演化边缘的分量，排除Wasserstein切几何中不可见的螺线轨迹畸变；由此导出求解器阶数感知的计算分配规则，并利用规范自由度选择数值有利的速度代表。

Result: 在精确高斯和混合模型上验证了机制，包括基于曲率的分配失效和连续最优传输非离散最优的情况；在预训练的CIFAR-10 EDM概率流ODE上，密度感知分配在低NFE区间持续改进固定更新Euler采样；匹配种子收敛到稠密Euler参考证实该方法加速了对同一高NFE流的近似而非改变渐近采样器。

Conclusion: 该方法通过密度水平误差分析分离了离散化最优计算分配，为低NFE生成采样提供了理论支撑的预算分配策略，且不改变渐近采样行为。

Abstract: Low-NFE generative ODE sampling is constrained not only by solver order, but also by how a fixed budget of network evaluations is distributed along the flow. We develop a density-level backward-error framework that identifies the component of a solver's modified-equation defect that changes the evolving marginal, while excluding solenoidal trajectory distortion that is invisible in Wasserstein tangent geometry. The resulting residual induces a solver-order-aware compute-allocation rule and, through gauge freedom, an objective for selecting numerically favorable velocity representatives of the same density path. This separates our approach from schedule search driven by trajectory or endpoint surrogates and from higher-order solver design. Exact Gaussian and mixture models establish the mechanism, including cases where curvature-based allocation fails and continuum-optimal transport is not discretization-optimal. On a pretrained CIFAR-10 EDM probability-flow ODE, density-aware allocation consistently improves fixed-update Euler sampling in the low-NFE regime. Matched-seed convergence to a dense Euler reference confirms that the method accelerates approximation of the same high-NFE flow rather than changing the asymptotic sampler.

</details>


### [196] [Generative Modeling via Nonlinear Schrödinger Bridges and Branching Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29476)
*Mathias Grau,Pierre Henry-Labordère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于前向-后向偏微分方程（PDE）的生成建模新框架，源自均值场博弈（MFG）理论，与非线性薛定谔系统密切相关。主要贡献：将这种联系重新诠释为生成建模，开发高效数值方法；推导并刻画经典薛定谔桥问题的非线性扩展（SBNL）；设计基于分支扩散和半线性 PDE 概率表示的模拟算法，具有极易并行、高维可扩展、适用广泛非线性交泛泛函等优势；在经典薛定谔桥恢复、凸惩罚非线性扩展、高维图像生成（MNIST、FFHQ）等任务上验证有效；同时该框架还统一了基于分数的生成建模、扩散模型和可解高斯薛定谔桥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成建模方法（如扩散模型、基于分数的模型、薛定谔桥）大多基于线性 PDE 或特定随机过程，难以直接融入非线性交互效应（如粒子间相互作用、凸惩罚项等）。均值场博弈理论自然产生前向-后向 PDE 系统，与非线性薛定谔方程相关，为构建更通用的生成建模框架提供了理论基础。本文旨在利用这一联系，推导非线性薛定谔桥（SBNL），并开发可大规模并行的数值求解方法，以支持高维复杂分布的生成任务。

Method: 1. 理论推导：从均值场博弈出发，建立耦合的前向-后向 PDE 系统，推导非线性薛定谔桥问题（SBNL），涵盖凸惩罚（Gross-Pitaevskii 型）和势能交互项。2. 数值算法：利用半线性 PDE 的概率表示，设计基于分支扩散的模拟算法，将 PDE 求解转化为粒子系统的蒙特卡洛模拟。3. 并行化：算法本质上是极易并行的（embarrassingly parallel），可高效扩展至高维。4. 统一框架：证明该框架包含基于分数的生成模型、扩散模型和可解高斯薛定谔桥作为特例。

Result: 数值实验表明：(1) 算法能准确恢复经典薛定谔桥解；(2) 在带有凸惩罚（Gross-Pitaevskii）和势能交互项的非线性扩展中表现良好；(3) 在高维生成任务上（MNIST 手写数字、FFHQ 人脸图像合成）取得竞争性结果；(4) 算法展现出良好的并行扩展性和对多种非线性交泛泛函的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了基于均值场博弈前向-后向 PDE 的统一生成建模框架（SBNL），将非线性薛定谔桥问题推广到广泛的非线性交互泛函，并开发了基于分支扩散的高效并行数值方法。该方法不仅理论完备，而且在高维图像生成等实际任务中表现优异，同时统一了现有主流生成模型（分数模型、扩散模型、高斯薛定谔桥），为生成建模提供了新的理论视角和计算工具。

Abstract: We propose a new generative modeling framework grounded in forward-backward partial differential equations (PDEs). These equations arise naturally in mean-field game (MFG) theory, and are closely related to nonlinear Schrödinger systems. Our main contribution is to reinterpret this connection within the setting of generative modeling and to develop an efficient numerical methodology for its practical implementation. Specifically, we derive and characterize a nonlinear extension of the classical Schrödinger bridge problem through a coupled system of PDEs, yielding a model we call SBNL (Schrödinger Bridge NonLinear). To compute its solution, we design a simulation algorithm based on branching diffusions and the probabilistic representation of semilinear PDEs. The resulting method is embarrassingly parallel, scalable to high dimensions, and applicable to a broad class of nonlinear interaction functionals. We validate the approach through numerical experiments, including recovery of the classical Schrödinger bridge, nonlinear extensions with convex penalties (Gross-Pitaevskii) as well as potential interaction terms, and high-dimensional generative tasks such as image synthesis on the MNIST and FFHQ human face dataset. Furthermore, the same framework embeds score-based generative modeling, diffusion models, and tractable gaussian Schrödinger bridges.

</details>


### [197] [Rethinking Agentic Model Orchestration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29504)
*Tong Guan,Tianxiang Zhan,Shirui Pan,Ming Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 FairAMO 评估协议，受控实验揭示智能体模型编排 (AMO) 在时间序列基础模型 (TSFM) 预测中的增益上限：最佳编排仅比单一最强 TSFM 降低 <6% MASE，增益源于候选模型互补性而非数量，历史证据价值快速饱和，冗余成员反而抵消收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AMO 方法在预测任务中常优于单一模型，但不清楚性能提升是来自更强/更互补的候选模型，还是编排机制本身。需要剥离这两个因素，量化编排的真实贡献。

Method: 引入 FairAMO 受控评估协议：冻结候选模型预测结果，仅变换编排策略；在 GIFT-Eval 基准上系统实验，对比不同编排器、候选集大小、历史窗口长度对 MASE 的影响。

Result: 1) AMO 确能提升准确性，但增益受限于候选 TSFM 上限，最佳编排仅比最强单一 TSFM 降低 <6% MASE。2) 增益取决于候选模型互补性（不同模型在不同任务擅长），而非候选数量。3) 增加成员收益递减，冗余成员可抵消增益。4) 历史证据价值快速饱和：少数近期窗口即捕获全部增益，更长历史无额外贡献。

Conclusion: 系统级预测增益主要源于强互补候选模型的集合，而非编排算法本身。FairAMO 将编排贡献与候选模型质量解耦，为未来 TSFM 编排研究提供基准与洞见：应优先构建互补候选池，而非过度优化编排策略。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) perform unevenly across forecasting tasks. Recent work uses agentic model orchestration (AMO) to select or combine TSFMs, often outperforming individual forecasters. Yet these gains may reflect stronger or more complementary candidates rather than better orchestration. To disentangle these effects, we introduce FairAMO, a controlled evaluation protocol with frozen candidate forecasts. Experiments on GIFT-Eval show that AMO improves accuracy, but its gains are bounded by the candidate TSFMs. The best orchestrators reduce MASE by less than 6% relative to the best single TSFM across all tasks. These gains depend on complementarity (different candidates excel on different tasks) rather than the number of candidates. Adding members yields diminishing returns, and a redundant member can erase these gains. The value of historical evidence also saturates quickly. A few recent windows capture all gains, and further history adds none. These findings separate system-level forecasting gains from the contribution of orchestration itself.

</details>


### [198] [Shortcut-Resistant CAM Distillation for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29508)
*Wenhai Wan,Teng Zhang,Shao-Yuan Li,Xinrui Wang,Qiang-Sheng Hua,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对长尾分布数据集中尾部类别泛化困难的问题，从捷径学习视角提出 SRCD（Shortcut-Resistant CAM Distillation）框架，通过在 CAM 空间将头部类别的对象聚焦解释蒸馏到尾部类别，抑制尾部类别对非语义捷径的依赖，在多个长尾基准上持续提升强基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集常呈长尾分布，尾部类别样本稀缺且常共享相似上下文，导致非语义信号（如背景、纹理）高度相关，成为模型学习的诱人捷径；而头部类别外观和环境多样，迫使模型学习更稳定的对象中心表示。现有方法未充分利用头部类别的抗捷径特性迁移至尾部类别。

Method: 提出即插即用的 SRCD 框架：1) 在类激活图（CAM）空间操作，CAM 提供类别特定的空间证据图；2) 从少量头部类别候选中聚合 CAM，基于一致性和集中度的能量模型加权构建抗捷径教师 CAM；3) 将教师 CAM 蒸馏到尾部类别 CAM，抑制尾部类别对捷径区域的证据质量依赖。理论分析量化了捷径依赖为 CAM 空间中捷径区域的证据质量，并证明 SRCD 能抑制尾部捷径。

Result: 在多个长尾基准（如 ImageNet-LT、iNaturalist 等）上进行广泛实验，SRCD 持续改进强基线（如 LWS、Balanced Softmax、DisAlign 等），在尾部类别准确率上显著提升，整体性能达到或优于现有 SOTA 方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过捷径学习视角重新审视长尾识别，揭示尾部类别更易陷入捷径学习的机制，提出 SRCD 在 CAM 空间实现头部到尾部的抗捷径知识迁移，理论与实验双重验证了方法有效性，为长尾视觉识别提供了新的可插拔解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often follow a long-tailed distribution, making generalization to tail classes difficult. We revisit this problem through the lens of shortcut learning, where models prefer the easiest predictive cues (e.g., background or textures) over object-centric semantics, especially under scarce and biased supervision. We find that this tendency is amplified for tail classes: limited examples often share similar contexts, making non-semantic signals highly correlated and thus tempting shortcuts, whereas head classes with diverse appearances and environments encourage more stable object-focused representations. Motivated by this observation, we propose Shortcut-Resistant CAM Distillation (SRCD), a plug-and-play framework that transfers object-focused explanations from head to tail classes. SRCD operates in the Class Activation Map (CAM) space, where a CAM provides a class-specific spatial evidence map for a prediction. SRCD aggregates CAMs from a small set of head-class candidates into a shortcut-resistant teacher using an energy-model weighting based on coherence and concentration, and distills it to the tail-class CAM. We provide a theoretical analysis that quantifies shortcut reliance as shortcut-region evidence mass in CAM space and shows that SRCD suppresses tail shortcuts. Extensive experiments on long-tailed benchmarks consistently improve strong baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Haifeng3/SRCD.

</details>


### [199] [BRIDGE: An Acceptance Gate to Block False Improvements in Model Updates, with a Retrospective Clinical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29515)
*Dwi Sunaryono*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIDGE 是一种模型无关的接受门控方法，通过交叉拟合和自助法下界显著降低模型更新中的虚假改进率，在多个数据集和任务上将虚假改进率从约 20% 降至 1% 以下，同时保持或提升真实增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型反复更新时，验证集上的正向增益可能是虚假改进，会误导临床决策。朴素规则接受所有正向验证增益，因同一数据用于选择和评估而存在选择偏差，过于乐观。

Method: BRIDGE 采用交叉拟合：每一折数据评估在其他折上选出的更新，减少选择偏差；仅当增益的自助法下界超过预设边界时才接受更新。方法模型无关，超参数固定。

Result: 在 11 个新数据集 (A) 上虚假改进率从 19.1% 降至 1.1%，合成任务 (B) 从 20.7% 降至 1.7%，12 个回顾性临床任务 (C) 从 20.0% 降至 0%，39 个支持数据集 (D) 从 12.5% 降至 0.5%，OpenML 14 个新数据集 (E) 从 25.6% 降至 1.0%。BRIDGE 去除了朴素规则接受的 97%-100% 的虚假改进，每部署更新的平均增益是朴素规则的 1.8 到 3.1 倍。95% 上界虚假改进率低于 5.5%（B 中独立任务正式为 3.56%，实测真实数据条件下为 0.33%-5.40%）。

Conclusion: BRIDGE 能有效消除模型更新中的虚假改进，同时保留甚至放大真实增益，为临床及通用场景下的模型更新提供了可靠的接受门控。

Abstract: Models are updated repeatedly, and an update that looks better on validation data but not on new data is a false improvement that can misguide clinical decisions. The naive rule accepts every positive validation gain and is optimistic when the same data choose and judge the update. BRIDGE is a model-agnostic acceptance gate that decides each update from predictions and the available labels, with fixed settings. Each fold of the data judges the update chosen on the other folds (cross-fitting) to reduce selection bias; acceptance requires the gain's lower bootstrap bound to exceed a margin. The four settings are (A) 11 new datasets, (B) synthetic tasks, (C) 12 retrospective clinical tasks, and (D) 39 supporting datasets. The false-improvement rate falls from 19.1% (naive) to 1.1% (BRIDGE) in (A), 20.7% to 1.7% (B), 20.0% to 0% (C), and 12.5% to 0.5% (D); every 95% confidence interval of the reduction over datasets or tasks excludes zero (not over the clinical sources). An equal-size random draw from the naive acceptances contains more false improvements in (A)-(E) (post hoc, p<0.001). On the OpenML collection (E) of 14 new datasets, the false-improvement rate falls from 25.6% to 1.0%. BRIDGE removes 97% to 100% of the false improvements that the naive rule accepts, and its mean gain per deployed update is 1.8 to 3.1 times the naive rule's. The 95% upper bound on the false-improvement rate is below 5.5%: formal in (B) for independent tasks (3.56%), and conditional on the real data tested (0.33% to 5.40%).

</details>


### [200] [Geometric knowledge spaces, Attention pairing and Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29526)
*Shang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何方法来推导注意力机制并探索新的Transformer架构，通过形式化知识世界和注意力配对的概念，统一了标准注意力、因果注意力、线性注意力、张量注意力及循环神经网络等架构，并引入新的几何空间以启发新型神经网络设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过几何视角统一理解各类注意力机制的本质，为注意力架构提供理论基础，并探索潜在的新型Transformer架构。

Method: 形式化定义知识世界和注意力配对概念，构建几何框架，在此框架下推导并统一多种注意力机制，同时引入新的几何空间以拓展架构设计空间。

Result: 该几何框架自然导出了标准注意力、因果注意力、线性注意力、张量注意力和循环神经网络等架构，并提出了若干新的几何空间，为新型神经网络架构提供了灵感。

Conclusion: 几何方法为注意力机制提供了统一的理论视角，不仅解释了现有架构，还通过新几何空间为未来神经网络架构创新指明了方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a geometric approach to deriving attention mechanisms and exploring potential new transformer architectures. We begin by formalizing the notions of knowledge worlds and attention pairings, then demonstrate how this framework naturally leads to the architectures of standard attention, causal attention, linear attention, tensor attention, and recurrent neural networks. Finally, we introduce several new geometric spaces that may inspire novel neural network architectures.

</details>


### [201] [Are Joint-Embedding Predictive Objectives Effective for Longitudinal EHRs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29528)
*Ashvin Gupta,Brendan Delaney,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在电子健康记录（EHR）序列表示中的适用性，实验表明JEPA虽训练稳定但持续落后于标准掩码语言模型（MLM），且减小目标规模无法恢复下游性能，说明稳定的潜在预测不足以实现有竞争力的EHR表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLM在EHR预训练中占主导，但重构单个事件可能过度强调噪声或弱信息观测，且不显式鼓励学习高层患者状态。JEPA通过预测潜在空间表示学习，在多领域表现强劲，因此作者探究JEPA能否适配EHR序列表示。

Method: 使用MIMIC-IV数据集，设计三种JEPA公式，对比标准MLM在EHR序列表示学习上的表现，分析目标规模与目标异质性对预测误差及下游性能的影响。

Result: 三种JEPA公式均稳定训练且无表示崩塌，但持续弱于MLM。目标规模而非目标异质性增加预测误差，但减小目标规模无法恢复下游性能。

Conclusion: 稳定的潜在预测本身不足以支撑有竞争力的EHR表示学习，凸显了为EHR定义临床上有意义的潜在目标的挑战。

Abstract: Masked language modelling (MLM) is the dominant pretraining objective for transformer encoders applied to longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs). However, reconstructing individual events may overemphasise noisy or weakly informative observations and does not explicitly encourage learning higher-level patient states. Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) instead learn by predicting representations in latent space and have shown strong performance in a variety of domains. In this paper, we investigate to what extent JEPA can be adapted to EHR sequence representation. Our empirical investigation using MIMIC-IV shows that although all three of our JEPA formulations train stably without representational collapse, they consistently underperform standard MLM. We find that target size, not target heterogeneity, increases prediction error, yet reducing the target size does not recover downstream performance. This suggests that stable latent prediction alone is insufficient for competitive EHR representation learning and highlights the challenge of defining clinically meaningful latent targets for EHR.

</details>


### [202] [Phase Transitions in Learning Driven by Extremal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29531)
*Ethan Levien,Trevor GrandPre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究极值回归问题（多实例学习的一种），分析了最大池化模型下软最大近似参数λ随梯度下降增加时的动力学行为。在大包n和高维度d的联合极限（α=ln n/d固定）下，推导了梯度流的低维投影，发现两个相变：与无序系统随机能量模型相关的内在冻结相变，以及现代Hopfield网络中的检索相变。数值模拟验证了相图和有限尺寸训练梯度收敛到渐近形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多实例学习中常用最大池化，但会导致梯度消失。现代流程用软最大近似最大池化，并将锐度λ作为可学习参数。当最大池化是真实数据生成过程时，学习成功需要λ在梯度下降中增加，这会将动力学推向梯度消失的参数空间区域。需要理解梯度下降在何种条件下失效。

Method: 研究一个极小模型，在包大小n和维度d同时趋于无穷大、且α=ln n/d保持固定的联合极限下，推导梯度流的低维投影。利用无序系统的随机能量模型和现代Hopfield网络理论分析相变行为，并通过数值模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 梯度流表现出两个截然不同的相变：内在冻结相变（与随机能量模型相关）和检索相变（现代Hopfield网络中出现）。数值模拟确认了相图，且有限尺寸训练梯度收敛到预测的渐近形式。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了极值回归中梯度下降动力学的相变结构，为理解软最大近似最大池化时的训练困难提供了理论基础，指出了内在冻结和检索相变作为梯度消失和学习成功的关键机制。

Abstract: We study an extremal regression problem, an example of multiple instance learning (MIL), where the predictors are given as multisets, called bags. The bag elements (called instances) possess hidden labels which are pooled to produce an observed bag-level label. Max pooling is common in MIL applications, but gives rise to vanishing gradients. To handle this, modern MIL pipelines approximate the max with a softmax, treating the sharpness as a learnable parameter, denoted $λ$. When the max pooling model is the true data generating process, learning will be successful when $λ$ is increased during gradient descent, thereby driving the dynamics into regions of parameter space with vanishing gradients. To understand the conditions under which gradient descent breaks down, we study a minimal model and derive a low dimensional projection of the gradient flow in the joint limit of large bags $n$ and high dimension $d$ with $α=\ln n/d$ fixed. The flow has two distinct phase transitions: an intrinsic freezing transition related to the random energy model of disordered systems, and a retrieval transition occurring in modern Hopfield networks. Numerical simulation confirms both the phase diagram and the convergence of the finite-size training gradient to its predicted asymptotic form.

</details>


### [203] [Block Diffusion for Longitudinal EHR: Accuracy, Efficiency, and Representation Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29533)
*Ashvin Gupta,Joshua Placidi,Brendan Delaney,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于块的掩码扩散模型（BD）作为自回归（AR）模型的替代方案，用于纵向电子健康记录（EHR）的零样本预测。在MIMIC-IV数据集上，BD展现出质量-效率权衡：小块配置（如B=8）保留了AR大部分预测性能，同时推理速度提升约1.43倍。冻结线性探针实验显示BD学习到的临床表征与AR相当。增加去噪预算收益递减，替代解码策略增益有限。总体而言，块扩散是纵向EHR建模的可行且计算高效的方法，迭代解码改进有望进一步缩小生成性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型虽在纵向EHR零样本预测中有效，但强制严格序列生成，难以处理时间聚集且相互依赖的临床事件。需要探索能捕捉事件间依赖关系、同时提升推理效率的替代建模范式。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上，控制分词方式、上下文长度、模型容量和评估条件一致，对比自回归（AR）模型与基于块的掩码扩散（BD）模型。系统性实验不同块大小、去噪预算及解码策略，评估生成质量、推理速度及下游临床预测任务上的表征质量（冻结线性探针）。

Result: BD呈现明显的质量-效率权衡：小块配置（B=8）达到约1.43倍推理加速，保留AR大部分预测性能。生成滚动性能在多数设置下仍低于AR，但冻结线性探针在临床预测任务上达成近乎相同表现，表明BD学习到的临床表征同等信息量。增加去噪步数收益递减，替代解码策略提供有限增益。

Conclusion: 块扩散是纵向EHR建模的可行且计算吸引力强的方法。当前生成性能与AR仍有差距，但表征质量已相当。未来改进迭代解码策略是缩小生成性能差距的有前景方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models have proved effective for zero-shot prediction from longitudinal electronic health records, but impose strictly sequential generation on trajectories containing temporally clustered and interdependent clinical events. We investigate block-based masked diffusion (BD) as an alternative on MIMIC-IV under matched tokenisation, context length, model capacity, and evaluation conditions. Across block sizes, BD exhibits a clear quality--efficiency trade-off, with small-block configurations retaining much of the predictive performance of AR while enabling substantially faster inference. A representative $B{=}8$ configuration achieves approximately $1.43\times$ faster evaluation. Although generative rollout remains below AR in most settings, frozen linear probes on BD and AR representations achieve near-identical performance across clinical prediction tasks, indicating that BD learns comparably informative clinical representations. Increasing the denoising budget yields diminishing returns, while alternative decoding strategies provide limited gains. Overall, our results show that block diffusion is a viable and computationally attractive approach to longitudinal EHR modelling, with further improvements in iterative decoding offering a promising route toward closing the remaining generative performance gap.

</details>


### [204] [Stochastic Pruning of the DPO Computation Graph: 1.4x Faster Training and Lower Peak Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29538)
*Yubing Wu,Wei Chen,Junmei Yang,Delu Zeng,Tiewen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于DPO（直接偏好优化）计算图的统一随机剪枝框架，通过独立采样响应位置用于评分、响应前缀用于执行、偏好分支用于反向传播，在训练步骤的每个阶段省略计算，实现了约1.426倍的步骤时间加速和30%的训练时间减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO在输出评分、序列执行和反向传播三个阶段都产生高昂的训练成本，仅减少监督信号并不一定能节省计算资源，因此需要一种系统性的方法在计算图层面进行剪枝以真正降低计算开销。

Method: 提出统一的随机剪枝框架，独立采样三个维度：(1)响应位置用于评分剪枝，(2)响应前缀用于执行剪枝，(3)偏好分支用于反向传播剪枝。在对应操作前做出选择以省略各阶段计算。使用逆概率加权获得条件无偏的单分支梯度估计器。

Result: 位置和序列剪枝主要降低峰值显存；分支剪枝在两个模型家族上相对于拼接式DPO基线实现1.426倍步骤时间加速，训练时间减少约30%，均值准确率略有下降，开放式基准分数变化依赖于具体基准。

Conclusion: 随机计算图剪枝的资源收益与被省略的计算量及训练目标的变化直接相关，该框架在显著降低计算成本的同时保持了可接受的模型性能。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) incurs training costs in output scoring, sequence execution, and backpropagation, so reducing supervision alone does not necessarily save computation. We introduce a unified framework for stochastic pruning of the DPO computation graph that independently samples response positions for scoring, response prefixes for execution, and preference branches for backpropagation. By making these selections before the corresponding operations, the framework omits computation at each stage of a training step. Our analysis characterizes the objective changes induced by position and sequence pruning and uses inverse-probability weighting to obtain a conditionally unbiased single-branch gradient estimator for the sampled objective. Controlled measurements show that position and sequence pruning primarily reduce peak memory, with most output-memory savings attributable to an implementation that retains full response supervision. Branch pruning yields a $1.426\times$ step-time speedup over a concatenated DPO baseline and reduces training time by about 30\% relative to stock DPO across two model families, with slightly lower mean accuracy and benchmark-dependent changes in open-ended scores. These findings relate the resource benefits of stochastic graph pruning to the computation omitted and the resulting changes in the training objective.

</details>


### [205] [WRDO: Weighted Refusal Direction Optimization in Aligned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29555)
*Grigoriy Evseev,Vladimir Solodkin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Aletov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出加权拒绝方向优化（WRDO），通过学习每个提示的拒绝方向的聚合权重，构建复合拒绝方向，增强拒绝干预的效果和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝干预方法使用单一残差流方向（有害与无害提示均值差），但忽略了有害提示的异质性，可能混淆不同的拒绝相关现象，导致干预脆弱。

Method: WRDO 从每个提示的拒绝方向库中学习聚合权重，构建加权复合拒绝方向，允许不同有害提示不等贡献。在三个开源模型上用三个护栏评判器和 HarmBench 评估，并测量通用能力基准，还研究防御设置：反向优化恢复消融模型的拒绝行为。

Result: 加权聚合提供更有效且灵活的拒绝表示操纵机制，优于单一方向方法，且在防御设置中能恢复拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 学习提示级拒绝方向的加权组合能显著增强拒绝干预的鲁棒性和适应性，为模型安全对齐提供新思路。

Abstract: Aligned large language models are trained to comply with benign requests while refusing harmful ones. However, recent work shows that this refusal behavior is fragile and can often be disrupted by simple activation- or weight-space interventions. In particular, refusal has been shown to be largely mediated by a single residual-stream direction, typically computed as a difference-of-means vector between harmful and harmless prompts. However, this standard construction aggregates heterogeneous harmful prompts into one uniform average, potentially conflating distinct refusal-related phenomena. In this work, we study whether refusal interventions can be strengthened by learning how to combine prompt-specific refusal directions. We propose Weighted Refusal Direction Optimization (WRDO), a procedure that constructs a composite refusal direction from a bank of per-prompt directions using learned aggregation weights. This approach allows different harmful prompts to contribute unequally to the final intervention direction. We evaluate WRDO against prior single-direction methods on three open-weight language models using three guard judges and HarmBench as an external evaluator. We further measure the effect of the intervention on general model capability through standard utility benchmarks. Finally, we investigate a defense-oriented setting in which the same optimization machinery is used in reverse to restore refusal behavior in an abliterated model checkpoint. Our results show that weighted aggregation provides a more effective and flexible mechanism for manipulating refusal representations.

</details>


### [206] [Implicit Feature Reweighting for Label-Free Graph Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29571)
*Tianjun Yao,Chen Xi,Xinyang Li,Lixian Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCARCE是一种无标签的图表示学习框架，通过环境混淆机制实现分布外泛化，无需类别标签即可在DrugOOD等数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络的OOD泛化方法通常依赖类别标签来识别预测性子结构，限制了实际应用场景；需要一种无标签、仅利用环境划分的鲁棒表示学习方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 变分图自编码器预热进行图重建；(2) 对抗混淆机制强制编码器生成无法被判别器区分环境的表示，同时保持重建保真度。理论分析表明环境混淆收缩了环境判别方向，等价于对环境相关特征施加更强的方向惩罚，实现特征重加权。

Result: 在合成数据集和DrugOOD数据集上，SCARCE在无标签方法中达到最先进的OOD泛化性能，且表示学习阶段完全不使用类别标签。

Conclusion: SCARCE提供了一种无标签、连续松弛的特征重加权机制，有效减少对虚假相关性的依赖而不丢失信息，为图神经网络的实际部署提供了可行的分布外泛化方案。

Abstract: Graph-level out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization is critical for deploying graph neural networks in real-world applications where distribution shifts are inevitable. Existing approaches typically require class labels during representation learning to decide which substructures are predictive, limiting their applicability.
